# Frontend Audit — plan + tracker

One file, two jobs:

1. **The plan** — the whole frontend cut into sessions that each fit one Claude Pro / Opus 5 (xhigh) context window, in an order where each session can lean on what the earlier ones established.
2. **The tracker** — the live record: what has been read, what was found, what was fixed, and what was read and found clean (so no later session re-derives it).

Companion to `docs/backend-audit-tracker.md` (boot/routes/middleware — closed) and `docs/services-audit.md` (`services/` — closed). Same house rules, same severity language.

## What this audit looks for

Three buckets, every session, always all three. A session that reports only bugs has not finished.

### 🐛 BUG — it is wrong now
- **State/effect defects** — `useEffect` that sets state it also depends on, stale closures over props, missing cleanup on listeners/timers/`AbortController`, `useQueries` without `combine` (new array ref every render → effect loop → tab freeze, cf. `[[usequeries-combine-referential-stability]]`), TDZ from a const read inside a closure declared above its `useState` (`[[closure-tdz-not-caught-by-tsc]]`).
- **Cache-correctness** — a mutation that invalidates the wrong `qk` key (or none), so a write lands in the DB and the screen keeps showing the old row; an invalidation that is too broad and refetches half the app; `keepPreviousData` screens that show another patient's data during a key change.
- **Contract drift / unsafe casts** — a read without `{ schema }`, an `as`/`as unknown as` that bridges a parsed payload into a view-model (defeats the fail-loud guard), a hand-written response `interface` shadowing a contract.
- **Funnel bypass** — bare `fetch()`, a mutation without the CSRF token, a raw-fetch POST missing `prefetchCsrfToken()`, an outbound call with no timeout.
- **Navigation** — `window.location.href` where `navigate()` belongs (full reload, loses cache + scroll); a redirect that drops query state.
- **Money/units** — rounding, currency mixing (IQD vs USD never converted implicitly), `parseInt` without radix, `Number()` on a `''`, negative amounts accepted.
- **Dates** — a `date` column treated as a `Date`, a `timestamp` formatted as UTC, month arithmetic that breaks on DST-free Baghdad wall-clock, week-start assumptions (Fri+Sat weekend here).
- **Race/lifecycle** — two in-flight submits, double-click double-create, a modal that unmounts mid-request, SSE reconnect storms, an async `setState` after unmount.
- **RTL/i18n** — a translated screen with an untranslated literal, a hardcoded `left`/`right`, a number formatted with Arabic-Indic digits (must stay Western), a date formatted from module-scope language instead of the live `language` prop.

### ✨ ENH — it works, but it should be better
- **Reuse** — a hand-rolled `.modal-overlay` instead of `<Modal>`; a hand-rolled title bar instead of `<ModalHeader>`; a third copy of a date/phone/money formatter that `utils/formatters.ts` already has; a bespoke confirm instead of `ConfirmDialog`/`useConfirm`.
- **Altitude** — a 900-line component that is three screens in a trench coat; props threaded 4 levels that a context or a `qk` read would settle; a `useState` pile that is really one reducer or one derived value.
- **Server-state hygiene** — server data parked in `useState` + `useEffect` instead of `useQuery`; a manual `loadX()` refetch where an invalidation belongs (the two deliberate loader exceptions — `PatientManagement`, `PatientSets` — are **not** findings).
- **Perceived speed** — a screen with no loader prefetch that flashes empty; an avoidable waterfall (route chunk → page chunk → query); an unsplit heavy dependency (GrapesJS, chart libs, canvas code).
- **Accessibility** — focus not returned on modal close, a click handler on a `<div>`, an icon button with no `aria-label`, a form field with no label association, a live region missing on async status.
- **Resilience** — a `catch` that swallows, an error path with no toast, a 5xx that renders a blank card instead of a retry, a missing `ErrorBoundary` on a lazy route.
- **Consistency** — inline styles or literal colors where design tokens exist; a `!important`; a dark-theme or RTL hole on a screen that claims support.

### 🧹 DEAD — it should not exist
- Unreferenced components/files/exports, unused props, unused `qk` keys, unused `queryOptions` factories, unused contract imports.
- Orphan `*.module.css` (no importer) and dead classes inside live modules — resolve the import alias **and** grep for prop-drilled class names and `composes:` before deleting (`[[css-audit-project]]`).
- Commented-out blocks, `TODO` stubs that shipped, feature flags with one branch, legacy shims for removed backends (WebSocket-era leftovers, pre-React-Query fetch helpers).
- Duplicate implementations where one is unreachable.

---

## Rules for every session

1. **Read before judging.** A finding needs a concrete failure scenario — inputs/state → wrong output — not "this looks risky". Same bar as the backend audit.
2. **Audit sessions do not refactor.** File findings; fix in the phase's remediation session. The one exception: a 🔴 gets fixed the moment it is confirmed.
3. **Record what was clean.** Every session ends with a "read and found clean" list. That list is the reason the next session is cheap.
4. **Every fix ends with `npm run gate`.** Typecheck (both configs) + eslint + stylelint + vitest + contracts `--strict` + build. A UI fix that changes a route or a mount also diffs `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt`.
5. **Verify in the running app, not just in the type system.** Dev on `:5273` (WSL) — `node scripts/e2e/smoke-aligner.mjs` and the `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs` harness for anything visual. Delete Playwright screenshots afterwards.
6. **Translated screens carry an extra checklist.** Any change to a screen in the i18n ratchet must keep the `eslint.config.js` file list, `RTL_ROUTES`/`RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`, and the `index.html` FOUC route check in sync.
7. **Update this file at the end of the session, before context runs out.** Status board row + findings + clean list. A session that ends without writing here did not happen.

### Severity legend

| | meaning |
|---|---|
| 🔴 | live data loss, money wrong, or a patient/doctor sees another patient's data — fix immediately |
| 🟠 | a real user-visible defect on a normal path |
| 🟡 | defect on an edge path, or a correctness smell with a narrow blast radius |
| 🔵 | latent / cosmetic / cleanup |

### Finding IDs

`FE-<session>-<n>` — e.g. `FE-F7-3`. Type tag after the ID: `[BUG]` / `[ENH]` / `[DEAD]`.

## Scope

**In scope** — `public/js/**` (staff SPA + Patient Portal bundle), `public/css/**`, the `*.module.css` next to components, the HTML entry points (`public/index.html`, `login.html`, `portal.html`, and the FOUC scripts in them), `vite.config.js`/`postcss.config.js` where they change runtime behaviour, and the frontend half of the shared contracts (how `shared/contracts/*` are consumed, not their server use).

**Out of scope** — anything the backend audits already closed (`routes/`, `services/`, `middleware/`, `migrations/`); the `aligner-portal-external/` submodule (its own repo, its own `CLAUDE.md`, audit separately); `node_modules` patches.

# The plan

Each session below lists its files, what to look for that is **specific to that surface**, and the cross-references it can assume are already settled.

---

## F0 · Mechanical baseline & orphan sweep (½ session)

Pure tooling — no judgement calls. Produces the numbers every later session checks itself against.

**Do:**
- `npm run gate` from a clean tree; record any pre-existing warning noise so later sessions can tell new from old.
- One-off unused-code scan. `knip`/`ts-prune` are not installed → F0 wrote `node scripts/audit/frontend-dead-scan.mjs` (no deps); use that. Treat the output as **leads, not findings** — dynamic `import()` in `ContentRenderer`/`routes.config.tsx` and the lazy preloader map will produce false positives.
- Orphan CSS-module scan: every `*.module.css` with no importer; every `public/css/**` file not reached from an entry HTML or an `@import`.
- `npm run build:analyze` — save the chunk map (sizes + what is in the big chunks) for F26.
- Grep baselines, recorded below so a later session can prove it did not add to them.

**Baselines — measured 2026-09-15 (plan), re-measured 2026-09-16 (F0 run).** Where the two differ the F0 number is the real one; the plan's figures were estimates made without running the scans. Update on each remediation session.

| scan | plan | **F0 actual** | note |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `window.location.href` | 12 | **12** → **10** (RA4) | all 12 classified and sanctioned; RA4 removed the two 401 redirects (see FE-F2-2 — `index.html`'s interceptor is the sole owner now). Originally: 2× 401 redirect (`loaderQuery.ts:42`, `loaders.ts:124`), 2× logout (`UserMenu.tsx:83`, `UserManagement.tsx:91`), 3× OAuth server redirect (`IntegrationsSettings` 3Shape/Drive/Contacts), 5× system protocol (`csimaging:`, `dolphin:`, `explorer:` ×2, `archformlocal:`). **No finding** — later sessions inherit the classification, not the re-derivation |
| `alert(` | 1 | **0** | the single hit is the word "alert()" in a `ToastContext.tsx` doc comment |
| `console.log` (in `public/js`) | 4 | **0 real** | 2 are JSDoc example lines (`core/utils.ts`), 2 are `if (import.meta.env.DEV)`-gated loader traces (`router/loaders.ts` 420, 526). **But `public/index.html` ships 11 unguarded `console.*` inline** — see FE-F0-5 |
| `useEffect(` | 189 | **198** | pool, not a defect count |
| `: any` / `as any` | 107 | **6** | the plan's 107 was a mis-measure (it counted the word `any` anywhere — 99 hits). Real annotations: `LookupEditorModal` ×3, `Dashboard` ×1, `PhotoEditor` ×1, `doctorColors` ×1 |
| `as unknown as` | 8 | **11** | `core/http.ts:210` (text passthrough), `core/storage.ts:43`, `core/utils.ts` ×2 (deepClone), `photo-render-watch.ts:85`, and 6 `e as unknown as MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>` in `CalendarGrid`/`CalendarDayContextMenu` — the calendar six are one pattern, F10 owns it — **F10's verdict: a live bug, not just a type smell** (the keyboard-opened menu has no `clientX`, so it renders at the viewport's corner — FE-F10-15). **None bridges a parsed contract payload into a view-model** (the shape the rule actually bans) |

**Gate baseline (`npm run gate`, clean tree, 2026-09-16): PASS.** The noise a later session must not mistake for its own:
- **4 eslint warnings, 0 errors** — all four in `public/js/pages/aligner/PatientSets.tsx` at 2275:17 and 2299:21 (`click-events-have-key-events`, `no-noninteractive-element-interactions`, `no-static-element-interactions`). Nothing else in the tree warns.
- **stylelint clean · 21 test files / 198 tests pass · contracts D1 0 (baseline 0), D2 9 (baseline 9) · both typechecks clean · build green.**
- Build-time notes, not defects: vite's "chunks larger than 500 kB" (only `grapes`), and a `[PLUGIN_TIMINGS] vite:css` note.

**Bundle baseline (`npm run build:analyze`, 2026-09-16)** — 89 JS chunks, CSS 989.55 kB across 59 files. Treemap at `dist/stats.html` (regenerated per build; re-run the command in F26).

| chunk | raw | gzip | loaded |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `grapes` | 1,098.68 kB | 290.04 kB | lazy (GrapesJS — the only >500 kB chunk) |
| `vendor-react` | 374.16 kB | 117.37 kB | **initial** |
| `main` | 237.79 kB | 63.63 kB | **initial** |
| `SettingsComponent` | 181.03 kB | 43.89 kB | lazy |
| `vendor-charts` | 179.89 kB | 62.49 kB | lazy — **not** in the initial graph (the `vite.config.js` comment about Chart.js loading eagerly is stale as of this build; F26 confirms per route) |
| `WorkComponent` 84.78 · `Select` (react-select) 83.94 · `PatientSets` 77.64 · `schemas` 75.09 (**initial**) · `PhoneInput` 62.64 · `photoswipe` 58.90 · `PhotoEditor` 52.92 · `CompareComponent` 43.58 · `Statistics` 40.38 · `GridComponent` 38.89 · `PatientSlideshow` 36.18 | | | |

Initial module graph from built `dist/index.html`: `rolldown-runtime`, `vendor-react`, `main`, `schemas`, `keys`, `storage`, `Modal` (+ `main`/`Modal`/`schemas` CSS) ≈ **700 kB raw / ~200 kB gzip**.

**Dead-code scan — tooling note.** `knip`/`ts-prune` are not in `devDependencies` and installing them was not worth a tree change, so F0 wrote `scripts/audit/frontend-dead-scan.mjs` instead (no deps; resolves relative + frontend alias specifiers). Re-run it in F24 and in every remediation session:

```bash
node scripts/audit/frontend-dead-scan.mjs            # all three scans
node scripts/audit/frontend-dead-scan.mjs --exports  # just the export sweep
```

Results (2026-09-16):
- **Unimported files: 0 of 307.** Every `.ts`/`.tsx` under `public/js` is reached from `App.tsx` (the staff SPA entry, imported by an inline module script in `public/index.html` as `/js/App.jsx`) or `public/portal.html` → `js/portal/main.tsx`.
- **Orphan `*.module.css`: 0 of 151. Unreached `public/css/**`: 0 of 20** — 17 imported by `App.tsx`, the rest by `PatientShell` (`work-card`, `sidebar-navigation`), `AlignerLayout` (`aligner-common`), `Calendar`/`DailyAppointments` (`appointment-calendar`), `LookupsSettings`/`LookupManagerModal` (`lookup-editor`).
- **Dead exports: 14 confirmed** (FE-F0-1), plus ~120 exported-but-only-locally-used type aliases — the latter are the `export` keyword being unnecessary, not dead code, and are **not** being filed one by one.

**Output:** the tables above, filled in; the confirmed-dead export list in the ledger as `[DEAD]` for the owning session, and the scanner in `scripts/audit/` so F24/F26 and the remediation sessions can re-run it.

# Phase A — foundations

Everything else sits on these four sessions. A defect found here usually explains three symptoms found later, so do not reorder Phase A behind feature work.

## F1 · HTTP funnel + client core (~4.3k)

`public/js/core/**` (12) · `public/js/services/**` (6) · `public/js/utils/**` (9)

Biggest: `fileSystemAccess.ts` 574 · `http.ts` 477 · `iniParser.ts` 310 · `utils.ts` 257 · `sse-appointments.ts` 223 · `formatters.ts` 215 · `sse-whatsapp.ts` 196 · `whatsapp-api-client.ts` 176 · `language.ts` 166 · `storage.ts` 154 · `chunk-reload.ts` 149 · `error-reporter.ts` 144.

**Look for:**
- `http.ts` — the CSRF 403 retry (exactly once? re-entrancy under concurrent mutations?), the 30s timeout vs long uploads, envelope unwrapping when `data` is legitimately `null`, schema-parse failure reporting, and whether `httpErrorMessage` surfaces the server's friendly text on every shape.
- The two SSE singletons — refcount symmetry (`ensureConnected`/`release` leaks on unmount paths), reconnect backoff, the liveness thresholds in `constants/sse-liveness.ts`, whether a forced reconnect can double-open.
- `error-reporter.ts` — throttle/dedupe/cap correctness, and the promise that it **never throws** (it is called from `componentDidCatch`).
- `chunk-reload.ts` — the Vite `preloadError` path (`[[admin-403-root-router-incident]]`: `preventDefault` → `undefined` → "reading 'default'").
- `formatters.ts` — the Arabic weekday hand-map, Western digits, and that `language` is a parameter, never module state.
- `fileSystemAccess.ts` + `iniParser.ts` — the least-reviewed files here; browser-API availability guards, and what happens on a cancelled picker.
- Dead: helpers left from the pre-React-Query era; anything exported and never imported.

## F2 · Server-state layer (~4.1k)

`public/js/query/**` (7, incl. 3 test files) · `public/js/router/**` (2) · `App.tsx` · `layouts/**` · `config/**` · `constants/**`

Biggest: `queries.ts` 1,757 · `loaders.ts` 568 · `routes.config.tsx` 551 · `keys.ts` 381 · `workTypeConfig.ts` 208 · `RootLayout.tsx` 154.

**Look for:**
- **Key hierarchy vs. invalidation reality** — build the map once: every `qk` key, every factory that uses it, every `invalidateQueries` call site in the app. The deliverable of this session is that map; F5–F22 all check their writes against it. Findings: keys that are not prefixes of their children, two factories sharing a key with different shapes, keys never invalidated by anything, keys invalidated by writes that do not touch them.
- Every `queryOptions` factory carries `{ schema }` (and the right one); `staleTime`/`gcTime` choices that make a screen show stale money.
- `loaders.ts` — `ensureQueryData` vs `fetchQuery` misuse, loaders that fetch data the route never renders, 401 redirect handling, and prefetch/chunk-warm pairing with `ContentRenderer`'s preloader map.
- `routes.config.tsx` — lazy boundaries, `errorElement` coverage per route, route params that reach a query key unvalidated.
- `client.ts` — the `QueryCache`/`MutationCache` `onError` reporting (does a Zod drift throw actually reach `/api/client-error`?), retry policy on 4xx.
- Dead: unused factories/keys (cross-check F0's knip leads).

## F3 · Contexts, shared hooks, error boundaries (~4.7k)

`contexts/**` (7) · `hooks/**` (17) · `components/error-boundaries/**` (4)

Biggest: `useStand.ts` 448 · `useWhatsAppAuth.ts` 430 · `PrintQueueContext.tsx` 337 · `useExpenses.ts` 282 · `useAppointmentsSync.ts` 277 · `GlobalStateContext.tsx` 264 · `useAppointments.ts` 248 · `useWhatsAppSync.ts` 237 · `ToastContext.tsx` 236 · `useDateManager.ts` 232 · `useMessageStatus.ts` 191.

**Look for:**
- Context value identity — a `value={{...}}` rebuilt every render re-renders every consumer (React Compiler helps, it does not guarantee).
- `GlobalStateContext` — what is actually in it, whether any of it is server state that belongs in React Query, and who still reads it.
- The SSE hooks (`useAppointments*`, `useWhatsApp*`, `useMessageStatus`) — subscribe/unsubscribe symmetry, the `invalidateQueries` fan-out on each event type, conflict recovery via `isInvalidStateTransition`, and double-subscription under StrictMode.
- `useDateManager` — week start, month boundaries, and any `new Date()` that should be wall-clock date-only.
- `PrintQueueContext` — queue state that survives navigation, and what happens if a print job's component unmounts.
- Error boundaries — which routes are actually wrapped; `RouteErrorBoundary` vs `GlobalErrorBoundary` vs the router's `errorElement`; whether a lazy-chunk failure lands in a class boundary (it must — `React.lazy` failures skip function boundaries).
- Dead: hooks with one caller that should be inlined, or zero callers.

## F4 · App shell + shared primitives (~3.0k)

`Modal` 297 · `ModalHeader` 96 · `ConfirmDialog` 53 · `UniversalHeader` 345 · `Navigation` 439 · `UserMenu` 147 · `ThemeToggle` 44 · `NavigationProgress` 26 · `PatientShell` 248 · `ContentRenderer` 377 · `PatientQuickSearch` 288 · `PatientSearchCombobox` 193 · `PhoneInput` 53 · `PhoneDisplay` 38 · `AlignerModeToggle` 47 · `SettingsTabNavigation` 82 · `PrintQueueIndicator` 202

**Look for:**
- `Modal` — focus trap correctness (first focus, restore on close, nested modals), scroll-lock leak if two modals open, Escape vs unsaved-form, the drag handle vs touch, `#modal-root` portal when it is missing.
- **The hand-rolled-overlay census** — `grep -rln "modalOverlay\|modal-overlay" public/js --include='*.tsx'` reads **0** as of 2026-09-15, so this session's job is to confirm that number and record it as a baseline; any later session that finds one files it against the owning feature.
- `ContentRenderer` — the lazy map vs `preloadPatientPage()` drift (a page string with no preloader = a guaranteed waterfall), and unknown-page fallback.
- `Navigation` — the `explorer:`/`csimaging:` protocol links (sanctioned `window.location`), and the UNC-vs-local path contract (`PatientsFolder` must stay UNC).
- The two patient searches — debounce, race (a slow response overwriting a newer one), keyboard nav, and why there are two.
- Header — role gating of the bells, and layout at 412px.

# Phase B — patient record & money

## F5 · Header bells, dashboard, statistics (~3.8k)

`ApprovalsBell` 364 · `AlertModal` 380 · `TaskFormModal` 351 · `TasksBell` 285 · `PortalActivityBell` 276 · `MyApprovalsBadge` 194 · `routes/TasksHistory` 255 · `services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}` 135 · `routes/Dashboard` 281 · `StatisticsComponent` 858 · `RevenueBreakdownView` 289 · `DoctorCommissionsView` 134 · `routes/Statistics` 9 · `utils/chartSetup`+`chartTheme` 79

**Look for:**
- Polling: what interval each bell uses, whether four bells poll four endpoints on the same timer, and whether they keep polling in a background tab.
- Badge counts vs. the list they open (read-time visibility rules: `escalate_at`/`snoozed_until`/`expires_at` vs `CURRENT_DATE` — `[[tasks-alerts-dual-surface]]`).
- Approvals: maker-checker roles on the client (a front-desk user must never see the admin queue), and optimistic state after approve/reject.
- Statistics: currency separation (IQD/USD never summed), the USD-equivalent ranking's exchange-rate source, date-range edge days, and chart theming in dark mode + RTL.

## F6 · Patient demographics & search (~3.3k)

`PatientManagement` 925 · `AddPatientForm` 800 · `EditPatientComponent` 711 · `ViewPatientInfo` 684 · `PatientFolderPicker` 136 · `PatientTypesReadOnly` 74

**Look for:**
- These three forms are **i18n-translated** (`patients` namespace) — full RTL/ratchet checklist applies.
- `PatientManagement` is a deliberate loader exception (URL-param search + scroll restoration): verify the exception still holds and is not hiding a stale-cache bug.
- Create vs edit divergence — `AddPatientForm` is the sole creation path; any validation rule present in one and missing in the other is a finding.
- Phone normalization + duplicate detection; the works-derived patient type (`classifyPatient`) display vs. what the server recomputes.
- The 29-`useState` pile in `PatientManagement` and the 800-line form: altitude candidates.

## F7 · Works & treatment items (~3.4k)

`WorkComponent` 915 · `NewWorkComponent` 954 · `WorkDetailItem` 489 · `WorkCard` 396 · `WorkDetailsPanel` 124 · `TransferWorkModal` 322 · `config/workTypeConfig` 208

**Look for:**
- `WorkComponent`/`WorkCard` are translated (`works` namespace); `WorkDetailsPanel`/`WorkDetailItem` are deliberately **English** (doctor-facing) — do not "fix" that.
- Work creation → invoice/type-reclassification side effects: which `qk` keys must be invalidated (patient info, works, appointments, patient type) and whether all of them are.
- Shade selection (Crown/Bridge, Veneers) — system/shade pairing, and the hardcoded system labels.
- Transfer: what happens to invoices/payments on a transfer, and the confirm UX for an irreversible move.
- Double-submit on create.

## F8 · Payments, invoices, expenses (~4.0k)

`PaymentModal` 1,271 · `PaymentFormDrawer` 264 · `DailyInvoicesModal` 285 · `DoctorPaymentsModal` 181 · `components/expenses/**` 1,017 · `routes/Expenses` 307 · `hooks/useExpenses` 282

**The single most bug-sensitive session.** Money that is wrong on screen is wrong in the clinic.

**Look for:**
- Rounding and currency: IQD vs USD kept separate everywhere, no implicit conversion, integer-minor-unit discipline matching the server's `moneyInt` non-negative rule.
- Overpayment/negative-amount guards on the client matching the server's; balance recomputation after a payment (aligner payments have their own guard — cross-check `[[aligner-remaining-semantics]]` in F17).
- `PaymentModal` is translated (`payments` namespace) and had a set-state-in-effect cascade fixed before — re-verify it did not regress.
- Expenses are translated (`expenses` namespace) and use the lookup-editor pattern; verify `invalidateKeys` actually refreshes the live dropdown.
- Approval tiers: an old-record edit must route to the pending queue, and the UI must say so rather than looking like a success.

## F9 · Clinical: visits, diagnosis, imaging links (~2.9k)

`pages/Diagnosis` 957 · `NewVisitComponent` 555 · `VisitsComponent` 295 · `WebCephModal` 405 · `TeethSelector` 286 · `ThreeShapeScansView` 180 · `XraysComponent` 170 · `DentalChart` 80

**Look for:**
- Dental-chart tooth-numbering consistency across `TeethSelector`, `DentalChart` and `Diagnosis` (one mapping or three?).
- Unsaved-work loss: `Diagnosis` and `NewVisitComponent` are long forms — navigation away, modal close, and route-change guards.
- External integrations from the client (WebCeph, 3Shape) — timeout handling and the "is the workstation on?" error path; 3Shape `/v3` parsing is flagged UNVERIFIED (`[[threeshape-webservice-integration]]`), so record what the client assumes.
- Dead: pre-`tshape:`-protocol leftovers.

# Phase C — scheduling & display

## F10 · Calendar + booking workflow (~4.6k)

`AppointmentCalendar` 791 · `CalendarGrid` 631 · `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` 527 · `EditAppointmentForm` 440 · `AppointmentForm` 394 · `CalendarHeader` 217 · `HolidayQuickModal` 213 · `MonthlyCalendarGrid` 210 · `PatientAppointments` 205 · `AnalogClock` 204 · `CalendarDayContextMenu` 201 · `PeriodNavigator` 106 · `CalendarContextMenu` 105 · `calendar.types` 115 · `doctorColors` 89 · `DoctorFilter` 75 · `CalendarLegend` 41 · `routes/Calendar` 12

**Look for:**
- Date math: Fri+Sat weekend, holidays, month/week boundaries, and the wall-clock (never UTC) rule. A single `toISOString()` on a date-only value is a 🟠 here.
- The four booking-workflow files are translated — Arabic short weekday names without "ال", Western digits, `language` passed explicitly.
- Slot conflicts: two users booking the same slot, the client's optimistic render vs the server's rejection, and whether the rejection is legible.
- Per-doctor filtering at scale — this clinic has 5 doctors and 94% NULL `dr_id`; the product target is up to ~20 doctors (see `CLAUDE.md`). Flag anything that only works because the doctor list is tiny.
- `doctorColors` — stable color assignment when a doctor is added/quits.

## F11 · Daily appointments (SSE), chair display, videos (~3.4k)

`components/react/appointments/**` 1,360 · `routes/DailyAppointments` 20 · `routes/ChairDisplay` 262 · `TvDisplaySettings` 1,005 · `routes/Videos` 724

**Look for:**
- The SSE screen's `keepPreviousData` + invalidate-on-event loop: does an event storm cause a refetch storm? Does the "Live | Stale | Offline" indicator tell the truth after a laptop sleep?
- `AppointmentCard.getCurrentStatus()` returns **English logical keys** feeding `data-status` selectors — untranslated on purpose. Do not file it.
- Chair display is the one sanctioned direct `new EventSource`; verify the CLOSED-stream self-reload (`CLOSED_STREAM_RELOAD_DELAY_MS`) and that the kiosk stays pinned light + LTR.
- Videos: streaming through the server (`/api/videos/:id/stream`), seek/range behaviour, and memory on long sessions.

# Phase D — imaging

The densest code in the app. These four sessions are smaller on purpose.

## F12 · Timepoint grid + file explorer (~3.2k)

`GridComponent` 1,165 · `files/**` 1,152 · `PhotoSessionDialog` 425 · `TimepointActionsMenu` 145 · `DeleteTimepointModal` 116 · `EditTimepointModal` 99 · `hooks/useImportFolder` 139

**Look for:**
- Image URL construction + `encodeRelPath` (path traversal, spaces, Arabic filenames, `#`/`?` in names).
- Delete scopes (`DeleteScope`/`FolderState`) — what each scope actually removes, and whether the confirm text matches it. Destructive + irreversible = highest bar in this phase.
- Load behaviour with a large timepoint (lazy loading, thumbnail vs full-res, `object-fit` and memory).
- Invalidation after import/rename/delete across `timepoints`/`gallery`/`photoVisibility`.

`compare/ComparisonEngine.ts` 672 · `compare/CompareOverlay` 452 · `CompareComponent` 334 · `compare/ControlsPanel` 315 · `compare/CompareStage` 213 · `compare/icons` 176 · `compare/types` 145 · `compare/useCompareShare` 144 · `compare/SelectionPanel` 132 · `compare/geometry` 102 · `compare/useSlideshow` 71 · `compare/useComparisonEngine` 50

**Look for:**
- The `useQueries` + `combine` fix that stopped the tab freeze — verify it is still there and still correct (`[[usequeries-combine-referential-stability]]`).
- Canvas/geometry: the mirror-rect + θ conjugation math (`[[photo-render-flip-math]]`), devicePixelRatio, and whether a resize recomputes or stretches.
- Memory: are decoded images released when the selection changes?
- Touch/pointer handling on the 412px viewport.

## F14 · Photo editor + sharing (~2.9k)

`photo-editor/**` 1,926 · `share/**` 461 · `localsend/LocalSendShareModal` 383 · `services/photo-render-watch` 103

**Look for:**
- Editor state vs. disk state — a rename/move that happens under an open editor.
- The sanctioned raw-`fetch` blob POSTs must attach `prefetchCsrfToken()`.
- Telegram share is a background job + poll (`[[telegram-bigfile-send]]`) — poll termination, error surfacing, and what happens when the tab closes mid-job.
- LocalSend discovery timeouts (`utils/fetch-timeout.ts` pattern) and the "is the workstation on?" message.

## F15 · Slideshow (~2.3k)

`slideshow/SlideshowBuilder` 580 · `PatientSlideshow` 392 · `SlideshowPlayer` 362 · `ManageConfigsModal` 201 · `AnnotationCanvas` 174 · `configResolver` 172 · `SaveConfigModal` 110 · `photoTypes`/`types` 109 · `FolderPickerModal` 58 · `useWakeLock` 56 · `useFullscreen` 42

**Look for:**
- `configResolver` precedence rules (per-patient vs default config) and what happens when a referenced photo is missing.
- Wake lock + fullscreen release on unmount/navigation/tab switch.
- Annotation canvas: coordinate space vs. the displayed image, and whether annotations survive a resize.
- Timer cleanup in the player (the classic leak).

# Phase E — modules

## F16 · Messaging (WhatsApp send/auth) (~2.4k)

`routes/WhatsAppSend` 466 · `SendMessage` 361 · `whatsapp-send/**` 705 · `whatsapp-auth/**` 326 · `routes/WhatsAppAuth` 95 · `utils/whatsapp-api-client` 176 · `utils/whatsapp-validation` 116 · `utils/whatsapp-send-constants` 117

(The WhatsApp hooks were read in F3 — check findings there first.)

**Look for:**
- The QR lifecycle: every `/api/sse/whatsapp` stream registers a QR viewer and `activeQRViewers > 0` gates QR generation — so an unreleased stream keeps WhatsApp init alive. Verify release on unmount.
- `whatsapp-api-client` is a sanctioned raw consumer reading **top-level** fields — it must not be migrated to the envelope. Confirm it is annotated as such.
- Bulk send: progress accuracy, partial failure reporting, and whether a mid-send navigation kills the batch.
- Recipient validation vs the server's, and the message body's clinic name coming from `CLINIC_MESSAGE_NAME` configuration — never a literal.

## F17 · Aligner sets & batches (~4.2k)

`pages/aligner/PatientSets` 2,340 · `BatchFormDrawer` 836 · `SetFormDrawer` 700 · `pages/aligner/aligner.types` 195 · `hooks/useSetDrawer`+`useBatchDrawer` 96 · `utils/aligner-labels` 58

**Look for:**
- `remaining_*_aligners` semantics: **not-yet-batched**, consumed at batch creation — never derive "delivered" from `total − remaining` (`[[aligner-remaining-semantics]]`). Any client-side arithmetic that does is a 🟠.
- `PatientSets` is a deliberate loader exception with 32 `useState` — verify the self-managed `loadBatches` cannot serve stale data after a sibling write, then file the altitude ✨.
- Money: set cost sign guard, payment vs balance display.
- The doctor-portal round trip — a batch/set delete cascades to portal announcements; the UI should say what it will remove.

## F18 · Aligner lists, Archform, announcements (~2.6k)

`ArchformMatcher` 792 · `AllSetsList` 582 · `Announcements` 420 · `AlignerDoctorsSettings` 313 · `PatientsList` 212 · `SearchPatient` 129 · `DoctorsList` 106 · `layouts/AlignerLayout` 52

**Look for:**
- `ArchformMatcher` reads a third-party SQLite via the server — stale/missing-file handling, and matching heuristics that silently mismatch (see `docs/archform-matcher.md`).
- Announcements are two-way over the CDC mirror: what the staff side shows when the mirror is behind, and whether "sent" means "recorded locally" or "visible to the doctor".
- List paging/filtering at the product ceiling (hundreds of thousands of rows, not this clinic's count).

## F19 · Stand inventory & POS (~4.6k)

`components/stand/**` 3,451 (19 files, biggest `ItemFormModal` 709, `POSCheckout` 317) · `routes/Stand*` 688 · `hooks/useStand` 448

**Look for:**
- POS arithmetic: line totals, discounts, change due, and the same currency discipline as F8.
- Checkout atomicity from the client's view — a failed sale must not leave the cart half-cleared; a double-click must not sell twice.
- Stock movements: adjust/restock/expiry flows and the invalidation set after each.
- Barcode input focus stealing and scanner keystroke handling.
- Charts (`SalesTrendChart`, `TopItemsChart`) — dark theme + RTL.

## F20 · Lab tracking, templates, label printing (~3.4k)

`LabelPreviewModal` 1,009 · `templates/**` 1,256 (GrapesJS) · `lab-tracking/**` 862 · `routes/LabTracking` 75 · `hooks/useLabCases` 116 · `hooks/useLabelModal` 35 · `config/labStages` 55

**Look for:**
- `LabelPreviewModal` is a sanctioned raw-`fetch` blob POST — CSRF token attached manually, and the 18-`useState` pile is an altitude ✨.
- GrapesJS: bundle weight (is it lazy?), editor teardown on unmount, and whether template HTML is ever rendered unsanitized into the app.
- Lab board: stage transitions that skip states, and drag/drop accessibility.
- Print path: what the printed output depends on (print CSS stays light — dark tokens are `@media screen`).

# Phase F — settings & admin

## F21 · Settings I — clinic/ops + lookup editor (~4.9k)

`EmployeeSettings` 756 · `GeneralSettings` 686 · `CalendarTimesSettings` 679 · `HolidayEditor` 556 · `LookupEditorModal` 450 · `LookupEditor` 413 · `CostPresetsSettings` 301 · `SettingsComponent` 281 · `LookupsSettings` 259 · `ExchangeRatesSettings` 251 · `LookupManagerModal` 122 · `LookupContextMenu` 101 · `hooks/useLookupManager` 102 · `SettingsTabNavigation` (read in F4)

**Look for:**
- **Clinic identity is configuration** — `CLINIC_LOGO`/`CLINIC_NAME` (header) and `CLINIC_MESSAGE_NAME`/`_AR` (message bodies) are deliberately distinct. Any UI that folds them together is a 🟠.
- Employee quit semantics: quit staff hidden everywhere except Settings; commission flag/rate preserved on quit; new-task assignment blocked (`[[employees-active-status]]`). Verify each on the client.
- Exchange rates: which rate a screen uses (latest vs today-only) — the two have different functions on the server.
- Lookup editor: the right-click entry point on every eligible `<select>`, `invalidateKeys` correctness, and what happens when a row in use is deleted.
- `CalendarTimesSettings` (21 `useState`) — altitude candidate.

## F22 · Settings II — integrations, system, users (~4.5k)

`IntegrationsSettings` 1,086 · `ProtocolHandlersSettings` 841 · `DatabaseSettings` 474 · `EmailSettings` 404 · `AdminUserManagement` 357 · `PortalAccessCard` 309 · `SyncStatusPanel` 298 · `UserManagement` 219 · `DatabaseBackupSettings` 116 · `SupabaseStatusSettings` 43 · `DolphinStatusSettings` 40 · `core/iniParser` (read in F1)

**Look for:**
- OAuth cards (Drive, Contacts ×N accounts, 3Shape): does "Connected" ever survive a revoked grant? Does disconnect clear the live client, not just the row? Is the connected account address shown (Contacts is multi-account and a wrong-account connect must be visible)?
- `SyncStatusPanel`/`SupabaseStatusSettings` — the status card must show backlog **size and age**, `stale`, and `drift`. A green card during a capture blackout is exactly the 2026-09-08 incident; verify the client renders every signal the endpoint now returns.
- Two kill switches must never be conflated in the UI (env flag = this process drains; `cdc_sink_control.enabled` = the DB records at all).
- User management: role changes applied to the live session, password rules matching the server, and admin-only routes gated client-side **and** relied on server-side (never only client-side).
- Backups: what the button actually does, and whether a long operation blocks the UI past the 30s funnel timeout.

# Phase G — portal & cross-cutting

## F23 · Patient Portal bundle + HTML entry points (~2.0k)

`portal/**` (10 files, 976 LOC TS + `portal.module.css` 569) · `auth/` 62 · the three entry HTMLs `public/index.html`, `login.html`, `portal.html` (FOUC scripts, theme/lang/font whitelists)

**Look for:**
- The portal is its **own raw Zod boundary** (not the `core/http` funnel) — every response parsed, CSRF via `portal.csrf.ts`, and no staff-app import leaking into the bundle (check the built chunk).
- Session separation: `shwan.portal` vs `shwan.sid`, and the segment-bounded `/api/portal` skip (a broad prefix strips the staff session — `[[announcements-portal-activity]]`).
- What a patient can see: only their own data, no staff-only field bleeding into a tab.
- The FOUC scripts: theme/language/font whitelists in sync with the registries; portal + `login.html` are deliberately untouched by the theme.

## F24 · CSS delta pass (targeted over ~49k LOC)

The 11-phase CSS audit closed 2026-06-11 (`[[css-audit-project]]`), so this is a **delta**, not a redo.

**Do:**
- Orphan `*.module.css` and dead classes for every component changed since 2026-06-11 (`git log --since` to build the file list).
- Token discipline: literal colors/spacing/z-index added since; `!important` outside print/a11y.
- Dark + RTL coverage for everything added since the audit closed (new screens, new modals, charts).
- Confirm the fallback-strip rule is still honoured (check a token is defined before removing its fallback — an undefined one is load-bearing).
- `npm run lint:css` clean.

## F25 · Accessibility + responsive pass (cross-cutting)

- Keyboard-only walk of the top flows: patient search → works → payment; booking; daily appointments; settings.
- Focus management in `Modal` across the app's real modals; focus return after close; no focus trap escape.
- Labels/roles/`aria-*` on the shared primitives, then the highest-traffic forms.
- 412×915 with `findHorizontalOverflow(page)` on every top route (the harness exists: `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`).
- Contrast in both themes for status colors and charts.
- Delete screenshots afterwards.

## F26 · Performance & bundle pass (cross-cutting)

- `npm run build:analyze` vs the F0 baseline: biggest chunks, anything heavy that is not lazy (GrapesJS, charts, canvas, PDF).
- Route-chunk → page-chunk → query waterfalls; loader prefetch coverage per route.
- Render cost on the three heaviest screens (`PatientSets`, `GridComponent`, `AppointmentCalendar`) — React DevTools profiler, not guesswork. React Compiler is on: **do not** add manual memoization without a profile.
- Query cache size/`gcTime` on image-heavy screens.

## F11 · Daily appointments (SSE), chair display, videos · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: `components/react/appointments/**` (`DailyAppointments` 326 · `AppointmentCard` 322 · `AppointmentsHeader` 193 · `DoctorFilterSelect` 146 · `StatsCards` 145 · `AppointmentsList` 119 · `ConnectionStatus` 65 · `MobileViewToggle` 44) · `routes/DailyAppointments` 20 · `routes/ChairDisplay` 262 · `TvDisplaySettings` 1,005 · `routes/Videos` 727 — 3,374 LOC — plus their eleven CSS modules (2,980 lines). Cross-checks the surface forced: `useAppointments`, `useAppointmentsSync`, `services/sse-appointments.ts` and `constants/sse-liveness.ts` (F3 read them; re-read here only for the plan's questions), `dailyAppointmentsLoader`, the two "restore the last date" entry points (`UniversalHeader`, `Dashboard`), `appointment.contract.ts` (daily / state / undo / quick check-in), `tv-display.contract.ts`, `video.contract.ts`, `chair-display.contract.ts`; the server halves the client depends on — `appointment.routes.ts` (daily read, state, undo), `appointment-queries.ts` (`updatePresent`, `undoAppointmentState`, `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized`), `AppointmentService.ts` (`quickCheckIn`, `getDailyAppointments`), `utils/date.ts#formatTime12/formatClock12`, `sse-broadcaster.ts` (both channels), `chair-payload-builder.ts`, `visit-queries.ts#getLatestVisitsSum/buildVisitSummary`, `chair-display.routes.ts`, `tv-display.routes.ts` + `tv-display-store.ts`, `video.routes.ts` + `utils/stream-file.ts` + `imaging/qrcode.ts`, `middleware/timeout.ts`; `SettingsComponent` (what the TV tab's unsaved flag drives), the `vite.config.js` proxy table and the `Caddyfile`; and the route-table gates for every endpoint above.

**Runtime-verified in a cloud container, not on the owner's dev box**, on the same recipe as F9/F10/RB3: a **throwaway** PostgreSQL 16 database installed with plain `npm run db:migrate`, then `npm run db:setup -- --yes …` with the clinic name set to **"Bright Smile Orthodontics"** (so an identity literal cannot hide behind this clinic's own name) and `npm run db:seed:demo`. `MACHINE_PATH`, `TV_DISPLAY_MEDIA_DIR` and `TV_DISPLAY_SETTINGS_FILE` pointed at scratch folders; the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init and the drift check were off; the database and the server ran on `Asia/Baghdad`. Express (`:3001`) and Vite (`:5173`) ran as separate processes so Express could be killed and restarted under an open page. **Fixtures added by SQL:** two unchecked appointments tonight (20:00, 20:30) and one checked in at 19:52 but not seated. **Real writes to the throwaway DB**, where the write was the point: check-ins and undos driven from a second logged-in context ("another desk"), one quick check-in, the future-day check-in (undone after), TV-display uploads, one probe video (deleted after). **Aborted on purpose:** one check-in POST (`route.abort`). **A frozen transport** was built with a small TCP proxy in front of Vite whose existing sockets could be paused without FIN or RST. The probes (`scripts/e2e/.f11-probe*.tmp.mjs`) launched the preinstalled Chromium through `executablePath`; they, the temporary `.env`, the scratch folders and the recorded video were deleted, the database and role were dropped, and PostgreSQL was stopped. No screenshots were taken.

The kiosk has a reload for exactly this; the staff singleton has nothing, so every open board drops to the 5-minute safety net after each deploy | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

The row moved to *Checked-in* with `present_time` "06:26", which FE-F11-1 then shows as 6:26 AM. The indicator read "Static", and nothing on screen looked unusual (undone after). The file never reached the folder, the staging folder was left empty, and the playlist was unchanged. It fails cleanly, but it always fails. Over the tunnel, or clinic Wi-Fi with a real clip, the normal case fails | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

The operatory kiosk says "Welcome to **Shwan Orthodontics**", and the printed video QR handout's footer says "Shwan Orthodontics". The public TV page's `<title>` and its empty-playlist placeholder are the same literal, while the Settings tab tells staff that "the screen shows the clinic name". The QR itself encodes **`https://remote.shwan-orthodontics.com/v/1`**, because `config.urls.publicUrl` falls back to this clinic's domain (`config/config.ts:149`). An install without `PUBLIC_URL` hands its patients a link to another clinic's server, and nothing on the modal says the address is a default | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-7 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `sse-appointments.ts:5-11,61-64` · `ConnectionStatus.tsx:14-19,25-29` · `ChairDisplay.tsx:78-80,136-148` · `sse-broadcaster.ts:110-115` | **"Live" and "● Connected" can sit over a dead stream.** The server's 25 s keep-alive is an SSE comment, which EventSource never surfaces, and freshness is just `readyState === OPEN`, so no client can notice a transport that went quiet without closing. `ConnectionStatus`'s docblock promises "heartbeat received recently"; nothing checks one. Runtime: the stream socket frozen (no FIN/RST), then a second desk checked a patient in → **still "Live" at +10 s and +70 s**, 0 GETs, row unchanged. The board's 5-minute safety net bounds the damage. The kiosk has none, and its recovery is `visibilitychange`, which a wall kiosk never fires, so a kiosk in that state keeps showing the last patient's name and photos to whoever sits down next. This answers the plan's laptop-sleep question: the indicator is right only if the tab was hidden for more than 2 minutes or Chrome itself tore the socket down on resume | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

That answers the plan's storm question: a burst costs one day-query per frame per open board. | FE-F11-17 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `useAppointments.ts:106-126` · `loaders.ts:388-436` · `PatientManagement.tsx:485-490` · `WorkComponent.tsx:472` | **A walk-in checked in from the patient screens can be missing from the board you return to.** The board's only live refresh is SSE, and its listener exists only while the page is mounted. The two quick check-ins invalidate `qk.patient` and `qk.calendar`, not `qk.appointments.daily`. On return, `dailyAppointmentsLoader` fetches the day again, but that result is only `initialData`, which React Query ignores when the key is already cached; a cache under 30 s old is fresh, so nothing refetches. Runtime (twice): board → *Search* → quick check-in → *Appointments* about 5 s later → the only GET was the loader's, and it **contained** the walk-in, but the board did not show it. It was **still missing at +34 s, +64 s and +124 s** with no further GET; nothing would fetch until the 5-minute safety net or another desk's write. (An earlier, noisier run did show the walk-in after an extra refetch whose trigger I did not identify; the two clean runs both reproduced.) The booking forms have the same gap for a same-day booking: create, edit and delete invalidate `qk.patient` and `qk.calendar` only. F2's map lists `appointments.daily` as "never invalidated, and that is correct"; that holds for other desks, not for the desk that made the write. **Fix:** invalidate `qk.appointments.daily(today)` in both quick check-ins, and have the loader `setQueryData` (or go through `loaderQuery`) so its fetch is not thrown away | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

**FE-F11-1 — the times are converted twice.** `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized` stores `presentTime: fmtTimeStr(r.present)`, and `fmtTimeStr` is `formatTime12(t)` with `withMeridiem` left `false`, so `13:08:00` leaves the server as `"01:08"`. The query's own comment on the sort shows the author knew that string loses the half of the day ("13:45→'01:45' which would collate PM check-ins before AM"). `AppointmentCard.formatTime` then parses `"01:08"`, sees hour 1, and appends `t('card.am')`. Hours 12:xx come out right (12 → PM) and 00:xx–11:xx come out right, so only the afternoon is wrong, which in this clinic is most of the day. Why 🟠: it is on every checked-in card and every undo tooltip, all afternoon, and staff use these stamps to see how long a patient has waited. **Fix:** send the raw `HH:MM:SS` for the three state times (the card already formats it with a localized marker, and the server's sort already uses the raw value), and declare them in `appointmentRowSchema` so the next change is caught. Do it together with FE-F11-11(c): the board should format `apptime` the same way instead of receiving "07:30 PM".

**FE-F11-2 — an action error is not a load error.** `runStateChange` keeps the right half of the contract: a 400 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` is recovered silently with a reload. Every other failure (a dropped connection, a 408, a 500 such as `updatePresent`'s "Appointment not found" for a row another desk just deleted) goes to `setActionError(httpErrorMessage(err, failMessage))`. The hook returns `error: actionError ?? (query error)`, and `DailyAppointments` renders its whole-page `errorMessage` for any `error`. The next successful `runStateChange` is the only thing that resets `actionError`. The SSE refetch doesn't, and neither does a date change, and neither does the 5-minute net. **Fix:** surface action failures as a toast (the conflict path already uses `window.toast`), keep `error` for the read alone, and give the read-error state the header plus a Retry, so a failed read of *another date* is not a dead end either.

**FE-F11-3 — CLOSED is final, and only the kiosk knows.** From the EventSource spec: a network error means "reconnect", but a response that is not `200 text/event-stream` means "fail the connection". Express itself refuses connections while it boots (it listens only after `mountRoutes`), so a direct client reconnects. Every proxy in front of it answers **502** instead: Caddy on the LAN, the tunnel remotely, and Vite in dev. `ChairDisplay` handles this with a delayed reload (`CLOSED_STREAM_RELOAD_DELAY_MS`); `sse-appointments` emits `error` and waits for a `visibilitychange` longer than 2 minutes, a `pageshow`, or the refcount dropping to zero. The same code is in `sse-whatsapp.ts`. **Fix:** in the channel (the FE-F1-7 extraction RA4 deferred to "F11 or F16"), treat CLOSED as "retry with backoff while refcount > 0". Let a 401 take the auth path: the funnel's next read will redirect. Do it with FE-F11-7's watchdog, since both belong in the same place.

**Note for FE-F3-8's remediation (the casts).** The card's real readers are `appointment_id`, `person_id`, `dr_id`, `patient_name`, `patient_type`, `patient_type_id`, `patient_type_name_ar`, `apptime`, `app_detail`, `present_time`, `seated_time`, `dismissed_time`, `hasActiveAlert`, `is_ortho_visit` and `has_visit` (a boolean from the server, typed `boolean | number | null` on the client). **Do not** widen the schema with `present` / `seated` / `dismissed`: delete their readers (FE-F11-9). FE-F11-1 changes the three time fields' format, so declare them after that fix.

**Found along the way, not F11's to fix:**
- **`Caddyfile`** still carries two `@websocket` matchers and `reverse_proxy @websocket` blocks, and the server's boot banner prints `local.shwan-orthodontics.com`. WebSockets are retired (CLAUDE.md); both are this deployment's config in the product. File with **F26** (or a deployment pass).
- **`chair-payload-builder.ts`** returns `null` when the payload build fails, after the epoch is bumped, so the kiosk is neither updated nor cleared. It is safe today only because `PatientShell` sends *cleared* before *loaded* (FE-F4-10 is about that order). Backend; note it with FE-F4-10.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp** (F10's hand-down). On the board it is right: a walk-in's "time" is the minute it arrived, and it sorts correctly. Its calendar consequence is FE-F10-2. Nothing more to file.

## F10 · Calendar + booking workflow · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: `AppointmentCalendar` 791 · `CalendarGrid` 631 · `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` 527 · `EditAppointmentForm` 448 · `AppointmentForm` 402 · `CalendarHeader` 217 · `HolidayQuickModal` 213 · `PatientAppointments` 211 · `MonthlyCalendarGrid` 208 · `AnalogClock` 204 · `CalendarDayContextMenu` 201 · `calendar.types` 115 · `CalendarContextMenu` 105 · `doctorColors` 89 · `DoctorFilter` 75 · `CalendarLegend` 41 · `routes/Calendar` 12 — 4,490 LOC — plus the five CSS modules (2,022 lines) and the global `css/components/appointment-calendar.css` (1,289). `PeriodNavigator` (106) is in the plan's list but belongs to Statistics: F5 read it and FE-F5-8 covers it, so it is not re-derived here. Cross-checks the surface forced: `calendar.contract.ts`, `appointment.contract.ts`, `holiday.contract.ts`; the five calendar factories and `qk.calendar.*`; `ContentRenderer`'s three appointment branches; the server halves the client depends on — `calendar.routes.ts` (all seven handlers), `CalendarViewService.ts`, `calendar-queries.ts` (`getWeeklyCalendarSlots`, `getCalendarStats`, `fillCalendar`), `AppointmentService.ts` (create / update / quick check-in rules), `appointment.routes.ts` (PUT / DELETE / broadcast), `appointment-queries.ts#APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT`, `lookup-admin-queries.ts#tblHolidays`, `employee-queries.ts` (the three doctor lists), `staff.routes.ts#/doctors`; the route-table gates for every endpoint above; `services/setup/` (what a fresh install seeds for the calendar) and `1789460500000_seed-product-constants.sql` (the `Clinic` employee); the i18n ratchet for the four booking files (`eslint.config.js`, `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`, the `index.html` FOUC check — all in sync).

**Runtime-verified in a cloud container, not on the owner's dev box.** Like F9 and RB3: a **throwaway** PostgreSQL 16, installed with plain `npm run db:migrate` on an empty database, then `npm run db:setup -- --yes …` and `npm run db:seed:demo` (20 fictional patients). `MACHINE_PATH` was an empty scratch folder; the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init and the drift check were off by env; dev ran on Vite `:5173` + Express `:3001`. The throwaway database was first left on UTC (the clock check flagged it) and was set to `Asia/Baghdad` before the time-sensitive checks. **Fixtures added by SQL:** a walk-in-shaped appointment at 15:07, one on the seeded demo holiday, six filling 12:00–14:30 on one day, one Implant booking, one at 20:30 with the late slots configured as `20:00,20:30`, and `get_appointments = true` on the demo Assistant. **Real writes to the throwaway DB**, where the write was the point: one appointment Update (unchanged values), one calendar delete, the Assistant booking (refused, 400), and four API bookings into one slot. **Aborted:** the drag-reschedule PUT, and every `/api/wa/send-appointment`. **Clock:** the month-arithmetic check fixed the page clock to Sat 2026-10-31 (`page.clock.setFixedTime`); the server clock was real. The probes (`scripts/e2e/.f10-probe*.tmp.mjs`) launched Chromium through `executablePath`; they, the temporary `.env`, the session file and the scratch folder were deleted, the database and role were dropped, and PostgreSQL was stopped. No screenshots. **No live-data facts are recorded for F10**; the queries the owner can run are in the F10 clean list.

Narrowing the window below 768 px forces a 1-day view **and persists** `cal-day-count = 1`, so the desktop zoom is lost on widening. `AnalogClock` (the chair display's clock) memoizes behind an `exhaustive-deps` disable, which makes React Compiler skip the component (harmless at one render a second) | ✅ fixed (RC2) |

**FE-F10-1 — the calendar hands the edit form a row it cannot read.** `CalendarContextMenu.handleEdit` navigates to `/patient/:id/edit-appointment/:apptId` with `state: { appointment }`, where `appointment` is the grid's `CalendarAppointment`: `{appointment_id, appDetail, drID, patientName, personID, slotStatus, slotDateTime, app_date, person_id}`. `EditAppointmentForm` prefers router state over its own fetch (`enabled: !existingAppointment`) and seeds `AppDetail: appt.app_detail || ''`, `DrID: String(appt.dr_id || '')`. Both keys are absent, so both selects open on their placeholder. Someone already noticed half of this: `CalendarViewService` adds `app_date` and `person_id` as "compatibility" aliases for exactly this form, and `calendar.contract.ts` documents them, but not the other two. The patient list's router state is the DB row (`APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT`: `app_detail`, `dr_id`), so that path seeds correctly. That is why this looks intermittent. Runtime: Implant with Karwan Omar opened from the calendar → `#doctor` `""`, `#details` `""`, *Selected time* "Sun, Oct 4, 5:00 PM"; the stored row is `{app_detail: "Implant", dr_id: 5}`. Validation then refuses Save until both are re-picked, so the damage is a **wrong** pick, not a silent blank: the appointment moves to another doctor's filter, legend colour and reminder. Why 🟠: it is the calendar's only edit path, it fails every time, and it invites a data change. **Fix:** always fetch `appointmentByIdQuery` (router state at most as `placeholderData`), which also closes the stale-state half: a Back/Forward into an old edit entry, or a stale list row (FE-F10-10), re-seeding pre-edit values into a whole-row PUT. Or at least map `appDetail`/`drID`.

**FE-F10-2 — the grid shows the slot table, not the appointments.** `getWeeklyCalendarSlots` is `FROM calendar LEFT JOIN appointments ON ta.app_date = tc.app_date`, so an appointment exists on the calendar only if a `calendar` row has **exactly** its timestamp, and a day exists only if it has `calendar` rows. `transformToCalendarStructure` builds days from those rows; `/range` then keeps only the configured `times` minus the hidden early/late lists, and `CalendarGrid` draws only those rows (the header's "N appts" sums the same rows). Four ways a real appointment falls out, three confirmed at runtime:
- **Past days.** `fillCalendar()` opens with `DELETE FROM calendar WHERE app_date < CURRENT_DATE`, and a fresh install's first fill starts today. So last week renders "Loading calendar data..." forever, and this week shows only today onward under a "6 days" title. Whether the live clinic has history depends on when *Regenerate* was last pressed; each press erases it.
- **Walk-ins.** `quickCheckIn` inserts `app_date = now` (e.g. 15:07:23), which is never a slot, so no walk-in is ever on the calendar, the month counts or the stats. The Daily Appointments list has them.
- **Hidden rows.** With `CALENDAR_SHOW_EXTENDED_SLOTS_DEFAULT = false`, early/late appointments are in the payload but have no row. The option's name suggests a user toggle, and the calendar has none. A time deleted in Calendar Times keeps its future `calendar` rows, and the picker keeps offering it (`/available-slots` reads `calendar`, not `times`), but the grid has no row for it.
- **The horizon.** Nothing extends `calendar`: *Regenerate* and `db:setup` are the only writers, and the `numbers` tally covers 0–366 days. `noteCalendarRange` logs a warning nobody reads. After the horizon, the picker shows every day as unbookable with no explanation.

**Fix (owner decision; server-shaped):** drive the grid from `appointments` (bucket each into its slot, or into a nearest-row / "other times" row) and keep `calendar` only for availability. At minimum: stop deleting past rows, render a row for every time that has an appointment, and give the grid the extended toggle the picker has.

**FE-F10-4 — the loading screen is the whole screen.** `calendarRangeQuery` and `calendarMonthQuery` have no `placeholderData`, so a new key (another week, another zoom, another view) starts with `data: undefined`. `AppointmentCalendar` returns its full-page `calendar-loading` block whenever `loading && !calendarData`, and that replaces `CalendarHeader` too. The comment above it says the opposite: "refetches (nav, zoom, mutations) keep the current grid visible". The worst case is the zoom slider: the 250 ms debounce moves the key, the header unmounts, and the `<input type="range">` being dragged disappears. Runtime (`/range` delayed 1.5 s): after Next, `.cal-bar` count 0 and "Loading Calendar..." shown; the slider was present at +150 ms and gone at +550 ms. **Fix:** `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` on both factories (the grid already dims nothing, so an `isFetching` hint in the toolbar is enough), and keep the full-page state for the first load only.

**FE-F10-7 — the per-slot limit is a UI suggestion.** `MaxAppointmentsPerSlot` is read in exactly two places: `transformToCalendarStructure`, which labels a slot `full`, and the month summary. `validateAndCreateAppointment` and `validateAndUpdateAppointment` never read it. The picker disables `full` tiles, from a read up to 30 s old, invalidated only by the booking desk's own writes. The calendar drag and the edit form don't look at capacity at all. So the plan's concurrency question has a short answer: two front-desk PCs taking the last seat both get 200. Runtime: four POSTs into 2026-10-07 16:00 with the default 3 → `[200, 200, 200, 200]`, slot `full` with 4. The scale half is the bigger question for the product. The limit is per **slot**, not per **doctor**, so a 20-doctor center (the CLAUDE.md ceiling: ~28 appointments per 30-minute slot) must set it near 30, and then it no longer limits any one doctor. **Decide:** enforce it on the server (a count under a lock, in the transaction), per doctor or per clinic, or relabel it as a soft warning.

**FE-F10-11 — two literals that make every new center look like this one.** `FIXED_DEFAULT_COLORS` and `DEFAULT_PICKER_HEX` are keyed by `employees.id` 1 and 7, with this clinic's doctors' names in the comments. The fresh-install seed inserts `Clinic` first, so it is id 1 (runtime: `SELECT id … → 1 | Clinic`), and the "neutral Clinic bucket" the legend and grid comments describe is amber on every new deployment. `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` treats `14:00`/`14:30` as "rarely used afternoon" slots regardless of settings, and falls back to this clinic's early/late lists when the options are missing. `db:setup` seeds 10:00–20:30 slots and no options, so a new center's picker hides its own midday. The server's `/range` treats missing options as "hide nothing", so the grid and the picker disagree. **Fix:** delete the id-keyed defaults (a colourless doctor is neutral, or give new doctors a palette colour when created), delete the `14:00`/`14:30` literals, and treat missing options as empty lists on the client too.

**Found along the way, not F10's to fix:**
- **`CalendarTimesSettings` saves the three extended-slot options but invalidates only `qk.settings.option(…)`**, not `qk.calendar.all()`. The grid's rows are computed server-side from the same options, so /calendar keeps the old rows for up to 30 s after a save. File with **F21**.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp choice** (`app_date` = the check-in moment) is F11's surface. FE-F10-2 records only what it does to the calendar.

## F9 · Clinical: visits, diagnosis, imaging links · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: `pages/Diagnosis` 957 · `NewVisitComponent` 555 · `VisitsComponent` 295 · `WebCephModal` 405 · `TeethSelector` 286 · `ThreeShapeScansView` 180 · `XraysComponent` 170 · `DentalChart` 80 — 2,928 LOC — plus their eight CSS modules (2,722 lines). Cross-checks the surface forced: `ContentRenderer`'s `visits`/`new-visit`/`work`/`diagnosis`/`xrays`/`scans` branches and the diagnosis route in `routes.config.tsx`; `diagnosisQuery`, `visitByIdQuery`, `visitsByWorkQuery`, `latestWiresQuery`, `webcephLinkQuery`, the two 3Shape factories and `qk.work.*`/`qk.visit.*`; `visit.contract.ts`, `work.contract.ts#diagnosis`, `media.contract.ts` (WebCeph), `threeshape.contract.ts`; the server halves the client depends on: `diagnosis.routes.ts` + `diagnosis-queries.ts` (the upsert), `visit.routes.ts` + `visit-queries.ts` (the photo-flag roll-up into `works`), `media.routes.ts` + `webceph-service.ts` (guards, timeouts, race), `services/threeshape/{client,dtos}.ts` + the 3Shape API PDF (tooth numbering), `patient-assets.service.ts` (the X-ray list), the `/clinic-assets` static mount, `/api/patients/:id/xray`, `whatsapp-media.routes.ts#sendmedia2` + `SendMessage`'s `?file=` seed; the `works` CHECK constraints and `unq_tblwork_active`; the route-table gates for every F9 endpoint; the `tooth_numbers` table and `public/images/teeth/chart/*.svg`.

**Runtime-verified, but NOT on the owner's dev box.** This session ran in a cloud container with no access to the clinic database (the owner offered mirror credentials; declined, because the mirror is two-way, so any write there reaches the clinic DB through reverse sync, and a read-only role was not needed to audit the code). Instead: a **throwaway local PostgreSQL 16** built from `migrations/pg` (the baseline applied with `psql` and `db:baseline:stamp`, because `db:migrate` cannot install a fresh database, see *Found along the way*), seeded with **fake rows only**: 2 patients, 2 ortho works, 2 visits, 1 diagnosis with ceph values, 3 wires, 2 employees, the work statuses and patient types. Dev ran on Vite `:5173` + Express `:3001`, with the three CDC sinks off, WhatsApp auto-init off and the drift check off. The Playwright probe (`scripts/e2e/.f9-probe.tmp.mjs`, deleted afterwards; no screenshots) launched the preinstalled Chromium through `executablePath`, because the harness's bare `chromium.launch()` finds no browser for the pinned Playwright in that container. **Real writes to the throwaway DB** were made where the write was the point: the first visit update, the final-photo visit, and the two constraint failures. **Aborted and recorded:** the diagnosis POST and the second visit PUT. **Mocked reads:** `GET /api/diagnosis/1` → 500, `GET /api/webceph/patient-link/1` → 500, and the 3Shape cases/media lists. The one `sendmedia2` call used `prog=Telegram` and was refused at the path guard, before any send. **No live-data facts are recorded for F9**: the queries the owner can run are in the F9 clean list.

### Findings

The fallback is broken as well: the placeholder's global `hidden` class loses the cascade to the module's `.placeholder {display:flex}` (runtime: class `… hidden`, computed `flex`), so a thumbnail that did load would have "View X-ray" stacked under it. UNN 14–16 is **UL6–UL8**; read as FDI, the other notation clinics use, it is **UR4–UR6**, the opposite side of the mouth. Cancel, the ← Back button, the sidebar, the header tabs and closing the browser tab all discard the typing silently. Also: Diagnosis navigates away on a 500 ms `setTimeout` that is never cleared and re-enables both buttons during it (FE-F6-9's shape), so leaving the page inside that window still yanks the user back to Works | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

Add/Edit started from Visit History lands on **Works**, not back on the history. The error banner's × clears only the delete error, so a load error cannot be dismissed. It opens from the **translated** patient-info page, so in Arabic it renders **English under `dir="rtl"`** (runtime: `html[dir=rtl]`, modal `direction: rtl`, English title), the mirrored English CLAUDE.md says untranslated UI must never get. It is not in the ratchet: translate it, or pin the modal body `dir="ltr"`. A failed **or still-loading** link read renders the **"Create Patient in WebCeph"** card, with no error shown (runtime: link GET 500 → Create card, 0 error text); the server's duplicate guard catches the click. The success timers are never cleared and overlap across uploads, and the file input is reset through `document.getElementById` | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

Runtime: both cards read *Invalid Date*, one with no `meta` file and one whose `seriesDate` was `20260301` (DICOM `YYYYMMDD`; the real CS-Imaging format is unverified — the owner can check one `meta` file). The browser-locale formatting is FE-F3-3's census, not re-filed | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

A retry is harmless (`overwrite: true`), but the message is wrong | ✅ fixed (RB3 + follow-up: race defaults to Caucasian, owner's call) |
