**Never invalidated by anything, and that is correct** — do not file these: every `lookups.*` leaf except `labs`/`employees`/`costPresets` (seed/controlled vocabulary, edited only through `adminLookups.table`) — **and except `lookups.patientPhones`, which is patient data, not vocabulary: F6 moved it to the defect list (FE-F6-9)**, every `reports.*` leaf (read-only rollups keyed by period), `auth.me`, `adminLookups.tables`, `settings.{supabase,dolphin,telegram}Status` + `telegram.sendProgress` + `tvDisplay` (own `refetchInterval`), `threeshape.*` (live pull, `retry:false`), `media.photoTypes`, `dailyInvoices`, ~~`visit.byId`~~ (**F9: wrong — it is a defect, FE-F9-2**: the edit form seeds from it and a save never refreshes it), `appointments.daily` + `whatsapp.messages`/`messageCount` (SSE-driven — the server's `broadcastDays()` fans out on every appointment write), `labCases.*` + `slideshow.list` + `portalActivity.list` + `approvals.list`/`history` + `announcements.*` (refetched by their own screens' `refetch()`/`refetchInterval`).

**Never invalidated and that is a defect** — the `qk.calendar.*` family: see FE-F2-1.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F2-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | 5 write sites | **no appointment write outside the calendar screen invalidates `qk.calendar.*`** — the booking slot picker can offer a slot that was taken seconds earlier | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `query/loaderQuery.ts:39` + `router/loaders.ts:121` | both 401 branches are **unreachable**: `public/index.html`'s `window.fetch` interceptor strips the status first, so session expiry renders the generic 500 card and `queryClient.clear()` never runs | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `query/client.ts:58` | `retry: 2` retries **every** error including 4xx, though the comment says "transient network/5xx" — six factories already hand-patch `retry: false` | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientAppointments.tsx:56` | deleting an appointment invalidates `patient.appointments` but not its sibling `patient.hasAppointment`, so the Works screen keeps claiming a future appointment | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-5 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/routes.config.tsx:502` | the `/calendar` route is annotated "100% SSE-driven real-time data" — it has **no SSE at all**; this is the comment that makes FE-F2-1 look like it can't happen | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-6 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/loaders.ts:415` | `patientManagementLoader` hand-rolls 5 raw `fetchJSON` reads that duplicate 5 existing `queries.ts` factories and land **outside** the RQ cache | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-7 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/loaders.ts:231` | the diagnosis deep-link is the one patient route with **no chunk warm-up** — its route has no `:page` segment, so `preloadPatientPage(page)` no-ops | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-8 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `query/keys.ts:96,102` | `qk.appointments` and `qk.whatsapp` are **namespace fictions** — their leaves have different root segments, so no parent key can invalidate the group | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F2-9 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `router/loaders.ts:207-278` | `patientShellLoader`'s entire return payload is unread — `PatientShell` has no `useLoaderData`; the loader is purely a prefetcher | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-10 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `query/keys.ts`, `queries.ts`, `loaders.ts` | 10 `qk` keys with zero references, plus the 4 FE-F0-1 items this session was asked to adjudicate (verdict below) | 🟨 partial (RA4) |
| FE-F2-11 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `config/labStages.ts:31` | the framework-try-in label keys off `MATERIAL_OPTIONS[0]/[1]/[3]` **by index** — inserting a material silently relabels a lab stage | ⬜ open |
| FE-F2-12 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `ContentRenderer.tsx:233,244,255` | three "coming soon" placeholder pages (`details`/`history`/`messages`) shipped and reachable by URL, unreachable from the nav | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `query/useApiMutation.ts` + `App.tsx:110` | `useApiMutation` — CLAUDE.md's "standard for new mutations" — is used by **4 files** against ~100 hand-rolled invalidate sites; `App.tsx`'s docblock still advertises the deleted 5-minute cache | ⬜ open |

**FE-F2-1 — the booking picker can hand out a slot that was taken 10 seconds ago.**
Five write paths create, move or delete an appointment from outside `/calendar`, and **not one of them touches `qk.calendar.*`**: `AppointmentForm.tsx:231` (create) and `EditAppointmentForm.tsx:280` (reschedule) invalidate only `qk.patient.all(personId)`; `PatientAppointments.tsx:56` (delete) invalidates only `qk.patient.appointments`; and `WorkComponent.tsx:462` + `PatientManagement.tsx:464` (quick check-in — which **creates** a same-day appointment) invalidate **nothing at all**. The stale reads are `qk.calendar.slots(date,'all')` and `qk.calendar.availability(...)` — which are exactly what `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` renders, i.e. the control inside those same two forms whose job is to stop a double-book — plus `qk.calendar.range/month/stats` behind `AppointmentCalendar`. Nothing self-heals it: the calendar subscribes to **no** SSE (grep: no `EventSource`, no `sse-appointments`, no `refetchInterval` anywhere in `AppointmentCalendar.tsx` or `components/react/calendar/`), and its only refresh is the manual `refetch()` at `AppointmentCalendar.tsx:259`. Failure scenario: front desk books patient A into 2026-09-17 10:00, closes the form, opens `/patient/2/new-appointment` for patient B within the 30 s global `staleTime`, picks 2026-09-17 → `availableSlotsQuery('2026-09-17')` serves the cached pre-booking slot list → 10:00 still shows free → double-booked. Bounded by `staleTime: 30_000`, which is why it is 🟠 and not 🔴, but the window is the exact duration of "next patient, same day". Fix: add `qk.calendar.all()` (and `qk.patient.all(personId)` for the two that invalidate nothing) to all five sites. See also FE-F2-5 — the route comment asserts this is impossible — and FE-F2-8 for why there is no `qk.appointments.all()` to reach for.

**FE-F2-2 — the server-state layer's 401 handling is dead code; `index.html` owns it.**
`loaderQuery` (`:39`) and `withAuth` (`loaders.ts:121`) both branch on `httpErr.status === 401` to clear the cache and redirect. Neither can fire in a browser. `public/index.html:231` installs a classic-script `window.fetch` patch that, on **any** 401, sets `window.location.href='/login.html'` and returns `Promise.reject(new Error('Authentication required'))` — a bare `Error` with no `status` and no `data`. `core/http.ts` calls the bare global `fetch` (`:318`, `:78`), and the patch is installed during parse while the app is a deferred module that additionally waits for `window.onload`, so the patched fetch is always the one in play. The 401 `Response` object therefore never reaches `handleResponse`. Consequences, in order of how much they matter: (1) `loaderQuery` falls through to its final branch and throws `Response(…, 500)`, so an expired session renders `RouteError`'s **generic** "something went wrong" card during the redirect instead of the purpose-built `error.status === 401` → "Unauthorized / You need to log in" card that sits unreachable at `RouteError.tsx:56`; (2) `queryClient.clear()` — commented "session over — don't leave cached data for the next user" — never runs. (2) is **not** a data leak: there is no query persister anywhere in `public/js` (grep: no `persistQueryClient`/`createSyncStoragePersister`), so the cache is in-memory and the hard navigation destroys it regardless. Fix is a decision, not a patch: either delete the two dead branches and document `index.html` as the 401 owner, or move 401 handling into `core/http.ts` and drop the interceptor (which FE-F0-5 already flags as a second client layer).

**FE-F2-3 — `retry: 2` is not the policy the comment describes.**
`query/client.ts:58` sets a bare numeric `retry: 2`, and TanStack Query retries *every* rejected query that many times — 400s and 404s included, with a 1 s → 2 s backoff. The file's own header says "retry 2 — transient network/5xx retry for idempotent reads", which is the policy `core/http.ts` implements for its own opt-in retry layer (`isRetriable`, `:265`), not this one. The two layers do not stack today only because `fetchJSON` defaults `retries` to 0 and no factory overrides it. Observable cost: a read that legitimately 404s — `labCaseQuery` on a deleted case, `expenseByIdQuery`, `transferPreviewQuery` on a removed work — costs 3 requests and ~3 s before the screen can say "not found". The tell is in the file itself: `threeShapeCasesQuery`/`threeShapeMediaQuery` (`:1502,1514`) carry `retry: false` with the comment "a not-connected / unreachable error is actionable, not transient", and `optionQuery` (`:1344`), `diagnosisQuery` (`:1101`) and both exchange-rate factories (`:722,735`) carry it too — six of 127. Fix: make the default a predicate that skips 4xx, and delete the local opt-outs it subsumes.

**FE-F2-4 — a sibling key the parent would have covered.**
`handleDelete` invalidates `qk.patient.appointments(personId)` = `['patient', id, 'appointments']`. `qk.patient.hasAppointment(personId)` = `['patient', id, 'has-appointment']` is its **sibling**, not its child, so it is untouched. `hasAppointmentQuery` backs the works screen's future-appointment flag. Scenario: delete the patient's only future appointment from the Appointments tab, click back to Works within 30 s → the screen still says the patient has an upcoming appointment. One-character class of fix: invalidate `qk.patient.all(personId)`, which is what the other 16 patient write sites do.

**FE-F2-5 — the comment that makes FE-F2-1 invisible.** `routes.config.tsx` labels `/calendar` "Monthly Calendar (100% SSE-driven)" with "// No loader - 100% SSE-driven real-time data". The calendar is 100% React Query: three `useQuery`s (`calendarRangeQuery`/`calendarMonthQuery`/`calendarStatsQuery`) and one manual `invalidateQueries(qk.calendar.all())`. The sibling comments on `/send`, `/send-message` and `/auth` are accurate (those really are SSE), which is what makes this one costly: an author adding an appointment write reads "SSE-driven" and correctly concludes they need no invalidation.

**FE-F2-6 — the one loader that does not use the cache.**
Every other loader goes through `loaderQuery` → `ensureQueryData`. `patientManagementLoader` instead issues five raw `fetchJSON` calls (`/api/patients/phones`, `/api/getworktypes`, `/api/getworkkeywords`, `/api/patients/tag-options`, `/api/patients/type-options`) and returns them as loader data — duplicating `patientPhonesQuery`, `workTypesQuery`, `workKeywordsQuery`, `tagOptionsQuery` and `typeOptionsQuery`, which already exist in `queries.ts` and already carry the same schemas. Three consequences: (a) **this is why `typeOptionsQuery` is dead** (FE-F0-1) — its only would-be consumer inlines the fetch; (b) the five results never enter the RQ cache, so a work type or patient tag edited through Settings → Lookups (which invalidates `qk.adminLookups.table`) cannot reach the patient-management dropdowns until a full route re-navigation; (c) `emptyOnHttpError` (`:35`) converts **any** HTTP status into an empty array, so a 403 or a 500 on one lookup renders a silently empty dropdown with no error anywhere. The screen's loader exception is legitimate (URL-param search + scroll restoration) — the exception is about *search*, not about bypassing the cache for five lookups.

**FE-F2-7 — the one patient route with the waterfall the design removes everywhere else.**
`patientShellLoader` opens with `preloadPatientPage(page)` to warm the tab's chunk in parallel with the data fetch. `page` comes from `params.page`. The diagnosis route is `':personId/work/:workId/diagnosis'` — it has **no `:page` segment**, so `page` is `undefined`, `preloadPatientPage` returns at its first guard, and `pages/Diagnosis.tsx` (957 LOC, the largest patient sub-page) only begins downloading after `PatientShell` mounts and renders it. `PatientShell` gets this right for rendering — it derives `effectivePage = isDiagnosisRoute ? 'diagnosis' : page` at `:121` — the loader just never got the same treatment. Fix: mirror that derivation in the loader.

**FE-F2-8 — two `qk` groups whose members share no prefix.** `qk.appointments.daily(d)` → `['daily-appointments', d]` but `qk.appointments.byId(id)` → `['appointments','by-id',id]`; `qk.whatsapp.messages(d)` → `['whatsapp-messages', d]` but `qk.whatsapp.messageCount(d)` → `['whatsapp-message-count', d]`. Both are annotated "keeps the legacy key shape", and `keys.test.ts:40` pins the two legacy shapes deliberately — so this is known, not accidental. The cost is that the JS object shape promises a hierarchy the cache does not have: there is no key that invalidates "this day's appointments" across both leaves, and no `appointments.all()` for a write site to reach for (FE-F2-1). Either give each group a real shared root and migrate the two SSE hooks' keys in one commit, or drop the grouping objects so the shape stops implying a prefix.

**FE-F2-9 — a 90-line loader result nobody reads.** `PatientShellLoaderResult` declares six fields; `patientShellLoader` builds them with three `as` casts (`as PatientData | null`, `as WorkData | null`, `as TimepointData[]`). `PatientShell.tsx` contains **no `useLoaderData`** — it reads `useParams` for routing and the RQ cache for data (3 `useQuery`s), which is precisely the documented design ("thin prefetchers into the React Query cache"). So the whole payload, the result interface, and the three casts are dead weight, and `currentPage` in particular is a decoy: `Navigation`'s `currentPage` prop comes from `PatientShell`'s own `effectivePage`, not from here. `labTrackingLoader` shows the shape this should be — `Promise<null>`. (Deleting the casts also removes three of the tree's remaining unchecked bridges out of a parsed contract payload.)

**FE-F2-10 — the dead inventory, and the FE-F0-1 verdicts this session owed.**
- **10 `qk` keys with zero references anywhere**: `aligner.work`, `adminLookups.all`, `stand.items.all`, `stand.sales.all`, `users.all`, `portalActivity.all`, `approvals.all`, `slideshow.all`, `settings.all`, `threeshape.all`. Nine are documented parent/prefix keys that no write ever needed (`stand.all()` is used instead of `stand.items.all()`, etc.) — harmless as documentation of the hierarchy, and `keys.test.ts` does not cover them. `aligner.work(workId)` is the odd one: it is not a parent of anything, its comment claims "Parent for a work's aligner data (sets/batches)", and the aligner sets screen keys off `qk.work.all` instead (`PatientSets:554`). That one is genuinely dead.
- **`patientInfoLoader` + `workDetailsLoader` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: superseded, not a lost-loader regression.** Repo-wide grep finds each exactly once, at its own declaration; `routes.config.tsx` imports eight loaders and neither is among them. `patientShellLoader` does strictly more than both (it prefetches patient info **and** work details **and** timepoints, and warms the page chunk) and is wired to every `/patient/*` route including `:personId/work/:workId/diagnosis`. No route flashes empty. Delete both, with `PatientInfoLoaderResult`/`WorkDetailsLoaderResult`.
- **`typeOptionsQuery` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: dead because of FE-F2-6**, not because a screen stopped needing it. It comes back to life the moment `patientManagementLoader` uses the factory.
- **`approvalsHistoryQuery` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: the screen was never built.** `GET /api/approvals/history` is a live, contracted server route with a client factory and **no consumer anywhere** — the maker-checker audit trail is unreachable in the UI. Not a client-side cleanup: **F5 owns the call** (build the screen or retire the endpoint).

**FE-F2-11 — a label that depends on array order.** `labStages.ts:31` reads `MATERIAL_OPTIONS[0]`, `[1]` and `[3]` positionally to decide between "Zirconia Core Try-In", "Metal Try-In" and the generic label. `MATERIAL_OPTIONS` is typed `readonly string[]` (the `as const` is widened away by the annotation), so TypeScript cannot check the indices and the inline `// 'Zirconia'` / `// PFM / Full Metal` comments are the only thing holding it together. Inserting one material before index 3 relabels a stage on the `/lab-tracking` board and on the work card. Fix: drop the `readonly string[]` annotation so `as const` gives literals, then compare against the literals by name.

**FE-F2-12 — three placeholder pages in the shipped switch.** `ContentRenderer`'s `renderContent()` has 22 `case` labels; 19 map to a `lazyPage` chunk and three (`details`, `history`, `messages`) render a hardcoded "…view coming soon…" card. None appears in `Navigation`'s item list, so they are reachable only by typing `/patient/7/details`. They are also the only three page strings with no preloader entry, which is why a mechanical preloader-vs-switch diff flags them — worth recording so F4 does not re-derive it as drift.

**FE-F2-13 — the standard and the practice.** `useApiMutation` is imported by exactly four files (`TvDisplaySettings`, `PatientSlideshow`, `useLabCases`, `Announcements`); raw `useMutation` is imported by **zero**. The other ~100 invalidation sites are hand-written `await postJSON(...)` + `queryClient.invalidateQueries(...)` inside a `try/catch`, which is exactly the shape in which FE-F2-1 and FE-F2-4 happen (the invalidation is a separate statement a reviewer can forget, rather than a required field on the mutation). Not something to convert wholesale, but worth stating so later sessions do not each rediscover the gap. Related and free: `App.tsx`'s docblock still promises "5-minute cache for static data (patient info, work details)" and "Appointments, messaging use component-level fetching" — both from the pre-`query/client.ts` era (the real defaults are `staleTime` 30 s / `gcTime` 5 min).

---

## F1 · HTTP funnel + client core · 2026-09-16

Scope read in full: `core/` (10 modules + the 2 test files' coverage), `services/` (6), `utils/` (9) — 4,267 LOC. Two findings were verified against a running dev server (`:3101`), not just read.

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F1-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `core/http.ts:204` | a `{ schema }` read silently **skips validation** when the 200 response isn't JSON — and an unmatched `/api/*` path returns the SPA shell as 200 `text/html` | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F1-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `core/http.ts:454` + `SetFormDrawer.tsx:246`, `Videos.tsx:322` | uploads inherit the 30 s API timeout; the same endpoint's other call site already had to override it to 120 s | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F1-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `core/http.ts:77` | `fetchCsrfToken()` is the one fetch in the funnel with **no timeout**, and it is awaited *before* the timeout is armed | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F1-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `utils/whatsapp-api-client.ts:94` | the bespoke client **retries POSTs** (the funnel never does) and has no timeout; one retried POST has no server-side in-progress guard | ⬜ open |
| FE-F1-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `core/fileSystemAccess.ts:99` | every IndexedDB helper opens a connection it never closes, and `openDatabase()` has no `onblocked` → a future `DB_VERSION` bump hangs forever | ⬜ open |
| FE-F1-6 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `core/iniParser.ts:49` | the INI round-trip **destroys** comments and out-of-section keys — Settings → Protocol Handlers rewrites the whole file | ⬜ open |
| FE-F1-7 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `services/sse-whatsapp.ts` | byte-for-byte copy of `sse-appointments.ts` (~150 identical lines) — every fix must be made twice | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F1-8 | [ENH] | 🟡 | ~20 files | ~20 hand-rolled local `YYYY-MM-DD` builders while `utils/calendarDate.ts#toLocalDateString` is the SSoT | ⬜ open |
| FE-F1-9 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | 4 files | 15 more dead exports that the F0 scanner **could not see** (they are referenced by their own module's default-export object) | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F1-10 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}.ts` | three hand-rolled window-`CustomEvent` invalidation buses running parallel to React Query | ⬜ open — **F5 verdict: retire** (FE-F5-13) |
| FE-F1-11 | [ENH] | 🔵 | 6 spots | small ones, bundled (see below) | ✅ fixed (RA4) |

**FE-F1-1 — the fail-loud guard has a hole, and the server hands it exactly the payload that opens it.**
`handleResponse` only validates inside the `contentType?.includes('application/json')` branch; the else branch is `return response.text() as unknown as T`, schema ignored. Verified live on `:3101` with an authenticated session: `GET /api/does-not-exist` → **200, `text/html`, 11,167 bytes** — the SPA shell. There is no `/api` 404 terminator anywhere (`grep`ed `app/mount-routes.ts`, `routes/api/index.ts`, `middleware/`), so every unmatched `/api/*` GET falls through `express.static('./dist')` into `routes/web.ts`'s `'/*splat'` catch-all, which only 404s paths that *look like assets*. Failure scenario: an endpoint is renamed or removed server-side (ordinary deploy drift) while a client build still calls it → `fetchJSON<T>(url, { schema })` **resolves** with an 11 KB HTML string, React Query caches it as the payload, and the screen renders empty or dies later in a child with an unrelated `TypeError`. CLAUDE.md calls the client schema "the only runtime guard in prod"; this is the one path where it doesn't run. Fix in `core/http.ts`: when `schema` is supplied and the body is not JSON, throw the same fail-loud `HttpError` `validateResponse` throws. (A JSON 404 terminator for `/api` is the deeper fix but is backend scope.)

**FE-F1-2 — the 30 s timeout is an API timeout applied to uploads.**
`postFormData` inherits `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` (30 s). The server gives `POST /api/aligner/sets/:setId/upload-pdf` **120 s** (`timeouts.long`, `routes/api/aligner-file.routes.ts:54`), and `PatientSets.tsx:1061` matches it with `timeoutMs: 120000` plus a comment saying the 30 s default "would abort large PDFs client-side while the upload was still completing server-side". `SetFormDrawer.tsx:246` posts to **the same endpoint** with no override — and accepts files up to **100 MB** (its own size check at line 235). So the same upload succeeds from the sets list and fails from the set drawer: the client aborts at 30 s, the user gets "Failed to upload PDF", and the server finishes the upload (and the Drive copy) anyway — so a retry duplicates it. Related, and worth the owner's call: `POST /api/videos` (`Videos.tsx:322`, educational-video upload) and `POST /api/patients/:personId/files/upload` (`SequenceSidebar.tsx` ×2, `FileExplorer.tsx:183`) run on 30 s on **both** sides — no `timeouts.long` on either route — so a video larger than ~30 s of LAN transfer cannot be uploaded at all.

**FE-F1-3 — the CSRF pre-flight sits outside the timeout it is supposed to be covered by.**
In `attempt()`, `headers['x-csrf-token'] = await ensureCsrfToken()` (line 313) runs *before* `withTimeout(...)` (line 316), and `fetchCsrfToken()` (line 78) issues a bare `fetch` with no `signal`. A server that accepts the connection but never answers `/api/csrf-token` (proxy holding it open, event loop wedged) hangs **every mutation in the tab** with no abort, no rejection and no toast — the one failure mode the funnel's 30 s rule exists to prevent. Single-flight means one stuck fetch stalls all of them. Fix: give that fetch its own `AbortSignal.timeout`, and let the failure surface as an ordinary mutation error.

**FE-F1-4 — a second HTTP client with the opposite retry policy.**
`APIClient.request()` wraps **every** request — GET and POST alike — in `RetryManager.withRetry` (3 attempts, 1 s → 2 s backoff), whereas `core/http.ts` never retries a mutation by design. Three POSTs go through it (`WhatsAppSend.tsx`): `WA_SEND`, `MESSAGE_RESET`, and `SEND_EMAIL`. `WA_SEND` is safe — the server has an `alreadyInProgress` guard — and `MESSAGE_RESET` is idempotent, but **`POST /api/email/send-appointments` has no in-progress guard** (`routes/api/email.routes.ts:49`): it regenerates the appointment PDF and re-sends the staff email on each attempt, so one failed-but-delivered send (502 from the tunnel, a restart mid-send, any 5xx after SMTP handoff) becomes 2–3 identical emails to the whole recipient list. Staff-facing, not patient-facing — hence 🟡, not 🟠. Two more defects in the same file: (a) **no timeout at all** — only an explicit `AbortController`, so a stuck WhatsApp request leaves the button disabled forever; (b) `finally { this.abortControllers.delete(requestKey) }` deletes **by key**, so when a second request reuses a `cancelPrevious` key, the first one's teardown removes the *second's* controller and a later `cancelRequest(key)` silently does nothing.

**FE-F1-5 — IndexedDB connections are never closed.**
All seven handle helpers (`saveHandle`, `getHandle`, `removeHandle`, `listHandles`, `clearHandles`, …) call `openDatabase()` and never `db.close()`. Today: one live `IDBDatabase` per call on a long-lived tab (the chair PCs and the aligner screens call these repeatedly). The latent failure is the upgrade path — bump `DB_VERSION` to 2 and every open connection blocks it; `indexedDB.open` then fires only `blocked`, which nothing handles, so `openDatabase()`'s promise **never settles** and "remember this folder" spins with no error and no timeout. Fix: close after each op (or keep one cached connection) and reject on `onblocked`.

**FE-F1-6 — saving the INI deletes what the parser didn't understand.**
`ProtocolHandlersSettings` reads the file, `mergeConfigs`, `formatIniContent`, then `writeTextFile` — a **full overwrite** (lines 130 → 303-307). `parseIniContent` keeps only `[section]` headers and `key=value` lines inside a section: comments are skipped (line 62), keys before the first section are dropped (`equalIndex > 0 && currentSection`, line 77), an inline `; comment` stays glued to the value, and duplicate keys collapse. So the first save from Settings strips every hand-written note from `C:\ShwanOrtho\ProtocolHandlers.ini` — on machines where that file is edited by hand during setup. Either round-trip unknown lines or state the overwrite in the UI.

**FE-F1-7 — the two SSE singletons are one file typed twice.** Normalize the channel name (`sed 's/appointments/CH/'` vs `s/whatsapp/CH/'`) and the diff is 81 lines, **all of them comments plus the event-registration block**. The emitter surface, `getFreshness`/`markStale`, the refcount `ensureConnected`/`release`, `connect`/`disconnect`, and the visibility/`pageshow` handlers are identical. Extract `createSseChannel({ url, events, logName })`; `sse-appointments` can then use the `WIRE_EVENTS` array form its twin already uses.

**FE-F1-8 — ~20 copies of one date formatter.** SSoT is `utils/calendarDate.ts#toLocalDateString`. Re-implemented in: `core/utils.ts#formatISODate`, `hooks/useDateManager.ts#getLocalDateString`, `services/tasks.ts#dateFromTodayYmd`, `utils/formatters.ts:196`, and inline in `ExpiringItemsPanel`, `SalesHistoryTable`, `SaleDetailModal`, `PhotoSessionDialog`, `UniversalHeader`, `DailyInvoicesModal`, `EditAppointmentForm` (×2), `AppointmentForm`, `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` (×2), `Expenses`, `DailyAppointments`, `AppointmentsHeader`, `StandReports`, `Dashboard`. They agree today (all use local getters, none uses `toISOString()`), so this is upkeep, not a live bug — but it is 20 places to get the UTC-shift wrong in.

**FE-F1-9 — dead exports the F0 scan structurally could not find.** Each is referenced by its own module's `export default { … }` object, which makes a textual scan see a second use:
- `core/utils.ts` — `deepClone` and `formatPhoneNumber`: **0 callers**. (`formatPhoneNumber`'s `digits.startsWith('+' + countryCode)` branch at line 176 is unreachable regardless — `digits` has already been `\D`-stripped at line 170, so it can never contain `+`.)
- `core/storage.ts` — `clear()`: 0 callers (it would also wipe `shwan_theme`/`shwan_language`/`shwan_arabic_font`/`chairId`, so nothing *should* call it as-is).
- `core/iniParser.ts` — **8 of 12 exports unused** (~110 lines): `validateIniConfig`, `getValue`, `setValue`, `removeKey`, `getSectionKeys`, `getSections`, `hasKey`, `countKeys`. Its only consumer imports four names.
- `core/fileSystemAccess.ts` — `listHandles`, `clearHandles`, `getFileFromDirectory`, `isPermissionError` unused; `getHandle` and `pickFile` are internal-only and need not be exported.

**FE-F1-10 — three CustomEvent buses beside the server-state layer.** `TASKS_CHANGED_EVENT`, `APPROVALS_CHANGED_EVENT`, `PORTAL_ACTIVITY_CHANGED_EVENT` are hand-rolled `window.dispatchEvent` fan-outs telling bells to refetch, where `queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.<domain>() })` is the documented mechanism (CLAUDE.md: React Query "owns all staff-app server state"). The three modules are otherwise identical in shape — reads already moved to `query/queries.ts`, only mutations + the signal left. F2 owns the call.

**FE-F1-11 — bundled small ones:**
- `utils/calendarDate.ts:249` — `parseLocalDate(date instanceof Date ? date : date)`: both branches of the ternary are the same expression.
- `core/storage.ts:32` — `isLocalStorageAvailable()` does a `setItem`+`removeItem` probe on **every** get/set/remove, so one read costs three localStorage ops. Memoize the probe per page load.
- `core/fileSystemAccess.ts:410` — `readTextFile(handle, _encoding)` ignores `_encoding` and always decodes UTF-8; a Windows-1252/UTF-16 INI comes back with replacement characters. Drop the parameter or honour it.
- `core/http.ts:454` — `postFormData`'s `headers: {}` is vestigial (the real Content-Type removal is the `body instanceof FormData` branch at line 307), and its `...options` spread sits **after** `method`/`body`, so a caller-supplied `method` would silently win.
- `public/index.html` FOUC RTL check vs `core/language.ts#isRtlRoute` — the inline script prefix-matches `/expenses/` but exact-matches `/dashboard` and `/appointments`, while `isRtlRoute` prefix-matches all three. No route has children today, so it is latent; the two lists must move together (CLAUDE.md's 3-place ratchet).
- `services/photo-render-watch.ts:85` — `timer: 0 as unknown as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>`; make the field optional instead of faking a handle.

## F0 · 2026-09-16 (mechanical — all five are **leads**, confirmed by the owning session)

F0 makes no judgement calls by design, so nothing here is a confirmed defect: each row is a mechanical observation with the session that owns the surface. None is 🔴 or 🟠 — the tree is gate-green and has no orphan files or orphan stylesheets at all.

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Owner | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F0-1 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | 8 files | 14 exports nothing references anywhere in the repo (detail below) | per file | 🟨 partial (RA4) |
| FE-F0-2 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `components/react/GridComponent.tsx:277` | the tree's only `eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax` without a reason comment — every other one of the 22 explains itself | F12 | ⬜ open |
| FE-F0-3 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `components/react/LookupEditorModal.tsx:109` | `useQueries` without `combine` → new array identity every render → the `referenceOptions` memo (line 115) recomputes every render. **Not the freeze class** (`[[usequeries-combine-referential-stability]]`): the memo is read only in render at line 294, no effect depends on it — so this is wasted work, not a loop. Add `combine` anyway | F21 | ⬜ open |
| FE-F0-4 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `pages/aligner/PatientSets.tsx:2275,2299` | the only 4 lint warnings in the whole tree (a11y: click handler on a non-interactive element, no keyboard listener) | F17 / F25 | ⬜ open |
| FE-F0-5 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `public/index.html` | the staff SPA's inline bootstrap is a **second client layer** beside `core/http.ts`, and F1/F23/F26 each own a piece of it (detail below) | F1 / F23 / F26 | ⬜ open |

**FE-F0-1 — the 14 confirmed-dead exports** (verified with a repo-wide grep including `shared/`, server code and the entry HTMLs; each appears exactly once, at its own declaration):

|---|---|---|

| `utils/formatters.ts` | `formatMoneyInput` (57) | F1 |
| `utils/phoneFormatter.ts` | `cleanPhone` (39) | F1 |
| `portal/portal.schemas.ts` | `PortalPatientData` (22) | F23 |

The two unused **query factories** and the two unused **loaders** are the interesting pair: F2 should establish whether a route lost its loader (a prefetch regression — the screen now flashes empty) or the loader was deliberately replaced by a component-level `useQuery`. **Answered by F2 (FE-F2-10): no route lost a loader.**

**FE-F0-5 — what is actually in `public/index.html`:**
- **A global `window.fetch` monkey-patch** (line ~231) that redirects to `/login.html` on **any** 401 and rejects with `new Error('Authentication required')`. It is **load-bearing, not dead**: `core/http.ts` has no 401 handling of its own. F1 owns the consequences — every 401 becomes a hard navigation, so a component `catch` can never show the server's message for one, and the rejection reaching the funnel is a generic `Error` rather than the funnel's own shape.
- **11 unguarded `console.*`** including a `[Auth] Global fetch interceptor installed` line on every load (`public/js` itself is clean — the CLAUDE.md "Winston, not console.log" rule stops at the bundle).
- **`window.addEventListener('error'` / `'unhandledrejection'`** that only `console.error` — additive with `core/error-reporter.ts`'s own listeners, so both run. Not a conflict; F23 decides whether the inline pair earns its place.
- **The React mount waits for `window.addEventListener('load')`** — i.e. for every image and stylesheet, not just DOM ready. F26 measures what that costs on a cold load.
- `window.performance.timing` (deprecated Navigation Timing Level 1) in the same block.

---

# Read and found clean (do not re-derive)

Per session, the files read closely that produced nothing. This list is what keeps later sessions cheap — fill it in even when it feels like filler.

## F11 · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F11 ledger (3,374 LOC + eleven CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's five questions, answered.** *Event storm:* one day-query per SSE frame per open board, with at most one in flight per client (FE-F11-12). *"Live" after a laptop sleep:* not trustworthy (FE-F11-7), and after a proxied restart the board never reconnects at all (FE-F11-3). *`getCurrentStatus()`:* not filed as i18n, as the plan says; it is filed as **dead** (FE-F11-9), because it reads fields the payload never has. *Chair CLOSED-stream self-reload:* correct in code (delayed by `CLOSED_STREAM_RELOAD_DELAY_MS`, latched, cleared in cleanup, gated by `cancelled`); its single-shot limit is FE-F11-15(d). *Kiosk pinned light + LTR:* verified at runtime (`data-theme="light"`, `dir="ltr"`, `lang="en"`, no app header), and the unmount restores the stored theme and language. *Videos:* the first request is `Range: bytes=0-` → 206, `streamFile` handles suffix, multi and unsatisfiable ranges and destroys the read stream on abort; the global 30 s timeout leaves a streaming response alone (`headersSent`). Memory is FE-F11-13.
- **The check-in state machine is sound on the server.** `updatePresent` takes `FOR UPDATE` inside a transaction, the forward guards match the card's enabled buttons one for one (undo check-in disabled once seated, undo seat disabled once dismissed, dismiss disabled until seated), and a stale click comes back as 400 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION`, which the client recovers from silently through the shared `isInvalidStateTransition`. Every state write broadcasts the appointment's **own** day. A double click cannot double-submit: the list is `pointer-events: none` from the click until the refetch lands.
- **Role gates.** The daily read, both state endpoints and quick check-in are admin / front_desk / clinical, and the client offers them to all three: correct. `/api/tv-display` is `USE [authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical)]`, matching `SettingsComponent`'s "deliberately available to every staff role" comment. Video writes are `CLINICAL_ROLES`; reads are open to any signed-in user; the share page `/v/:id` is public by design (educational content).
- **The doctor filter scales.** It is a client-side filter over one day's payload (≤ ~400 rows at the CLAUDE.md ceiling). The dropdown and the card badges share `useAppointmentDoctors` with the calendar, and each colour is the employee's own `appointment_color`, so adding a doctor never recolours another. `DoctorFilterSelect` portals to `<body>`, clamps to the viewport and removes its three listeners on close. The a11y gaps are FE-F11-11(e).
- **Stats.** The chips are derived from the two **filtered** lists, so they follow the doctor and search filters and equal the server's whole-day numbers when unfiltered. The count-up animation is StrictMode-safe: it resumes from `displayed` rather than guarding on previous props, and clears its intervals in cleanup.
- **The kiosk's visit summary is safe HTML.** Everything is escaped, then only attribute-less `<br>`, `<font color=blue>` and `</font>` are re-enabled, and `buildVisitSummary` emits exactly those. A user who types them gets a line break or a blue span, nothing more. The chair server channel checks `chairId` against `1–10` on both sides, guards stale loads with `chairEpoch`, expires the replay after 12 h and sits behind the auth gate. Runtime: the three intraoral images loaded through the session cookie.
- **The TV tab's settings draft is correct.** The render-phase seed is keyed on the serialized server payload (the per-image map sorted, so key order never reads as dirty), a remote change re-seeds only when nothing is edited locally, *Discard* moves the seed with the draft, and the 20 s status refetch never clobbers an edit. A failed upload leaves nothing behind (runtime: the staging folder was empty after the timed-out upload). The playlist half is FE-F11-14.
- **Videos.** The upload carries 120 s on both sides (FE-F1-2's fix holds). The thumbnail has a fallback image and a keyboard path (`role="button"`, Enter/Space), the print window escapes the title and URL, and every write invalidates `qk.videos.all()`, matching F2's map.
- **SSE transport hygiene on the server** matches CLAUDE.md: `setTimeout(0)` on both halves, the four headers, a 2.5–3.5 s jittered `retry:`, one shared 25 s keep-alive interval (`unref()`'d), and teardown ends every stream and clears the chair maps.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp** (F10's hand-down) is right for the board: a walk-in's time is the minute it arrived, and it sorts correctly. Nothing more to file.
- **CSS modules:** 0 dead classes across the eleven modules (the scan's two hits in `Videos.module.css` are the `w3.org` inside a data URI) and 0 `!important`. The physical `left`/`right` properties are flipped by postcss-rtlcss. Literal colours are limited to box-shadow `rgba()`s and the kiosk's own palette (22 in `ChairDisplay.module.css`), which is deliberately outside the theme because the kiosk is pinned light.
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** FE-F3-9: decided (FE-F11-8a). FE-F3-8: the card's reader list is under the F11 table. FE-F1-7: pair the channel extraction with FE-F11-3 and FE-F11-7 in RC, since both fixes belong in it. FE-F2-8 (the `qk.appointments` namespace fiction): unchanged; RC can fold it into FE-F11-10/-17. FE-F4-10 (the chair beacon order): unchanged, still a server change.
- **Gate baseline re-measured (2026-09-29):** `npm run gate` green. ESLint shows 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (still at 2285/2309); vitest 34 files / 279 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 8 (at baseline); both typechecks clean; build green. F11 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F11-1, afternoon stamps shown as AM (last 30 days): `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE present >= '13:00') AS pm, count(*) AS total FROM appointments WHERE present IS NOT NULL AND app_day >= CURRENT_DATE - 30;`
  - FE-F11-4, check-ins on days that have not happened yet (certainly wrong): `SELECT appointment_id, app_day, present FROM appointments WHERE app_day > CURRENT_DATE AND (present IS NOT NULL OR seated IS NOT NULL OR dismissed IS NOT NULL);`
  - FE-F11-16: `SELECT option_value FROM options WHERE option_name = 'VideosPath';` (set on this clinic; the point is every new install). FE-F11-6: whether the service's environment sets `PUBLIC_URL`.

## F10 · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F10 ledger (4,490 LOC + five CSS modules + `appointment-calendar.css`). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The clinic week is right everywhere on this surface.** The grid pages by `addWorkingDays` and anchors on `getWeekStartSaturday`; the month grid has six Sat–Thu columns and the server skips `getDay() === 5`; the picker omits the Friday cell and offsets the 1st correctly when it falls on a Friday; the server's `getCalendarGridEnd` never lands on a Friday. FE-F3-1's weekend fix holds, and no calendar file re-derives a weekend.
- **Wall-clock discipline holds on the write path.** Every appointment write sends a local `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS` built from date/time strings (form, edit form, drag), never `toISOString()`. The server casts it `::timestamp`. `APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT` returns `to_char(… 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS')`, so the list and the edit form parse a local time. The picker's `formatLocalDate` and the forms' date extraction use local getters. The only UTC parses are the date-only ones in FE-F10-16. The `toISOString()` in `/available-slots`' `dateTime` field is never read by the client.
- **The translated booking workflow is in the ratchet and renders correctly.** The four files are in `eslint.config.js`'s `i18next/no-literal-string` list; `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` and the `index.html` FOUC regexes match `appointments`, `new-appointment` and `edit-appointment(/.*)?` exactly. `formatAppointmentDateTime`, `formatAppointmentListDateTime`, `formatScheduleDate`, `formatMonthName` and `calendarWeekdayHeaders` all take `language` as a parameter. The Arabic weekday headers are the short forms without "ال", in Sat–Thu order, and digits stay Western. The calendar screen itself (`/calendar`) is untranslated and LTR, which is correct.
- **FE-F2-1's fix holds.** Create, edit and delete from the patient screens invalidate `qk.calendar.all()` as well as `qk.patient.all()`; `qk.calendar.all()` is a true prefix of every calendar leaf (range, month, stats, availability, slots). The gap is the reverse direction (FE-F10-10).
- **Server-side rules match what the forms assume.** Create and update share `createAppointmentBody` (enumerated, strict) and run the same holiday / doctor / one-per-patient-per-day checks. The PUT and DELETE broadcast both affected days to the Daily Appointments SSE, so that board is not stale after a calendar write. A second DELETE of the same id returns 200, so the calendar's missing in-flight guard (FE-F10-20) cannot surface an error. The create form maps the two conflict codes onto translated messages (the edit form's gap is FE-F10-17).
- **Double-submit is guarded** on both forms (`loading` disables Cancel and Create/Update) and in the holiday modal (`saving`).
- **Doctor colours are stable** when doctors are added or leave: a colour is the employee's own `appointment_color`, not a position in a list, so adding, reordering or deactivating one doctor never recolours another. `useAppointmentDoctors` builds the legend and the tints from one memoized read, so they cannot disagree. The literal defaults are FE-F10-11. At a 20-doctor center, doctors without a picked colour render neutral. That is a design choice, not a defect.
- **Per-doctor filtering scales.** The filter goes into the query key and into the SQL (`ta.dr_id = $doctorId` inside the JOIN), not into a client-side filter over the whole payload. `hideEmptySlots` then collapses empty rows. A 30-day window at the CLAUDE.md ceiling (~400/day) is ~12k appointments in one `/range` payload; the zoom maximum (`N_MAX = 30`) is the thing to revisit if a center ever reaches it, and F26 can measure it.
- **Holiday handling on writes is right.** The server refuses a booking or an edit into a holiday (`HOLIDAY_CONFLICT`, worded on create). The picker makes holiday days non-clickable. The calendar's drop target refuses a holiday column (`dropEffect: 'none'`, no `onDrop`). `fillCalendar` skips holiday dates. The visibility of appointments already on a holiday is FE-F10-3.
- **Cleanup and listeners.** The "+N more" popover, both context menus and the month panel each remove the document listeners they add. The menus arm their outside-click listener on the next frame and cancel that frame in cleanup. `HolidayQuickModal` clears its focus timer. The forms cancel their Web Animations flash on unmount. `AnalogClock` pauses its 1 s tick on a hidden tab and re-syncs on return. The zoom debounce timer is cleared per change.
- **Render-phase seeds are keyed correctly.** `HolidayQuickModal` (open + holiday id, reset while closed), `EditAppointmentForm` (appointment identity), the picker's three option seeds and its auto-expand key each re-run exactly when their input changes, and none writes state it reads in the same guard. The seed *source* is FE-F10-1, not the seeding.
- **CSS modules: only `.dayTooltip` is dead** (FE-F10-19). 0 `!important` in the five modules (`AppointmentForm.module.css`'s only hit is a comment explaining why it avoids one). `appointment-calendar.css`'s dead and unstyled classes are in FE-F10-19; its dynamic classes (`count-*`, `tint-zebra`, `cal-legend-swatch neutral`) are live through template strings.
- **Gate baseline re-measured in this environment (2026-09-29):** `npm run gate` green. 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (now at 2285/2309 — the same two elements, moved by later edits); 34 test files / 279 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 **8** (the script's baseline is now 8, one under CLAUDE.md's "baseline 9"); both typechecks clean; build green. F10 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F10-2, the slot table's reach: `SELECT min(app_date), max(app_date), count(*) FROM calendar;`. Appointments the grid can never show inside that window: `SELECT count(*) FROM appointments a WHERE a.app_date >= (SELECT min(app_date) FROM calendar) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM calendar c WHERE c.app_date = a.app_date);`. And the hidden rows: `SELECT option_name, option_value FROM options WHERE option_name LIKE 'CALENDAR_%';`
  - FE-F10-3: `SELECT count(*) FROM appointments a JOIN holidays h ON a.app_date::date = h.holiday_date WHERE a.app_date >= CURRENT_DATE;`
  - FE-F10-7, slots already over the limit: `SELECT app_date, count(*) FROM appointments GROUP BY app_date HAVING count(*) > COALESCE((SELECT option_value::int FROM options WHERE option_name = 'MaxAppointmentsPerSlot'), 3) ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 20;`

## F9 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F9 ledger (2,928 LOC + eight CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The tooth-numbering answer the plan asked for.** `DentalChart` and `TeethSelector` use one notation: quadrant + `1–8` for permanent teeth, `A–E` for primary teeth. They share the 32 chart SVGs, which are named by that code, so `tooth_numbers.tooth_code` **must** keep those exact strings or the selector's images break. `DentalChart` emits `UR6` / `Between UR1 and UL1` as free text into the notes. `TeethSelector` stores `tooth_numbers.id`s and sorts right quadrants 8→1 and left 1→8 by `tooth_number`, which is correct Palmer display order. `Diagnosis` has no tooth mapping at all (free text). The only divergence is the 3Shape tab (FE-F9-6) and the Diagnosis placeholder. `tooth_numbers` is not seeded by any migration; that is the documented clinical-vocabulary data load, not a frontend defect.
- **Role gates match the server.** Every visit endpoint, both diagnosis writes and every WebCeph endpoint is `authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical)` = `CLINICAL_ROLES` = all three roles. The WorkCard buttons (New Visit / Visits / Diagnosis) are ungated, and that is correct. `GET /api/diagnosis/:workId` carries only `validate(params)` in its own chain, and is authenticated by the `USE /api [authenticate]` layer above it. `/clinic-assets` sits behind `USE / [authenticateWeb]`.
- **Double-submit is guarded.** `NewVisitComponent`'s `loading` disables both submit buttons, and implicit Enter-submission is suppressed while the default button is disabled (F7's rule). Diagnosis Save and Reset share `saving || deleting`. The diagnosis upsert is idempotent on `UNIQUE (work_id)`, so even the timer window in FE-F9-7 cannot create a second row. The WebCeph create is guarded server-side: a local-row check before the external call, and "already in WebCeph" refused. Both upload paths resolve the WebCeph id from the DB and never from the client.
- **Dates are handled tz-safely where they matter.** `formatISODate`/`formatDate` pass bare `YYYY-MM-DD` through without `new Date()`. The diagnosis GET returns `to_char(dx_date,'YYYY-MM-DD')`, and the POST builds local midnight (`parseLocalDate`). `visit_date` is passed through as a string (`toDateOnly`). The Visit History sort's `new Date(visit_date)` parses both sides as UTC midnight, so the comparison is consistent. `visit_date` is `NOT NULL`, so no `NaN` sort. The only browser-locale formats (WebCeph `createdAt`, X-ray dates) are FE-F3-3's census.
- **Visit contract round-trip.** The form's `''` for "no wire/operator" is coerced by `optionalCoercedInt` (`'' → undefined`) and stored as NULL. The five flags are `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, and the form always sends booleans. The edit seed maps every NULL text column to `''`, so the `z.string().optional()` fields never receive `null`. The latest-wires quick-pick sets numeric ids that the `<select>` shows as selected.
- **Invalidation that is right.** Visit add, update and delete all refresh `qk.work.all(workId)`, which covers `visits`, `latestWires` and the shell's `details`. Diagnosis save and delete invalidate `qk.work.diagnosis` while the page is still mounted, so the refetch lands before the navigation. The WebCeph create writes the new link with `setQueryData` (no refetch flash). The gaps are FE-F9-2 (`visit.byId`) and FE-F9-4 (`patient.all`).
- **3Shape client assumptions, recorded for when it is first connected.** The client reads only the normalized contract (`cases[].{id, workflowStatus, creationDate, indications[].{from,to,type,material}}` and `media[].{id, mediaType, captureDate, uniteCloudLink, files[]}`). The server's `dtos.ts` matches the PDF's camelCase and parses leniently (`safeParse` + skip). Both reads are `retry: false`, deliberately, with a documented reason. The thumbnail and download proxies take the 3Shape id verbatim in the path (the service says it arrives already percent-encoded; the one thing to check on the first live response). `uniteCloudLink` opens with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`. The not-connected / unreachable error text comes from the server via `httpErrorMessage`, which is the "is the workstation on?" path CLAUDE.md asks for.
- **Legacy `tshape:` leftovers: none on the client.** The only trace in `public/js` is `Navigation.tsx:112`'s comment, which accurately records the replacement. The protocol handler itself (`protocol-handlers/`, the `TShapePath` INI key) is **intentionally kept** as a fallback per `3shape-integration.md`. Not dead.
- **CSS modules: nothing to file.** Eight modules, 2,722 lines: 0 `!important`, 0 dead classes (every class in each module has a `styles.x` reader in its component), and one literal colour: `NewVisitComponent.module.css:880`, a `-webkit-tap-highlight-color` rgba (cosmetic, not a themeable surface). The X-ray placeholder's cascade loss is FE-F9-5 and is not re-filed.
- **i18n posture is as designed.** Diagnosis, the visit screens, X-rays and 3D Scans are doctor-facing English, on LTR routes (none are in `RTL_ROUTES`/`RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`), and are not findings. Only `WebCephModal` renders inside a translated RTL route (FE-F9-9).
- **Gate baseline re-measured in this environment (2026-09-28):** `npm run gate` green. 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings; 26 test files / 229 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9 at baseline; both typechecks clean; build green. F9 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F9-1's blast radius: `SELECT count(*) AS diagnoses, count(*) FILTER (WHERE coalesce(c_sna,c_snb,c_anb,f_vertical,o_overjet,i_upper_crowding) IS NOT NULL) AS with_workup FROM diagnoses;`
  - FE-F9-2's frequency: `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE updated_at IS NOT NULL) AS edited, count(*) AS visits FROM visits;`
  - FE-F9-4's reach: `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE f_photo) AS final_photo_visits, count(*) FILTER (WHERE i_photo AND f_photo) AS both_same_visit FROM visits;` and `SELECT count(*) FROM works w WHERE w.status = 2 AND w.f_photo_date IS NOT NULL;`
  - FE-F9-9: `SELECT count(*) FROM patients WHERE web_ceph_patient_id IS NOT NULL;`

## F8 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F8 ledger (3,633 LOC + three CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **FE-F7-5's hand-down is answered: no UI path can put EUR on a ledger.** `PaymentModal` takes its account currency from the work, which is `USD`/`IQD` only since RB1 (enum on the three work bodies and on `addWorkWithInvoice`; `insertFullPaymentInvoice` throws on anything else). Its payment-currency select is USD / IQD / MIXED, and the invoice body carries only `usdReceived`/`iqdReceived`. Aligner-set payments are USD-only by contract (FE-F8-6 is about the *work's* currency, not EUR). The expense form offers IQD / USD only. One server-side looseness remains: `expense.contract.ts` types `currency` as `z.string().optional()`, so only a non-UI caller could write another code (live: IQD 4,331 · USD 19, nothing else). Worth the same one-line `z.enum` RB1 gave the work bodies when FE-F8-1's slice runs. It is not filed, because no UI can produce it.
- **Currency separation holds on every F8 screen.** The payment modal converts only the foreign leg, and change is always IQD. The expense summary, the expense table and both statistics drill-downs keep IQD and USD in separate figures and never add them. `DailyInvoicesModal`'s cash totals sum `iqd_received`/`usd_received` (cash actually taken), not `amount_paid` (the ledger currency), which is right for a cash count.
- **The payment math matches the server's rules.** The client's overpayment check is the server's rule: remaining = `total_required − discount − TotalPaid`, with the discount subtracted (unlike FE-F7-6's history box). The server re-checks under a row lock (`addInvoiceWithBalanceGuard`), so the client check is a fast fail, not the guarantee. `change` is submitted exactly when `isChangeDisabled` says it is tracked, and the receipt shows what was recorded (the RA-era fix holds). A negative amount typed into the modal reaches the server and 400s through `moneyInt`, and the toast shows the server's text through `httpErrorMessage`. The rounding directions are deliberate and consistent: what the patient owes rounds **up**, what they hand over rounds **down**, and change rounds down to 1,000 IQD. Registering a whole-thousand IQD amount therefore can never come out short.
- **The set-state-in-effect cascade has not regressed.** `PaymentModal` has **0** `useEffect`s. The six keyed render-phase blocks each write only state outside their own key and converge (runtime: typing, mode switches and the rate editor all settle, and ESLint is clean on every F8 file). The rate editor closes when the payment date changes, so it can never write to a date other than the one it opened for, and it refuses to overwrite a day's own rate. `unsavedGuard` + `resetKey` are wired as RA5 recorded.
- **The rate path is the right one.** The modal reads `exchangeRateForDate`, which carries the last rate forward and says so in the banner ("carried forward from …"). The server validates change against `getExchangeRateAsOf(paymentDate)`, the same as-of semantics. A rate save invalidates `qk.exchangeRates.all()`, which prefixes the modal's `forDate` key and the Settings history.
- **Cache after a payment.** Save invalidates `qk.work.all(workId)`, which covers the payment-history list and the work header. Closing the modal (after success or not) invalidates `qk.patient.all`, which covers the works list with its Paid/Remaining. `dailyInvoices` and `reports.*` are keyed by date/period and not invalidated, as F2 recorded. The only gap is the aligner path (FE-F8-7).
- **Approval tiers say so.** A front-desk edit or delete of an expense created before today returns `outcome: 'pending'`. The page then toasts it (in English, FE-F8-8), calls `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2's fix holds at `useExpenses:230,255`) and leaves the row untouched instead of looking applied. `addInvoice` is not a hold-tier write, and an invoice **delete** is F7's (`WorkComponent`).
- **Role gates match the server.** `addInvoice`, `updateExchangeRateForDate` and `POST /api/aligner/payments` are `admin|front_desk`, and the modal is only reachable through the card's `writeFinance`-gated button (RB1). The aligner drawer's button is `caps.writeFinance`. Expense reads are any-authenticated, and expense writes are `admin|front_desk` (+ `requireRecordAge`): the page hides Add / Edit / Delete behind `caps.writeFinance` and shows the table read-only to clinical. The statistics drill-downs are `FINANCE_ROLES` / `ADMIN_ROLES` and are only opened from views gated the same way (F5).
- **The expense form basics.** The amount is digits-only (`[^\d]` stripped), so it is integer and non-negative like the contract's `moneyInt`. Only the sub-level matching the chosen category is sent (subcategory, lab or employee). The category change resets all three. Submit is guarded by `submitting`. The Lab dropdown's right-click editor invalidates `qk.lookups.labs()`, which is exactly `labsQuery`'s key, so the live dropdown refreshes (checked by code). The hardcoded category ids 5 (Employees) and 7 (Lab) are seed ids, documented as fixed across deployments in `config/expenseCategories.ts`, the same posture as the backend constant.
- **`PaymentFormDrawer`.** Digits-only amount, the balance check mirrors the server's set-balance rule (the authoritative check runs locked in `createAlignerPayment`), dismiss is blocked while saving, and the server's message reaches the drawer (`PatientSets` rethrows it via `httpErrorMessage`). The `Balance || set_cost` fallback would misread a zero balance, but the only opener requires `Balance > 0`, so it is unreachable. Its missing `unsavedGuard` is RA5's documented deferral, not re-filed. Its English strings are fine: `PatientSets` is untranslated (staged rollout).
- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for both F8 translations.** The six expense files and `PaymentModal` are in `eslint.config.js`'s list. `/expenses` is in `RTL_ROUTES` and in `index.html`'s FOUC list. `PaymentModal` inherits the Works route's RTL. `payments.json` (73 keys) and `expenses.json` (86) have EN/AR parity, and **every key is used**. Money renders with Western digits everywhere (`toLocaleString('en-US')` / `Intl.NumberFormat('en-US')`). The browser-locale dates in `ExpenseTable`, `DeleteConfirmModal` and `DailyInvoicesModal` are already counted in F3's census (FE-F3-3), and `useExpenses`' shared `loading` flag is FE-F3-12. Neither is re-filed.

The literal `rgba(255,255,255,.95)` field backgrounds on the expense filter card are deliberate and annotated: theme-independent fields on a fixed gradient card, with a stylelint disable and a note on why a flipping token turned them white-on-white.
