## F7 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F7 ledger (3,425 LOC + six CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for the Works page.** `WorkComponent` and `WorkCard` are in `eslint.config.js`'s list. `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` has exactly `/^\/patient\/[^/]+\/works\/?$/`, and `index.html`'s FOUC check carries the same regex. `works.json` has 135 keys with EN/AR parity, every literal `t('…')` key exists, and the four `filter.*` keys are live (read through `labelKey`). `WorkDetailsPanel`/`WorkDetailItem` are English by design (doctor-facing, CLAUDE.md). `NewWorkComponent` and `TransferWorkModal` are English and off the list: staged rollout. The only i18n findings are FE-F7-11a and FE-F7-12.
- **Double-submit is guarded on every create/update path.** `loading` disables both submit buttons and is set synchronously inside the submit handler. Implicit Enter-submission is suppressed while the form's default button is disabled. Both confirm dialogs' buttons share `loading`. The "Mark as fully paid" path cannot fall into the duplicate-active dialog, so it can never be re-sent through `/api/addwork` without its invoice: `unq_tblwork_active` is partial on `status = 1`, and `addWorkWithInvoice` inserts status 2.
- **Work-creation invalidation is complete.** `qk.patient.all(personId)` covers works, `info` (the works-derived patient type, recomputed server-side in the create transaction) and `hasAppointment`. The fully-paid invoice arrives as the works row's `TotalPaid`. Update invalidates `patient.all` + `work.all(workId)`. Finish-existing-then-add invalidates before and after the add, correctly. The gap is the lifecycle writes only (FE-F7-8).
- **Item writes refresh exactly what they change.** `qk.work.detailsList(workId)` is the key the panel reads, and it is awaited before edit mode closes. The item routes touch no `works` row, so no works-list invalidation is owed. Lab-case writes go through `useLabCases` (F3's reference implementation). The draft is rebuilt from the row on every `enterEdit`, so a refetch while reading never leaks into the next edit. Item cost is digits-only and integer.
- **Shade pairing is correct client-side.** Changing the system clears the shade. The shade select is disabled until a system is chosen and lists only that system's values. The stored `shade_system` is the hardcoded label from `shade-queries.ts#getShades`, so the label is a stored key and renaming it orphans rows. That is the documented design (`[[shade-selection-feature]]`), and 1 live row uses it. The contract does not check the pair server-side; the client is the only pairing guard. The lab dropdown keeps a deactivated lab selectable by its joined name, which is the pattern FE-F7-1 needs.
- **Transfer semantics are right on the server.** Only `works.person_id` moves, and everything keyed by `work_id` goes with it: invoices, visits, items, diagnoses, aligner sets/batches, implants, screws. Time points, appointments and patient files are per-person and stay, which is correct. Both patients' types are recomputed in the same transaction. Same patient → 400; an active-work conflict at the target → 409. It is admin-only on both sides (`isAdmin` / `authorize(admin)` on preview and transfer), `PatientQuickSearch` excludes the source, and the confirm is two-step with a "cannot be undone" warning. The preview is keyed on the work id only, which is correct because it does not depend on the target. The step advance is a render-phase transition guard, and Back clears the selection.
- **Discount editing matches the server.** The client hint is the server's `validateDiscount` rule (discount ≤ total − paid), and the server validates before the role gate, so an over-limit front-desk discount 400s instead of queuing. Non-admin changes go to the approval queue with `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2 holds at `NewWorkComponent:317`, `WorkComponent:324,754`). All 4 live discounted works carry a `discount_date`, so the "default to today" branch cannot turn an unrelated edit into a phantom discount-date change.
- **Work delete** reads the 409's `details.dependencies` in the server's `WorkDependencies` shape and handles the hold-tier `pending` outcome. Its dialog is `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader variant="danger">` with a matching `titleId`/`ariaLabelledBy`. The status-change and duplicate-work dialogs are wired the same way. `NewWorkComponent`'s 409 reads both server conflict shapes: `{workId,typeName,…}` for a duplicate, `{work_id,type,doctor}` for a status change.
- **CSS modules: nothing to file.** Six modules, 2,138 lines: 0 literal colours, 0 `!important`, 0 dead classes (the three "unused" `btn*` names are a comment and a `:global(.btn)`). Physical properties are left for postcss-rtlcss to flip, as designed. The unflipped chevron glyph is FE-F7-12.
- **`WorkComponent` is not keyed by `personId`, and it doesn't need to be.** Its per-patient state (`checkedIn`, `patientPhotoError`, `expandedWorks`, search) would carry over on a direct `/patient/A/works` → `/patient/B/works` navigation. No in-app path does that: nothing inside the shell links patient to patient, and the sticky tab only ever points at the current patient. Latent only; recorded so nobody files it.
- **Auto-expand** uses the repo's render-phase keyed guard and is idempotent. Only one work per patient can be active (the unique index), so "first active work" means "the active work". The re-seed trigger is FE-F7-14's.
- **Live-data facts recorded so nobody re-queries them** (2026-09-28). `works.currency`: IQD 6,180 · USD 1,606 · 0 NULL · 0 EUR; 2 USD works above 10,000. 35 IQD works have totals under 10,000, all legacy (2018–2025, `updated_at` NULL, so not FE-F7-3's fingerprint). Five of them are still **active** and look like mislabelled USD amounts: 592 (Ortho, 1,500, 2018), 10029 (Filling, 50, paid 50), 10192 / 10193 (Aligner Lab, 570 / 690), 10768 (Ortho Aligners, 525). These were handed to the owner to check on 2026-09-28 — a data check, not a frontend finding. `estimated_duration`: 552 × 0, 21 × >0. `discount`: 7,746 NULL, 36 × 0 (FE-F7-4b), 4 × >0. `work_items`: 1,139 rows, `item_cost` all NULL, 1 implant row, 1 shade row. `lab_cases`: 0. Works by doctor: Shwan Elias 5,933 · Snowbar (Doctor, quit, no commission) 1,208 (104 active) · Rojena 347 · Clinic (Doctor, pseudo) 211 · Haliz (**Assistant**, commission) 86 · Yassen (Receptionist) 1. FK delete rules: `invoices`, `visits`, `diagnoses`, `implants`, `aligner_sets` → `works` are CASCADE, while `work_items` and `screws` are NO ACTION. `deleteWork` counts dependencies before deleting, so none of the cascades can fire from the UI.

## F6 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F6 ledger (~3.4k LOC, plus `PhoneInput`, `phoneFormatter` and the two loaders). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for all three translated screens.** `ViewPatientInfo`, `EditPatientComponent` and `AddPatientForm` are in `eslint.config.js`'s `no-literal-string` list. `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` matches exactly `/patient/:id/{patient-info,edit-patient,add}`, not the subtree, and `index.html`'s FOUC check carries the same three regexes. Phone and email values carry `dir="ltr"`, and `PhoneInput` pins its own. The lookup-row values staying untranslated is the documented staged state (CLAUDE.md), not a finding.
- **The works-derived patient type is displayed as the server stores it, and refreshed when it changes.** `ViewPatientInfo` shows the materialized `patient_type_name`. Edit shows it read-only and `disabled`, and `updatePatient.body` strips any `patient_type_id` a client sends. Add has no picker, so the intake selector alone seeds the first work. Every works write invalidates `qk.patient.all`, which covers both `info` and `full`. The client never re-derives the type.
- **Intake validation matches `intakeSchema` exactly.** X-ray fee > 0, consult ≥ 0, and the fee input admits digits only, so it is integer and non-negative like `moneyInt`. The x-ray type is limited to OPG/CBCT/Cephalo on both sides. The currency resets to IQD, the currency the presets are quoted in. The 409 `DUPLICATE_PATIENT_NAME` path reads `details.code`. The 422 `IntakeConfigError` surfaces the server's message. On success the button stays disabled through the redirect, so a second click cannot create a duplicate.
- **Search mechanics in `PatientManagement` are sound apart from FE-F6-3/-13.** The debounced auto-search calls the latest `executeSearch` through a ref, so a completed search can't echo a second one. Each search aborts the previous one. Reset kills the debounce and the in-flight request. A sort change refetches from offset 0. Load More pages with the current sort and offset. `hasMore`/`totalCount` come from the server. RA4's FE-F2-6 fix holds: the five dropdowns read the RQ cache, which the loader filled through `ensureQueryData` + `emptyOnHttpError`. FE-F4-4's shared matcher gets `nameStartsWith`.
- **Row actions are guarded.** While `loading`, `.tableLoadingOverlay` sets `pointer-events: none`, so a double-clicked check-in cannot fire twice. Check-in invalidates `qk.patient.all` + `qk.calendar.all` (RA1's FE-F2-1 fix holds). Both delete flows (list and Edit) guard with `deleting`, send the hold-tier `pending` outcome to `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2 holds), and warn on `folderRemoved === false`. Both delete dialogs are `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader variant="danger">` with a matching `titleId`/`ariaLabelledBy`.
- **Edit form basics.** The TDZ fix holds: `patientTypeLabel` is declared below `formData` (`[[closure-tdz-not-caught-by-tsc]]`). `formatISODate` passes a `'YYYY-MM-DD'` DOB through untouched, with no UTC round-trip. Estimated cost is digits-only with display commas and goes out as a string that the server's `toInt` parses. Translate-name is bounded and never silently blanks the names. Save/Cancel return to `state.from`, falling back to works.
- **`ViewPatientInfo` writes.** Estimated cost: `moneyInt` coerces `''` to `0`, which clears the value (shown as `-`). `currency` is `.min(1)`. The save invalidates `qk.patient.all`. Presets are filtered to the selected currency. Alert dismiss refreshes `qk.patient.alerts` and calls `notifyTasksChanged()`. `AlertModal` edits keep `surface_mode` through `showInHeader`, so editing a push task here does not demote it to a context alert. The WebCeph open state lives in `?webceph=1`, set through a functional updater that preserves the other params. Money and phone display use Western digits (`toLocaleString('en-US')`, `formatPhoneForDisplay`), and the phone display tolerates a stored `+` in `country_code`.
- **`PatientTypesReadOnly`**: read-only by design and excluded from `LOOKUP_TABLE_CONFIG` so the classifier's rows cannot be edited. It has loading, error and empty states. Nothing to report.
- **`PatientFolderPicker`**: lists folders and images only, falls back to an icon per `relPath` when a thumbnail fails, uses the file-explorer query and helpers, and has a retry. Its one cast is in FE-F6-10.
- **Server gates for everything these screens call** were checked against `route-table.txt`. `/api/patients/search`, `phones`, `type-options`, `:id`, `:id/info` and `:id/alerts` GET are open to any authenticated user. Create and `transliterate-name` are all three roles. Alert add/edit/status/snooze and quick check-in are all three roles, so the client's all-roles rendering of those is correct. The only mismatch is FE-F6-6.
- **Live-data facts recorded so nobody re-queries them**, from `patients` on 2026-09-28: `phone` is 10 digits (no leading 0) in 5,472 of ~5,490 non-empty rows; `country_code` is `964` × 6,824 or NULL × 20, with no `+`; `language` is 0 × 6,840, 1 × 3, 2 × 1; 4 `patient_name`s are untrimmed. The unique index is `ix_name_id (patient_name) INCLUDE (person_id)`, on `citext`.

## F5 · 2026-09-27

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F5 ledger (~3.9k LOC, incl. `PeriodNavigator` and F8's `DoctorPaymentsModal`). Everything below was checked and needs no further work — **do not re-derive it.**

- **Client role gates match the server's, endpoint for endpoint** (checked against `route-table.txt`). Approvals list / history / approve / reject / acknowledge / approve-all / acknowledge-all are `authorize(admin)` and only `ApprovalsBell` (admin-only render) calls them; `/api/approvals/mine` is any-authenticated and backs the front-desk `MyApprovalsBadge`; `/api/tasks*` + `/api/alerts/*` are `CLINICAL_ROLES` = all three roles, as `TasksBell`'s all-roles render assumes; `/api/portal-activity` reads are any-authenticated and its two PATCHes all three roles. Statistics: `/api/statistics` is `FINANCE_ROLES`; yearly / multi-year / commissions / revenue-breakdown / doctor-payments are `ADMIN_ROLES`, which is exactly `isAdminOnlyView()` (Monthly → yearly endpoint, Yearly → multi-year). No F5 request can 403 on a normal path: every query is `enabled` on `effectiveViewMode`, and a clinical user gets the lock card with the queries disabled.
- **Polling is three endpoints per admin/front-desk session, two for clinical — not four on one timer.** Tasks, portal activity and approvals (or `mine`) each run their own `refetchInterval: 5 min`; RQ's `refetchIntervalInBackground` defaults to false, so all of them **pause in a hidden tab**. The only waste is the unconditional visibility refetch (FE-F5-13).
- **Badge counts agree with the lists they open.** `TasksBell`'s badge and list are the same array, and the read-time visibility rules (active · not snoozed past `CURRENT_DATE` · not expired · push, or context with `escalate_at <= CURRENT_DATE`) are applied once, server-side, in `getHeaderTasks`. Portal activity: badge = day-groups with an unread row among the 200-row feed, list = all those groups. Approvals: badge = holds + notices, both listed (the live DB's 57 pending notices are why the admin badge reads high — by design, they are FYIs). `MyApprovalsBadge`: badge = pending holds only, as its docblock says.
- **FE-F3-2's fix holds on every hold-tier path.** Each `outcome === 'pending'` branch now calls `notifyApprovalsChanged()`: `NewWorkComponent:317`, `WorkComponent:324,754`, `EditPatientComponent:316`, `PatientManagement:504`, `useExpenses:230,255`.
- **The portal-activity `is_read` NULL mismatch is unreachable.** The client counts `!r.is_read` (NULL = unread) while `markActivityRead` updates only `is_read = false`, so a NULL row could never be cleared — but the portal has no INSERT grant on `is_read` (DB default `false`, `phase3-announcements.sql`), and the live table holds 0 NULLs.
- **Currency separation holds on all three statistics tabs.** Commissions and Breakdown never add IQD to USD; the USD-equivalent is null-propagating (no house rate — `totals.usdEq` stays `null` unless a row converted), the ranking chart is omitted rather than drawn empty when there is no rate, and the Daily view's converted figures render `—` via `formatConverted`. The breakdown rate is the server-echoed latest real rate; the monthly view's is per-day on the server. `DoctorPaymentsModal`'s headline totals come from the clicked aggregate row, never from summing a possibly-truncated list (and it says so).
- **Charts are theme-correct except the title (FE-F5-15).** `chartSetup.ts` registers an explicit subset (not `chart.js/auto`); both chart effects list `resolvedTheme` in their deps and rebuild; the `${hex}cc` alpha trick is safe because `--success-green` and `--primary-color` resolve to 6-digit hex in both themes (`#22c55e`/`#40c057`, `#007bff`/`#4dabf7`); each effect destroys its instance on cleanup.
- **Popover geometry is fine down to 360 px.** All four set `max-width: calc(100vw - 24px)` and `placePopover` clamps `left` to ≥ 8, so nothing overflows horizontally.
- **`<Modal>` census, case-insensitive this time: still 0 hand-rolled overlays.** `grep -rni "modaloverlay\|modal-overlay"` finds one hit, `DailyInvoicesModal`'s `overlayClassName={styles.statisticsModalOverlay}` — a prop on `<Modal>`, sanctioned. (F4's case-sensitive grep would not have matched `…ModalOverlay`; this confirms it missed nothing.)
- **`AlertModal`** — create/edit bodies match `alertBody` exactly (numbers `parseInt(…, 10)`, `''` clears the two dates, `assignedTo` omitted so context alerts stay unassigned); render-phase init keyed on open + id; `notifyTasksChanged()` + `onSave` refresh both surfaces. Its missing `unsavedGuard` is RA5's documented deferral, not re-filed; its English strings are FE-F5-14.
- **`TaskFormModal`** — render-phase populate/reset keyed on open + `alert_id`; the patient typeahead debounces 250 ms and aborts the previous request; create posts exactly `createTask.body`; `unsavedGuard` is wired (RA5).
- **`services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}.ts`** — re-confirmed F1's verdict: thin mutation wrappers through the funnel (CSRF via `postJSON`/`putJSON`/`patchJSON`/`deleteJSON`). Mutation responses carry no `{ schema }`, correctly (the rule is for reads).
- **`DoctorCommissionsView`** — per-currency totals, quit doctors included for their periods, drill-down is a real `<button>` (keyboard-reachable). `commission_percentage` is the doctor's *current* rate applied to past periods — a server-side design choice (`[[doctor-commission-feature]]`), not a client defect.
- **Dashboard** — the saved order merges forward (unknown keys dropped, new cards appended), the role filter is applied at render so a hidden card keeps its slot, the RTL arrow mapping is symmetric, and the card-footer arrow is flipped by hand in CSS because postcss-rtlcss cannot reach a Font Awesome glyph. On the i18n ratchet with nothing to report.
- **CSS modules** — the literal fallbacks in `MyApprovalsBadge`/`ApprovalsBell` (`var(--warning-100, #fef9c3)` etc.) are all behind **defined** tokens with dark overrides, i.e. dead fallbacks for F24's strip, not dark-theme holes. `StatisticsComponent.module.css`'s ~23 classes with no reader in its own component are all used by `DailyInvoicesModal`, which imports the same sheet. `!important`: StatisticsComponent's is `@media print`; Dashboard's two are FE-F5-17.

## F4 · 2026-09-17

Scope read in full: the 17 shell/primitive components listed in the plan (2,975 LOC). Everything below was checked and needs no further work — **do not re-derive it.**

- **The hand-rolled-overlay census is still 0.** `grep -rln "modalOverlay\|modal-overlay" public/js --include='*.tsx'` → **0 files**, matching the 2026-09-15 baseline the plan asked this session to confirm. Every overlay in the staff app goes through `<Modal>`; 67 `<Modal>` render sites across 59 files. Any later session that finds one files it against the owning feature.
- **The header at 412 px is clean** (runtime-measured, Galaxy-S23-Ultra portrait, `/dashboard`): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth === 412` with **zero** overflow offenders, header height 92 px, and all five right-region controls at full size — three 42×42 bells, the 46×24 theme pill, the 60×34 user pill — with the three nav tabs (94/109/71 px) wrapped onto their own row. `universal-header.css` carries four breakpoints (1024 / 768 / 640 / 480) and the 768 one pins `max-width:100vw; overflow-x:hidden` on the container.
- **Bell role-gating is correct and reads from the shared enum.** `UniversalHeader:331-332` renders `<ApprovalsBell/>` only for `ROLES.ADMIN` and `<MyApprovalsBadge/>` only for `ROLES.FRONT_DESK`, imported from `@shared/auth/roles` (not a string literal). `TasksBell` and `PortalActivityBell` are deliberately all-roles. Whether the *endpoints* behind them enforce the same split is **F5**'s, not a hole here.
- **The body-scroll lock is genuinely nest-safe.** `openCount` is module-scoped and refcounted, the scrollbar-width compensation only applies at count 1, and the saved `overflow`/`paddingRight` are restored only at 0 — so the live Expense→Lookup nesting (and any future stack) cannot leak `overflow:hidden` onto `<body>`. `aria-hidden` is set on `#app-root` **and** `#single-spa-application`; both exist in `public/index.html` (216/270) and `#modal-root` (225) is their **sibling**, so the dialog is never inside the hidden subtree. (The `single-spa-*` id is dead branding from a retired framework — cosmetic, and it belongs to **F23**'s HTML-entry-point pass, not here.)
- **Focus handling in `Modal` is right apart from the naming bug (FE-F4-2).** Initial focus prefers `initialFocusRef`, falls back to the first focusable, then to the dialog itself with `preventScroll`; restore is guarded by `document.contains(prev)` so focus can't be thrown at `<body>` when the trigger unmounted; the Tab trap wraps both directions and handles the zero-focusable case. `initialFocusRef` is passed by exactly two consumers (`PaymentFormDrawer:146`, `files/FileExplorer:548`) and **both pass a stable `useRef`**, so the `[isOpen, initialFocusRef]` deps can't churn.
- **The backdrop-dismiss gesture is implemented correctly** (separate from FE-F4-6, which is about the *default*): `onMouseDown` records whether the press started on the backdrop and `onClick` requires both ends to be there, so releasing a drag over the backdrop does not dismiss. The drag itself is transform-on-a-ref (no re-render), clamped to keep 60 px on screen on both axes, re-centred on every open, auto-disabled for `/drawer/i` overlays and `≤768 px`, and cleans up `user-select` + pointer capture on unmount.
- **`ContentRenderer`'s lazy map cannot drift**, re-confirmed: 19 `lazyPage()` registrations cover 20 page strings (`'work'` and `'diagnosis'` share the `Diagnosis` chunk by construction), `preloaders` is populated *by* `lazyPage`, and `preloadPatientPage` swallows rejections so a speculative warm-up never files a spurious `[client-error]`. The only switch cases with no preloader are the three dead stubs (**FE-F2-12**). Unknown `page` renders a "Page Not Found" card rather than throwing.
- **A lazy patient-tab chunk failure lands in a class boundary**, as it must: both `PatientShell` routes (`routes.config.tsx:397`, `:411`) are wrapped in `<RouteErrorBoundary>`, which F3 established is class-backed. (That the boundary is *wider* than the Suspense is FE-F4-17 — a scoping question, not a coverage hole.)
- **`ThemeToggle`, `NavigationProgress`, `ModalHeader`, `PhoneInput` — nothing to report.** `ThemeToggle` is a real `<button role="switch" aria-checked>` driven by the *resolved* theme (so it shows the right side under `auto`); `NavigationProgress` is stateless and unmounts itself; `ModalHeader` wires `titleId`/`aria-label`/`data-modal-drag-handle` correctly and is the only place the close button's markup lives; `PhoneInput` pins `dir="ltr"` so digit groups and the caret stay LTR inside an Arabic form, and `PhoneDisplay` does the same for display + `tel:` links.
- **`UserMenu` logout is correct.** It is one of CLAUDE.md's sanctioned `window.location` exceptions, and it calls `queryClient.clear()` **before** the reload, so the next user in that tab cannot read the previous user's cached patient data. Popover close-on-outside-click/Escape/resize all unregister on cleanup.
- **The protocol-handler contract still holds.** `explorer:` (Navigation `:220`) and `csimaging:`/`dolphin:` are the sanctioned full-navigation exceptions; `PatientsFolder` is still UNC in the DB (`\\CLINIC\Clinic1\`, trailing separator present) and `VideosPath` still local (`C:\clinic1\ovideos\`), matching CLAUDE.md's client-facing-vs-server-local split. The staleness of the *client cache* is FE-F4-3; the values themselves are right.
- **Why there are two patient searches — answered** (see FE-F4-13): `PatientSearchCombobox` is the successor and owns `PatientManagement`'s two fields; `PatientQuickSearch` survives for `TransferWorkModal` alone. Both use the same matching rules, and the combobox's keyboard contract (ArrowUp/Down + Enter to jump, plain Enter to submit, Escape to dismiss with `stopPropagation` so a host modal stays open) is implemented correctly, with `aria-activedescendant` and `onMouseDown` preventDefault so a click beats the blur.
- **`UniversalHeader`'s sticky patient tab is sound.** The render-phase keyed guard (`patientCode !== stickyCode`) is the repo idiom and is idempotent; the `/patient/(\d+)` anchor correctly excludes `/aligner/patient/:workId`; `sessionStorage` (not `localStorage`) keeps it per-tab; and the tab reads `patientInfoQuery` on the **same** key `PatientShell` uses, so it resolves from cache and refreshes on any patient invalidation.
- **`PatientShell`'s scroll memory is correct**: keyed by `pathname + search` (so a timepoint slot gets its own slot), saved rAF-throttled with cleanup, restored across up to 8 frames for late-growing photo grids.
- **`SettingsTabNavigation`'s mobile auto-scroll is horizontal-only** (`container.scrollBy`), so it cannot move the page; the active-button ref is conditional and re-attaches before the effect reads it.

## F3 · 2026-09-16

Read closely, nothing found (or the only findings are the ones filed above):

- **Error-boundary coverage is complete, and the class/function split is right.** All 41 `path:` entries in `routes.config.tsx` were walked: every route that renders a component is wrapped in `<RouteErrorBoundary routeName="…">`; the only unwrapped entries are the eleven `<Navigate>` redirects and `/chair-display`, which carries its own `errorElement: <RouteError />`. `RouteErrorBoundary` → `ErrorBoundary`'s **default export** (`ErrorBoundaryWithNavigate`, which supplies `resetKey={location.pathname}`) → the **class** `ErrorBoundary`, so a `React.lazy` chunk rejection — which React re-throws during render and function boundaries cannot see — lands in a class as it must. `GlobalErrorBoundary` correctly imports the **named** class instead, because it wraps `<RouterProvider>` in `App.tsx` where `useNavigate`/`useLocation` would throw. This answers the question F2 left open.
- **`ErrorBoundary.componentDidCatch` is correct** — `selfHealChunkError(…, 'render-boundary')` runs first and returns early while a reload is in flight (so a stale tab heals silently), and only a chunk error that survived its reload is reported; everything else goes to `reportClientError` with `componentStack`. `componentDidUpdate` clears a caught error when `resetKey` changes, so navigating away from a crashed screen is not a dead end. `RouteError` runs the same self-heal from an effect (out of render) and reports only `reload-exhausted`.
- **`ThemeContext`, `LanguageContext`, `FontContext` — clean, and structurally identical on purpose.** Lazy `useState` initializers read the same stored value the `index.html` FOUC script already applied (so the mount reconcile is a no-op on the happy path), `value` is memoized, every `storage`/`matchMedia` listener is removed in its cleanup, and `setLanguage` writes storage → DOM → i18next → state in that order so there is no frame of stale direction. `applyLanguageAttributes(lang, path?)` resolves `dir` through `resolveDirection(lang, currentPath)`, i.e. **route-scoped**, and the cross-tab `storage` handler calls `i18n.changeLanguage` as well as the attribute apply — the two halves that would otherwise flip layout without flipping strings.
- **`ToastContext`'s memo is deliberate and correct** (it carries the comment explaining the refetch-storm it prevents), the auto-dismiss timer is cleared on unmount, manual close and auto-dismiss are idempotent against each other, and `window.toast` is installed and `delete`d symmetrically. The only gap is the missing live region (FE-F3-4).
- **`ConfirmContext` cannot leak a promise** — replacing a pending confirm resolves the old one `false` first; the only note is where that resolve happens (FE-F3-10).
- **The SSE hooks' subscribe/unsubscribe symmetry is total.** `useAppointmentsSync` (5 transport listeners + `appointments_updated` + `window online`), `useWhatsAppSync` (10 listeners) and `useWhatsAppAuth` (6) each `off()` exactly what they `on()` and call `sseAppointments.release()` / `sseWhatsapp.release()` in the same cleanup, and every `setTimeout`/`setInterval` in the three files is cleared except the one noted in FE-F3-5. Under StrictMode's mount→cleanup→mount, the refcount returns to 0, `disconnect()` nulls the handle, and the second mount's `connect()` re-opens — the pending first `ensureConnected()` promise resolves off the emitter's `connected` event rather than a specific `EventSource`, so it cannot hang.
- **`useAppointmentsSync`'s recovery pipeline is sound** — one debounce window shared by the reconnect, `online` and 5-minute-safety-net triggers; the retry recursion routed through `runRecoveryRef` to dodge the TDZ/immutability trap; `cancelledRef` set in the effect body and cleared in cleanup so a late retry after unmount is a no-op; `markStale()` on failure so the "Live | Stale" indicator reflects the gap instead of lying. The broadcast handler double-checks `receivedDate === expectedDate` even though the server no longer filters. Only the midnight derivation (FE-F3-9) is open.
- **The QR-viewer gate survives the app-wide SSE subscription.** `GlobalStateProvider` holds a permanent refcount on the WhatsApp stream for the whole session, and `services/messaging/sse-whatsapp.ts` registers a QR viewer for every stream — so `activeQRViewers > 0` is true whenever any staff tab is open, which would defeat the documented on-demand-init gate. It does not, because `whatsapp.ts:2313` blocks the on-demand path outright under `WHATSAPP_AUTO_INIT=false` and says why in a comment naming this exact subscription. Register/unregister is latched on both sides (`cleanedUp`, `closedEarly`) so the count cannot drift. Nothing to file — recorded so **F16** does not re-derive it.
- **`GlobalStateContext`'s WhatsApp reconcile is the right design.** `whatsapp_client_ready` and `whatsapp_qr_updated` are one-shot events that never replay to a stream that opens later; reconciling from `/api/wa/initial-state` on every `connected` (plus once immediately when the transport was already fresh) closes the whole missed-event class for every consumer at once. The QR handler reads the frame's own `clientReady` rather than inferring it from a blanked `qr`, which is what keeps non-finance tabs from believing WhatsApp is unlinked. Both behaviours are commented with the bug they fixed; leave them alone.
- **`useAppointments`' mutation path** — POST → `invalidateQueries` → render with no optimistic state, `loadAppointments` returning the post-invalidation query status so the sync hook can retry, and silent recovery on the server's 400 conflict. The loader seed is gated on `initialData.loadedDate === date && !initialData.error` and carries `initialDataUpdatedAt`, so a stale loader payload cannot masquerade as fresh, and a date change correctly drops to a normal fetch. Only the casts (FE-F3-8) and the duplicated predicate (FE-F3-18) are filed.
- **`useLabCases` is the reference implementation** — seven mutations, all through `useApiMutation`, all invalidating both `qk.labCases.all()` **and** `qk.work.detailsList(workId)` via one shared helper, with `workId` passed in the variables precisely because the raw row does not carry it. This is the shape FE-F2-13 wants everywhere.
- **`useImportFolder`** — `loadSaved` is written as a `.then`-chain (not `async`) specifically so no `setState` runs synchronously in the mount effect; `requestPermission` is never called outside a user gesture; `loading` starts from `supported` so the unsupported case needs no effect. Its only defect is inherited from FE-F1-5 (the IndexedDB handles it opens are never closed).
- **`useLocalizedName`, `useAppointmentDoctors`, `useLabelModal`, `useSetDrawer`, `useBatchDrawer`, `useMessageCount`** — clean. The four drawer/modal hooks are single-caller extractions out of `PatientSets`, which is deliberate decomposition of a 2,340-line screen, **not** a one-caller-should-be-inlined finding. `useAppointmentDoctors` memoizes `buildDoctorColors` on the query data so the legend and the card tints cannot disagree.
- **No hook in `hooks/` has zero importers** (counted: `useStand` 22, `useExpenses` 7, `useLocalizedName` 5, `useWhatsAppAuth` 4, `useDateManager`/`useLabCases`/`useLookupManager` 3, `useImportFolder`/`useAppointmentDoctors` 2, the rest 1).
- **`useMessageStatus`'s SSE→invalidate loop** — a 400 ms debounce coalesces the per-message status burst during a live send, `query.isLoading` (not `isFetching`) drives the placeholder so the table never blanks mid-send, and the debounce timer is cleared on every dep change. `EMPTY_MESSAGES` is a module constant, so the empty case keeps a stable identity.
- **`PrintQueueContext`'s persistence survives navigation as designed** — `sessionStorage` seeded through a lazy initializer (no mount effect), written on every queue change, and the provider sits in `RootLayout` above `RootLayoutInner`, so neither the queue nor the open print modal unmounts on a route change. `buildDefaultLabels` goes through the shared `buildLabelsFromRanges`, so a template batch's sequence 0 is preserved rather than `|| 0`-ed away.
- **The browser-locale date census (FE-F3-3), recorded so no session re-derives it** — 53 sites in 36 files: `SyncStatusPanel` (4) · `MessageStatusTable`, `IntegrationsSettings`, `AppointmentCalendar` (3 each) · `AppointmentTab`, `PatientSets`, `WorkComponent`, `ViewPatientInfo`, `NewWorkComponent`, `HolidayEditor`, `DailyInvoicesModal`, `AdminUserManagement` (2 each) · `TasksHistory`, `ChairDisplay`, `VisitsTab`, `PhotosTab`, `PaymentsTab`, `Announcements`, `useMessageStatus`, `TemplateCard`, `StockMovementsModal`, `ItemTable`, `LabCaseModal`, `fileHelpers`, `SelectionPanel`, `XraysComponent`, `WorkDetailItem`, `WebCephModal`, `ProtocolHandlersSettings`, `PortalAccessCard`, `MonthlyCalendarGrid`, `HolidayQuickModal`, `CalendarDayContextMenu`, `AnalogClock`, `ExpenseTable`, `DeleteConfirmModal` (1 each).
- **The weekend definition, settled once and now authoritative** (FE-F3-1, confirmed by the owner 2026-09-17): the clinic week is **Sat–Thu, FRIDAY the only day off**. Implemented in `CalendarViewService.ts:441` (`if (d.getDay() === 5) continue`), `utils/calendarDate.ts#addWorkingDays`/`getWeekStartSaturday`, `SimplifiedCalendarPicker`, `useDateManager.getDefaultDate()` (Thursday → +2 to reach Saturday) and `utils/arabic-day.ts` (index 5 → `''`). `MonthlyCalendarGrid.isWeekend` was the single dissenter and is gone. **CLAUDE.md's "Fri+Sat weekends" prose was wrong and has been corrected**, including the ~2M → ~2.4M ceiling arithmetic derived from it; the week shape is now its own top-level note there. No later session needs to re-derive this.

## F2 · 2026-09-16

Read closely, nothing found (or the only findings are the ones filed above):

- **`query/keys.ts` — the hierarchy holds.** Every group's `all()` is a true prefix of every leaf beneath it (checked leaf by leaf, all 152), **no two factories share a key** (mechanical check over all 127 `queryOptions` blocks), and **no factory omits a parameter its `queryFn` interpolates into the URL** — the classic "patient A's data served under patient B's key" shape is absent. `normId()` collapses number/string ids at the SSoT and `keys.test.ts` pins that, plus the number-parent/string-child prefix match. The only two structural oddities are filed (FE-F2-8, FE-F2-10).
- **`query/queries.ts` — the schema discipline is total.** 124 of 127 factories carry `{ schema }`; the three that do not (`diagnosisQuery`, `supabaseStatusQuery`, `dolphinStatusQuery`) each carry an inline `eslint-disable … --` with the reason, exactly as the convention requires. No factory hand-writes a response `interface` (all are `z.infer<typeof …>`); the one place an interface is declared (`SyncSinkStatus`/`SyncDriftReport`/`SyncSinkStatusResponse`, `:1540-1597`) is the deliberately contract-less sync-status pair and is documented as such.
- **The `queries.ts` SSoT is actually observed.** Only **three** `useQuery` calls in the whole app define a `queryKey` outside `queries.ts` — `useAppointments`, `useMessageStatus`, `useMessageCount` — and all three are the SSE screens the tracker sanctions. Zero raw `useMutation` imports anywhere.
- **`enabled` guards are right where a key would otherwise be built from a placeholder.** Every factory that coalesces a nullable id into its key (`workId ?? 0`, `id ?? 0`, `categoryId ?? 0`, `doctorId ?? 0`, `startDate ?? ''`) pairs it with an `enabled` that is false in exactly that case, so the placeholder key is never fetched into.
- **`query/client.ts` error sinks** — `queryCache`/`mutationCache` `onError` forward only 5xx + contract-drift (`validation !== undefined`) to `/api/client-error`; a 4xx is correctly filtered, and the report is a raw POST so it cannot re-enter the caches. The `retry` value is the one finding (FE-F2-3).
- **`query/useApiMutation.ts`** — the variadic `onSuccess` forward is arity-agnostic (survives TanStack adding a context arg), invalidations are awaited before the caller's `onSuccess`, and `isInvalidStateTransition` is unit-tested across all the odd shapes. Nothing wrong with the helper; only its adoption (FE-F2-13).
- **`query/loaderQuery.ts` error mapping** — AbortError rethrows for React Router, an already-mapped `Response` propagates untouched (so a nested loader's 401 isn't double-handled), any numeric status becomes `Response(status)` for the `errorElement`, everything else becomes 500. Correct, except that the 401 arm is unreachable (FE-F2-2).
- **`router/loaders.ts` — the other seven loaders.** `patientShellLoader` parallelises patient/work/timepoints with `Promise.all` and gates the timepoints leg on the page actually needing it; `alignerPatientWorkLoader` validates `workId` with a radix-10 `parseInt` and 404s a work with no `person_id` before the second read; `templateDesignerLoader` correctly no-ops into create mode; `labTrackingLoader`'s `{}` prefetch **does** hit the component's key (RQ's `hashKey` drops `undefined`-valued properties, so `LabTracking`'s `{status:undefined,…}` filters hash identically to `{}`); `dailyAppointmentsLoader` is a deliberate raw fetch that is bridged into RQ by `useAppointments`'s `initialData` + `initialDataUpdatedAt` — **not** a double fetch.
- **`router/routes.config.tsx` mechanics** — `lazyRoute().preload()` swallows its rejection so a speculative warm-up can't file a spurious `[client-error]` (the render path still surfaces a genuinely dead chunk), and `withPreload` pairs every loader-bearing route with its chunk. All 41 `path:` entries reviewed: each nested group ends in a `path:'*'` → `<Navigate>` so there is no dead-end route, and `/chair-display` sits outside `RootLayout` with no context consumers at all (grep: `ChairDisplay.tsx` uses none of `useToast`/`useTheme`/`useLanguage`/`useConfirm`/`useGlobalState`/`usePrintQueue`), so the kiosk's provider-free mount is safe.
- **`errorElement` coverage: 2, and that is enough.** `<RouteError />` on `/chair-display` and on `RootLayout`; every other route relies on the `RouteErrorBoundary` wrapper for render errors and bubbles loader failures to the root one. `RouteError` is self-sufficient — `useNavigate` + `<Link>` for recovery, and it runs the same `selfHealChunkError` the class boundary does — so replacing the layout is survivable. (Which boundary catches what, and whether `RouteErrorBoundary` is class-backed, is **F3**'s to settle.)
- **`ContentRenderer`'s preloader map cannot drift** from its lazy components: `preloaders` is populated *by* `lazyPage()`, so one page string maps to exactly one factory by construction. The only switch cases with no preloader are the three dead stubs (FE-F2-12). F4 can take this as answered.
- **`layouts/RootLayout.tsx`** — provider nesting is deliberate and documented (Theme outermost so `#modal-root` inherits the vars); the header's `dir` is pinned to the *language* while `<html dir>` follows the *route*, which is the documented RTL split; the `applyDirectionForPath` navigation watcher covers the axis `LanguageContext` doesn't. `ScrollRestoration`'s `getKey` includes `location.search`, so the patient-management deep-link keeps its own scroll slot.
- **`layouts/AlignerLayout.tsx`, `config/workTypeConfig.ts`, `config/expenseCategories.ts`, `constants/sse-liveness.ts`, `query/keys.test.ts`, `query/useApiMutation.test.ts`** — clean. (`workTypeConfig` correctly re-exports the ids from the cross-boundary `shared/treatment-taxonomy` rather than re-declaring them.)
- **Settings option keys are consistent**: `optionQuery(name)` is used for exactly three option names (the calendar slot settings) and `CalendarTimesSettings` invalidates exactly those three; `GeneralSettings` writes different names and invalidates `qk.settings.options()` + `qk.branding()`. The sibling relationship between `settings.options` and `settings.option(name)` is harmless here — no reader crosses it.

## F1 · 2026-09-16

Read closely, nothing found (or the only findings are the ones filed above):

- **`core/http.ts`'s CSRF machinery** — single-flight `ensureCsrfToken`, exactly one transparent retry on a 403 `EBADCSRFTOKEN`, and no infinite loop (`csrfRetried`). Checked against the server: `middleware/csrf.ts:125` sends `{ success:false, error:'…', code:'EBADCSRFTOKEN' }`, which is exactly what the client matches on. Concurrent 403s cost an extra token fetch but stay correct.
- **`httpErrorMessage`** — `utils/error-response.ts` types `error` as `string` on every path, so the `data?.error || data?.message || err.message || fallback` chain can never print `[object Object]`.
- **Envelope unwrap** — deliberately conservative (`success === true` AND a `data` key); bare arrays and top-level `{success, user}` shapes pass through. Matches its tests.
- **The M8 retry policy** — mutations are never retried (`!mutation` in the retriable check), `AbortError` never retries, `TimeoutError`/`TypeError`/502/503/504 do. `withTimeout` cleans up both the timer and the caller-signal listener, and the CSRF retry path re-arms its own timeout.
- **`core/error-reporter.ts`** — throttle (30 s per signature), dedupe, the 50/page cap, and the never-throws guarantee all hold; `isChunkFetchMessage` skips the self-healing chunk noise; a contract-drift `HttpError` (which carries `validation` and **no** `status`) correctly passes the `unhandledrejection` filter and gets reported.
- **`core/chunk-reload.ts`** — the `vite:preloadError` contract is right: `preventDefault()` happens **only** when a reload is actually starting, so the `undefined → reading 'default'` manufactured error from `[[admin-403-root-router-incident]]` can't come back.
- **`core/language.ts` / `core/theme.ts` / `core/font.ts`** — registry + raw-string storage round-trip agrees with the inline FOUC scripts; `applyDirectionForPath` keeps route-scoped RTL honest. (One latent list drift filed in FE-F1-11.)
- **`utils/formatters.ts`** — the Arabic weekday hand-map is index-correct for `getDay()`, digits stay Western in both languages (`numberLocale: 'en-US'`), and `language` is an explicit parameter everywhere, never module state — the React Compiler staleness trap is avoided as documented.
- **`services/sse-appointments.ts`** — refcount lifecycle is symmetric; `connect()` always closes the previous handle; the `visibilitychange`/`pageshow` handlers attach once and are guarded by `refcount > 0`; `disconnect()` only fires at refcount 0.
- **`services/photo-render-watch.ts`** — one `ensureConnected`/`release` pair per job, `settle()` is idempotent, and the per-job timeout covers a server restart mid-render.
- **`services/tasks.ts` / `approvals.ts` / `portal-activity.ts`** — thin, contract-typed mutation wrappers; correct funnel use (CSRF via `postJSON`/`putJSON`/`patchJSON`).
- **`utils/phoneFormatter.ts`, `utils/chartTheme.ts`, `utils/chartSetup.ts` (explicit Chart.js registration, not `/auto`), `utils/aligner-labels.ts`, `constants/sse-liveness.ts`** — clean.
- **`core/http.test.ts` (242 ln) / `core/chunk-reload.test.ts`** — 21 cases covering unwrap, schema fail-loud, CSRF attach/cache/retry/give-up, timeout, retry policy and `httpErrorMessage`. The gaps are the two paths filed above: a non-JSON body **with** a schema, and the CSRF fetch's own timeout.

# Remediation log

Per remediation session: what was fixed, the commit, whether `npm run gate` passed, and anything deliberately **not** fixed (with the reason — a deliberate non-fix must end up annotated in the code too, the way `docs/services-audit.md` does it).

## RC2 · 2026-09-30 — the rest of Phase C: F10's other 🟡, the 🔵 of F10 + F11, and RC1's partial rows

The session RC1 queued. **25 rows closed, none left partial**: F10's five remaining 🟡 (FE-F10-3, -4, -8, -9, -11), RC1's ten partial rows (FE-F10-5, -6, -10, -16, -17, -18, -19, FE-F11-9, -11, FE-F2-8), and every other 🔵 of F10 and F11 (FE-F10-12, -13, -14, -15, -20, FE-F11-8, -12, -13, -14, -15). **Phase C is now fully remediated**: no F10 or F11 row is open.

**`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, only F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings, **36 files / 292 tests**, contracts **D1 0 / D2 8**, build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed by exactly one line**, and the diff was accepted:

```
- GET     /api/calendar/stats  [validate(query)]
```

**One migration, data only**: `1789460600000_pin-legacy-doctor-colours.sql`, see FE-F10-11. No DDL, so no Supabase half; `cdc_capture` replicates its two UPDATEs like any Employee Settings save. It ran clean on a fresh install, where it is a no-op.

### Owner decisions taken during RC2 (2026-09-29)

| Finding | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F10-13 | Who counts as a "calendar doctor"? The booking lists, legend and daily board used the `get_appointments` flag; the calendar filter and the server used position = Doctor. | **Position = Doctor** (active employees). Every list reads `/api/doctors`; the server keeps its rule. The edit form still shows an appointment's departed doctor as "(current)". |
| FE-F10-11 | `doctorColors.ts` coloured employee ids 1 and 7. How does this clinic keep its two colours once the literals go? | **Guarded data migration**: it writes the two hexes only where the id **and** the name match **and** no colour is set yet. |

### What changed, per finding

**F10's 🟡**
- **FE-F10-3**: a holiday no longer hides its appointments. The week grid renders a holiday column's bookings (no covering sash) and its header reads "Holiday · 1 appt"; the month cell keeps its count.
- **FE-F10-4**: the range and month factories carry `placeholderData: keepPreviousData`. The toolbar and zoom slider stay mounted and enabled during a load, and a spinner in the toolbar (`aria-busy`) replaces the full-page "Loading Calendar...".
- **FE-F10-5** (completes RC1): the client pages months from the 1st (`monthStep`), so Jan 31 → Next is February. Switching to Month opens the visible week's month.
- **FE-F10-6** (completes RC1): the `/month` read computes the month's own stats, doctor-filtered, and the header rounds the percentage. `GET /api/calendar/stats` (always one week), `getCalendarStats`, `calendarStatsQuery` and `getWeekEnd` are gone. The shared slot-settings read lives in `CalendarViewService`.
- **FE-F10-8**: a drop on a past slot is refused on the client, and the server now refuses one too (`PAST_SLOT`, mapped by `bookingError`). A move to another day sends the WhatsApp confirmation, like the edit form. The drag payload is the appointment id, not a position.
- **FE-F10-9**: Update navigates once. Back and Cancel fall back to the patient's appointment list when there is no in-app history (`ContentRenderer#goBackOr`), so a pasted link never leaves the app.
- **FE-F10-10** (completes RC1): the calendar's holiday writes refresh `qk.adminLookups.table('tblHolidays')`, and Settings → Holidays' writes refresh `qk.calendar.all()`.
- **FE-F10-11**: no staff or schedule literals are left.
  - `doctorColors.ts` has no id table. A doctor without `appointment_color` is neutral everywhere, including the Employee Settings picker ("None (neutral)").
  - The picker's `14:00`/`14:30` extras and its 12:00–13:30 / 21:00–22:30 fallbacks are gone. "Extended" means the `EarlySlots`/`LateSlots` options, exactly as on the grid, and none when they are unset.

**F10's 🔵**
- **FE-F10-12**: the extended slots render once, in time order. An all-booked extended set shows without a toggle.
- **FE-F10-13**: one definition, per the owner's decision above.
  - `/api/doctors` returns active Doctors in the Settings sort order, each with its `appointment_color` (`staff.contract#doctors`). It feeds the booking forms, the calendar filter and legend, and the daily board (`useAppointmentDoctors`).
  - `AppointmentService.verifyDoctor` requires an active Doctor for a new booking or a doctor change, while an edit that keeps a since-departed doctor stays valid (`INVALID_DOCTOR`, translated).
  - The edit form lists that doctor as "<name> (current)", using the `DrName` the by-id read now carries.
  - Employee Settings shows a swatch only for calendar doctors.
- **FE-F10-14**: new `roleCaps().manageLookups` (admin + front desk, the mount's own tier; pinned in `role-registry.test.ts`). The day menu offers the holiday actions only to those roles. A failed appointments-on-date check now shows in the holiday modal instead of vanishing.
- **FE-F10-15**: new `hooks/useFloatingMenu.ts`, shared by both menus.
  - `anchorFrom()` places a keyboard-opened menu under its element, and the six `KeyboardEvent as unknown as MouseEvent` casts are gone.
  - Menus are clamped to the viewport, take focus, move with the arrow keys, and return focus on Escape.
  - The day header opens its menu by click or Enter, and the menu gains "Open day view". The month grid's click panel has the same button, so day view is no longer double-click-only.
  - The stray "0" is gone.
- **FE-F10-16** (completes RC1): the four client sites parse dates with `parseLocalDate`.
- **FE-F10-17** (completes RC1): no English literal reaches the translated booking files.
  - `bookingError` maps `PAST_SLOT` and `INVALID_DOCTOR` too, and the WhatsApp fallbacks are catalog strings.
  - The picker's month buttons are named and mirror in RTL. Its tiles use the new `formatters#formatClockTime` (12-hour plus the language's marker).
- **FE-F10-18** (completes RC1): no hand-written types or casts over contracted reads are left.
  - New `BookingForm.tsx` holds the ~180 lines the two forms duplicated. `AppointmentForm` and `EditAppointmentForm` are thin wrappers, and `BookingForm.tsx` is in the i18n ratchet.
  - The void PUT is a plain `putJSON`. The picker's types derive from the contract, `CalendarHeader` imports its types, and the four `console.error` calls are gone.
  - `qk.calendar.availability(startDate, endDate)` / `slots(date)` take the real parameters.
- **FE-F10-19** (completes RC1): dead code removed.
  - Selection mode (`initialDate`/`initialViewMode`/`mode`/`onSlotSelect`/`selectedSlot`, `CalendarMode`, the month grid's `mode`), `TINT_MODE`, the slot's "＋" mark and its `role="button"`.
  - The dead CSS. `.calendar-error` and its actions are now styled.
- **FE-F10-20**: all three deletes (calendar appointment, calendar holiday, patient list) go through `useConfirm`, toast success and carry an in-flight guard; the bespoke modals and their CSS are gone. Narrowing the window forces one day without persisting it. `AnalogClock` lost its `exhaustive-deps` memo, so React Compiler handles it.

**F11's 🔵**
- **FE-F11-8**: new `utils/appointmentsDate.ts` (`rememberAppointmentsDate`/`appointmentsPath`).
  - A board following today is not pinned in `sessionStorage`, so the header and the Dashboard tile open today. A picked date still reopens.
  - A board showing today rolls to the new day at midnight, while a picked date stays put.
  - An unknown `?dr=` reads "All doctors" and is dropped from the URL.
- **FE-F11-9** (completes RC1): `StatsCards`' `cards` variant and its CSS, the `/appointments` route's calendar-CSS import, Vite's `/sse` proxy and the stale comments are gone.
- **FE-F11-11** (completes RC1): the accessibility gaps are closed.
  - The header's weekday uses the shared explicit-language formatter (سبت, not السبت), and the day chevrons mirror in RTL.
  - The search box has a label, and the mobile toggle is a labelled group with `aria-pressed`.
  - The doctor listbox opens by key, moves with the arrows, and returns focus on Escape.
- **FE-F11-12**: `useAppointments` splits `initialLoading` (skeletons) from `busy` (a click in flight, or another day's placeholder). A background refetch neither dims nor blanks the lists.
  - `useAppointmentsSync` coalesces a burst per day: three writes from another desk cause one read.
  - Refresh from another date reads today once.
  - The flash timer is single and cleared.
- **FE-F11-13**: Videos.
  - The player uses `ModalHeader`, and closing it cancels the stream: the source is set in an effect and emptied on cleanup, not a `src` prop that StrictMode's double setup would leave empty.
  - Delete uses `useConfirm` with a guard.
  - The upload form has `unsavedGuard`, and closing it mid-upload aborts the request (no toast, no video).
- **FE-F11-14**: an upload appends to a pending playlist and a delete removes only that file from it. Neither discards the draft any more.
  - The tab's unsaved flag covers the playlist.
  - *Seconds per picture* can be cleared while typing; it keeps the last value on blur.
- **FE-F11-15**: the kiosk.
  - (a) The visit date is parsed locally.
  - (b) The server keeps a `Set` of streams per chair, so two kiosks on one chair both stay connected and both get the patient.
  - (c) The idle clock follows a resize.
  - (d) On a CLOSED stream the kiosk reloads only once `/health/basic` answers. It probes 15 s after the error, then at growing intervals (15 s, 30 s, then every 60 s), so a server that is still down never strands it on a proxy's error page.
- **FE-F2-8** (completes RC1): `qk.whatsapp` has a real root (`['whatsapp']`), and `keys.test.ts` pins it.

### Runtime verification: 65/65 on a throwaway demo database

A throwaway PostgreSQL 16 cluster was set to `Asia/Baghdad` and built with `db:migrate`. `db:setup` set it up as "Bright Smile Orthodontics", and `db:seed:demo` added the demo data. It used scratch `MACHINE_PATH`/TV directories, with every sink and WhatsApp off. Fixtures:
- a booking on the demo holiday;
- a flagged Assistant, and a departed doctor with a future booking;
- early/late slot options.

Express and Vite ran as separate processes, and Playwright used `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium`. Page clocks were pinned wherever "today" matters. The database, role, `.env`, probes and recordings were deleted afterwards.

| Area | Checks | Covers |
|---|---:|---|
| Calendar + booking | 36/36 | FE-F10-3, -4, -5, -6, -8 (server `PAST_SLOT`, id payload, past drop sends nothing, day move sends WhatsApp), -9, -11, -12, -13 (Assistant 400, `/api/doctors` shape, "(current)", booking list), -14, -15a/c/d, -17, -20; `/api/calendar/stats` gone |
| Daily board | 15/15 | FE-F11-12 (3 writes → 1 read, no dim/skeletons, Refresh reads once, filter reads nothing), -8a/b/c, -11a/b/e, the Arabic ص/م marker |
| Videos, TV tab, kiosk | 14/14 | FE-F11-13 (ModalHeader, stream cancelled on close with 0 bytes after, unsaved guard, upload abort, delete), -14 (upload and delete keep the pending order, seconds box), -15a/b/c/d (server down: probes at 15/30 s and stays; server back: one reload, reconnected) |

Probe mistakes corrected along the way:
- Headless Chromium runs no native drag session, so the drags were dispatched as synthetic HTML5 events.
- `/calendar` had been opened by `goto` with no history, which correctly takes the pasted-link fallback.
- The playlist-row delete button had been mis-selected.
- A kiosk wait was too short.

**One real defect was found by runtime and fixed before commit**: the first version of the FE-F11-13 player fix emptied a prop-driven `src` on cleanup, and StrictMode's setup → cleanup → setup left the dev player blank.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)

- **An unknown `/api/*` path answers 200 with the SPA shell**, not a JSON 404 (seen when probing the removed `/api/calendar/stats`). This is pre-existing, from the web router's catch-all. File with a backend/routing pass.
- **Vite's dev proxy keeps a proxied SSE response open when Express dies**, so in dev the board and kiosk sit on "Live" until the silence watchdog fires. This is dev-only: Caddy/the tunnel answer with a 502, which is the path verified above.
- **The WhatsApp confirmation's partial-failure warning shows the server's `message` verbatim** (English on the Arabic form). File with **F16**.

## RC1 · 2026-09-29 — Phase C's three 🟠, the owner rules, and F11's 🟡

The first Phase C slice: all three 🟠 (FE-F10-1, FE-F10-2, FE-F11-1), the four findings that needed an owner decision, and every F11 🟡. Along the way it took three older findings the work passed through (FE-F1-7, FE-F3-8, FE-F2-8's appointments half). **15 fixed, 10 partial**; each partial row names what is left. **Gate green**: 0 errors and F0's 4 warnings; vitest **36 files / 291 tests** (+2 files, +12 tests); contracts D1 0 / D2 8; both typechecks and the build clean. **38 of 38 runtime checks** passed on a throwaway database (below).

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RC1 (2026-09-29)

| Finding | Decision |
|---|---|
| FE-F10-2 | **Build the grid from the appointments.** Days come from the date range (Sat–Thu, holidays marked). Rows are the configured times, plus any time that has an appointment. The `calendar` table stays only for the booking picker's availability, and Regenerate stops deleting past rows. |
| FE-F10-7 | **Enforce `MaxAppointmentsPerSlot` per clinic slot**, on the server, under a lock in the booking's transaction. |
| FE-F11-4 | **Refuse future days, allow past days.** A future day's check-in, seat and dismiss are refused by the server and not offered by the board. Past days stay open for corrections, and undo is never blocked. |
| FE-F11-16 | **Default `VideosPath` under `MACHINE_PATH`** (`clinic1/ovideos/`) when the row is missing, and make it editable in Settings. |

### What changed, per finding

- **FE-F11-1 🟠 — the stamps.** `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized` now sends the three stamps as 24-hour `HH:MM:SS` (trimmed; the columns are plain `time`, which legacy rows may carry with fractions). `apptime` is sent as 24-hour `HH:MM`. `AppointmentCard.formatTime` turns both into `h:mm` plus the active language's marker. The contract now declares every field the card reads, with a strict 24-hour `clockTime`, so a regression to 12-hour fails loud. The same bug had already been fixed once for the appointment PDF (the comment in `appointment-queries.ts#getAppointmentsWithPhones`).
- **FE-F10-1 🟠 — Edit from the calendar.** `EditAppointmentForm` no longer reads router state. It always reads `appointmentByIdQuery` (`refetchOnMount: 'always'`) and seeds from the contract type, so both the Back/Forward re-seed and the `ExistingAppointment` cast are gone. `CalendarContextMenu` and `PatientAppointments` stop passing state. `appointmentById.response` now declares the fields the form seeds from, and the server's `AppointmentSummaryRow` is made honestly nullable (`app_detail`, `dr_id`). The dead `app_date`/`person_id` aliases went from `CalendarViewService`, `calendar.contract` and `calendar.types`. A save also invalidates `qk.appointments.all()`.
- **FE-F10-2 🟠 — the grid source.** New `getAppointmentsInRange` (one range scan of `ix_tblappointments_appdate_optimized`), plus `buildGridDays` for `/range` and `buildMonthDays` for `/month` in `CalendarViewService`:
  - **Columns.** Every working day of the span is a column, past ones included.
  - **Rows.** The visible configured times plus every time that has an appointment, so a walk-in at 15:07, a booking in a hidden early/late row, and one at a deleted time all render.
  - **`full`.** A slot is `full` against the clinic-wide count, even when the grid is filtered to one doctor.
  - **Stats.** The week stats come from the configured rows of the non-holiday days and follow the doctor filter.
  - **Regenerate.** `fillCalendar` no longer deletes past rows.
  - **The horizon.** The hourly `noteCalendarRange` check now **extends** the slot table when it runs short, instead of only logging. The fill is additive and idempotent.
  - **Removed.** `/api/calendar/week` and `calendar.contract#week` had no caller (FE-F10-19's server half); the route table diff is below.
  - **Tests.** Seven new unit tests (`CalendarViewService.test.ts`) pin the four ways an appointment used to drop out, the clinic-wide `full` rule, holidays in the stats, and month ends.
- **FE-F10-7 — the slot limit.** `assertSlotHasRoom` takes `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('appointment-slot:' || ts::text))`, counts the slot and refuses with `SLOT_FULL`, inside the same transaction as the insert. `createAppointment` now accepts the caller's transaction. On update, only a move into **another** slot is checked, so an appointment in a slot that is already over the limit stays editable. Walk-ins (arrival minute) are exempt. On the client, the new shared `bookingError()` maps `HOLIDAY_CONFLICT` / `APPOINTMENT_CONFLICT` / `SLOT_FULL` for both booking forms. The edit form had no mapping before (FE-F10-17's first item). A `SLOT_FULL` refusal also refreshes the picker's cached slots. The calendar drag shows the server's English message on the English calendar.
- **FE-F11-4 — other days.** `updatePresent` refuses a forward step when `app_day` is after today (`FUTURE_APPOINTMENT`, 400). `undoAppointmentState` is untouched. The board withholds check-in, seat and dismiss on a future day, and the hook toasts the translated refusal if one gets through.
- **FE-F11-16 — the videos folder.** `defaultVideosPath()` (`clinic-paths.ts`), and `getVideosPath()` returns the option or that default. The two video reads bind the path instead of `CROSS JOIN`ing an options row that might not exist; that join made the list empty on a fresh install. The upload creates the folder, and it no longer caches the path for the life of the process. `db:setup` gains a **Videos folder** step that writes the row when it is absent, so it appears in Settings → General.
- **FE-F11-2 — action vs. read errors.** A failed state change is now a translated toast and never becomes the page's `error`. A failed read keeps the header and shows a translated message with **Retry**. The four `console.error` catches and the English fallbacks are gone (FE-F11-11(d)).
- **FE-F11-3 + FE-F11-7 + FE-F1-7 — the SSE channel.** Both singletons are now instances of one `SseChannel` (`services/sse-channel.ts`). The channel retries a CLOSED stream with backoff (`CLOSED_STREAM_RETRY_DELAYS_MS`) while anyone holds it. It also reopens an OPEN stream that has delivered nothing for `SILENT_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS` (60 s). Both server broadcasters now send a named `event: ping` every 25 s in place of the invisible `:\n\n` comment. The chair kiosk runs the same silence check on its own EventSource. Four unit tests (`sse-channel.test.ts`) use a fake EventSource and fake timers. `docs/whatsapp-integration.md` and CLAUDE.md now describe the ping and the channel.
- **FE-F11-5 — TV uploads.** New `timeouts.upload` (10 min, `TIMEOUTS.UPLOAD`) on `POST /api/tv-display/media`, and the client's `postFormData` uses the same 10-minute budget.
- **FE-F11-6 — identity.**
  - **Kiosk.** The welcome line and the printed QR footer read `CLINIC_NAME` through `brandingQuery`. With no name set, the kiosk says just "Welcome".
  - **TV page.** The public TV page's `<title>` and placeholder are filled per request from `CLINIC_NAME`, HTML-escaped.
  - **TV settings.** The TV tab drops the daemon path and the source-file list, and points IT to `docs/tv-display.md`.
  - **QR address.** `config.urls.publicUrlIsDefault` flows through the QR endpoint (`usesDefaultAddress`, contracted), and the share modal warns when the link is the built-in domain.
- **FE-F11-17 + FE-F10-10 + FE-F2-8 — keys and invalidation.**
  - **Keys.** `qk.appointments` has a real root: `all()`, with `daily` = `['appointments','daily',d]`. `keys.test.ts` pins the prefix.
  - **Loader.** `dailyAppointmentsLoader` now `fetchQuery`s into the cache (`staleTime: 0`) instead of returning `initialData`, which React Query ignored for a cached key.
  - **Writes.** The two quick check-ins, both booking forms, the patient-list delete and the calendar's reschedule/delete all invalidate `qk.appointments.all()`. The calendar's two also invalidate the patient.
  - **Read factory.** There is a new `dailyAppointmentsQuery` factory, so `useAppointments` no longer defines a key outside `queries.ts`.
- **FE-F3-8 + FE-F11-10 — casts.** The daily board, its hook and its loader use the contract's `DailyAppointmentRow` / `DailyAppointmentStats`. Three hand-written row types, two `AppointmentStats`, `useLoaderData() as LoaderData`, four array casts and two status casts are gone. `Videos` uses `VideoRow` and the contracted QR type.
- **FE-F11-9 (partial).** `getCurrentStatus`, `isWaiting`, `data-status`, `.waiting`, `isCheckedIn`, `getStats`, the English `emptyMessage` default, the `actions.checkedIn` and `errors.failedToLoad` strings and two stale comments are gone. CLAUDE.md's appointments note now describes the 24-hour times instead of selectors that never existed.

The route table diff, reviewed before `vitest -u`:

```
~ POST    /api/tv-display/media  [timeout(600000ms) -> uploadMediaFiles]
- GET     /api/calendar/week  [validate(query)]
```

No migration and no Supabase DDL. The new `options` row is written by `db:setup` only, and only when it is absent.

### Runtime verification — 38 of 38 checks passed

Same recipe as F11: a throwaway PostgreSQL 16 database (`db:migrate` → `db:setup` as "Bright Smile Orthodontics" → `db:seed:demo`), scratch `MACHINE_PATH` and TV folders, sinks and WhatsApp off, and Express and Vite as separate processes. The fixtures were the evening appointments and the 19:52 waiting patient again, a walk-in at 15:07:23, and bookings on Sunday 4 Oct at a hidden late slot (20:30) and at a non-configured time (18:15). The late slots were configured with extended rows hidden. The probes, `.env`, the database, the role and the scratch data were removed afterwards, and no screenshots were taken. Five first-run failures were probe mistakes, rerun and passed:
- the Edit probe clicked a past appointment, which the calendar never offers to edit;
- the four "concurrent" bookings fetched four CSRF tokens that invalidated each other;
- `/tv-display` was fetched through Vite, which does not proxy it.

| Area | Checks |
|---|---|
| Stamps (FE-F11-1) | 19:52 → "7:52 PM", booking "7:30 PM"; the walk-in reads "3:07 PM" twice; no afternoon stamp reads AM across the demo day (10:25–19:52); Arabic "7:52 م" / "7:30 م"; 0 `data-status` attributes |
| Errors (FE-F11-2) | an aborted check-in → toast, header and 10 cards stay; a 500 on the next day's read → header + "Could not load the appointments." + Retry, and Retry recovers |
| Other days (FE-F11-4) | Thu 1 Oct shows no check-in buttons; the API refuses 400 `FUTURE_APPOINTMENT`; undo on it → 200; a past-day check-in (Sep 28, appointment 249) → 200, stamped |
| Walk-in cache (FE-F11-17) | board → Search → quick check-in → Appointments: the walk-in is on the board |
| SSE (FE-F11-3/-7) | Express killed under an open board → 502 → "Connection Error"; after the restart the board went back to **Live on its own** (responses `200, 502, 502, 200`) and received another desk's check-in. The streams were frozen by a TCP proxy (no FIN/RST) after the check-in and a kiosk patient load: **board and kiosk both caught up at ~75 s** |
| Grid (FE-F10-2) | 26 Sep – 1 Oct renders 6 columns, past days with their appointments; the 15:07 row holds the walk-in (API and DOM); 4 Oct renders the booked 20:30 row but not the empty 20:00, and the 18:15 booking; the month view counts the walk-in; `month?date=2026-10-31` ends Oct 31 and its grid ends Thu Nov 5 |
| Edit (FE-F10-1) | calendar → Edit Appointment: the form's Doctor and Type equal the opened appointment's own (#71: 3 / Follow Up) |
| Slot limit (FE-F10-7) | 4 concurrent bookings into a max-3 slot → `200, 200, 200, 400 SLOT_FULL`; moving another appointment in → 400 `SLOT_FULL {booked: 3}`; a doctor change inside the full slot → 200; the edit form shows "This time slot is already full. Please pick another time." |
| Videos (FE-F11-16/-6) | with the `VideosPath` row deleted, an upload lands in `machine/clinic1/ovideos/`; the QR modal warns about the built-in address; the printed footer is "Bright Smile Orthodontics" |
| TV (FE-F11-6/-5) | the public page's title and placeholder are the clinic name with no "Shwan"; the tab no longer shows `lgtv-scheduler`; a 6 MB clip at 160 KB/s (~41 s) completes |
| Kiosk (FE-F11-6) | "Welcome to Bright Smile Orthodontics" |

### Left for RC2

F10's other 🟡 (FE-F10-3, -4, -8, -9, -11) and F10's and F11's 🔵. Among them: FE-F11-8 (the midnight roll-forward decided in F11), FE-F11-12 (the refetch dimming and the stale "coalesced" comment), FE-F11-13/-14/-15, and the partial rows above.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)

- **The patient-portal link uses the same `publicUrl` fallback** (`PatientPortalService.ts`), so an install without `PUBLIC_URL` hands patients another clinic's address. `config.urls.publicUrlIsDefault` now exists. File with **F23**.
- **The server boot banner prints `local.shwan-orthodontics.com`** (index.ts) on every install. Same class as F11's Caddyfile note; file with F26 or a deployment pass.

## RB3 · 2026-09-29 — F9's three 🟠, then the rest of F8 + F9

The session the board queued after RB2: F9's three 🟠 first, then every other F8 and F9 finding that did not need an owner decision. **24 fixed, 3 partial** (FE-F9-11, FE-F9-12, FE-F8-13 — each names what is left; FE-F9-11 was completed by the same-day follow-up at the end of this section, so the ledger reads **25 fixed, 2 partial**), plus RB2's *found along the way* (a failed background refetch closed an open expense edit) and F9's server-side *found along the way* (the photo roll-up ignored a corrected visit date). Runtime testing found **one more defect in FE-F9-4's class**, fixed here too (see FE-F9-4 below). **`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, only F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (now at 2285/2309: RB3 added 10 lines above them), **33 files / 276 tests** (7 files and 47 tests up on RB2), contracts **D1 0 / D2 8**. The D2 baseline was ratcheted from 9 to 8 because `media.patientLink` is now modelled. Build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed by exactly these lines**, and the diff was accepted:

```
- USE     /clinic-assets  [serveStatic]
+ GET     /api/patients/:personId/xray/preview  [validate(params,query)]
+ GET     /api/patients/:personId/xray/send-path  [validate(params,query)]
- GET     /api/getworkforreceipt/:workId  [authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical) -> validate(params)]
- GET     /api/expenses/summary  [validate(query)]
~ POST    /api/webceph/upload-image       [timeout(255000ms) -> uploadImage -> validate(body)]
~ POST    /api/webceph/upload-from-file   [timeout(255000ms) -> validate(body)]
```

No migration, no schema change, no Supabase DDL. The three new server paths are two GETs and one request timeout. The `/clinic-assets` removal takes away a static mount of the **whole** clinic volume that any logged-in user could read. Its only consumer, the X-ray card, never rendered through it.

### What changed, per finding

**F9's three 🟠**
- **FE-F9-1**: failed diagnosis read.
  - `diagnosisQuery` no longer catches: only the route's `200 null` means "none yet", and a failure throws (RQ's transient retry applies).
  - `Diagnosis` is split into a thin route component and a `DiagnosisEditor` keyed by `workId`. The editor reads with `refetchOnMount: 'always'` and seeds **once, from the read this mount made** (`isFetchedAfterMount && isSuccess && !isFetching`). Until then there is no form, so there is nothing to Save.
  - A failed read shows an error panel with the server's message, **Retry**, and *Back to works*, and says why the form stays closed.
  - The works list no longer gates the page (it only names the work in the delete confirm).
- **FE-F9-2**: stale visit edit.
  - The visit form reads `visitByIdQuery` with `refetchOnMount: 'always'` and seeds only from the post-mount read. `ContentRenderer` keys it by `workId:visitId`.
  - A save marks `qk.visit.byId(visitId)` stale (`refetchType: 'none'`, since the form is leaving). A delete removes it.
- **FE-F9-3**: X-ray *Send*.
  - New `GET /api/patients/:personId/xray/send-path` (contract `patient.xraySendPath`, charset-validated `file`/`detailsDir`). It runs the viewer's own `processXrayImage` and returns the image's server path, the way `/api/convert-path` serves the photo senders.
  - The card opens a blank tab **inside the click** (so a slow render can't trip the popup blocker), fetches the path, then points the tab at `/send-message?file=<path>`. On failure it closes the tab and shows a toast.

**The rest of F9**
- **FE-F9-4**: the photo flags' roll-up into the work.
  - *Client:* every visit save and delete also invalidates `qk.patient.all`. The three lifecycle boxes carry inline hints ("marks the treatment Finished", "sets the treatment's initial-photo / debond date").
  - *Client:* a save that finishes the work (Final Photo newly ticked) or reopens it (un-ticked) asks first: *Finish treatment?* / *Reopen treatment?*. Deleting the Final Photo visit says it reopens the treatment.
  - *Server* (`visit.routes.ts#visitRollupConflict`): these failures now return worded **409**s instead of a generic 500:
    - `ck_works` / `ck_works_deb` / `ck_works_debiph` → the photo-date ordering rule;
    - `unq_tblwork_active` → "another active treatment";
    - **new in RB3**: `photof_index` / `photo_index`, the partial unique indexes allowing **one** Final Photo and **one** Initial Photo visit per work. Runtime found this state reachable from the UI: finish through a Final Photo visit, *Reactivate* on the Works card, then tick Final Photo on a later visit. That returned "Failed to add visit" (500).
  - `utils/pg-errors.ts` exports `isCheckViolation`.
  - F9's server note is fixed too: `applyPhotoUpdate` now moves the work's `i_photo_date` / `f_photo_date` / `debond_date` when a **still-set** flag's visit date is corrected.
- **FE-F9-5**: X-ray thumbnails.
  - New `GET /api/patients/:personId/xray/preview?detailsDir=` sends `…/<detailsDir>/t.png` with `dotfiles: 'allow'`, scoped to that one fixed, charset-validated path (`patient-assets.service#xrayPreviewPath`).
  - The placeholder is conditional rendering (a `brokenPreviews` set), not the global `hidden` class that lost the cascade.
  - Dev now shows thumbnails without a Vite proxy change, because `/api` is already proxied.
- **FE-F9-6**: new `utils/toothNotation.ts#unnToPalmer` (tested). The 3D Scans tab prints 3Shape's UNN numbers in the app's Palmer notation ("Bridge UL6–UL8 · Crown UR6") with the raw UNN in the tooltip. The Diagnosis placeholder `#24` became `UL4`.
- **FE-F9-7**: new `hooks/useUnsavedRouteGuard.ts`, the page-sized sibling of `<Modal unsavedGuard>`.
  - `useBlocker` plus the shared `useConfirm` with the `common:unsaved.*` strings, and `beforeunload` for reload and close. A same-path/query navigation is never blocked.
  - `allowNextNavigation()` lets the page's own post-save redirect through.
  - It is wired into `Diagnosis` and `NewVisitComponent`. Cancel, Back, the sidebar and the header tabs now ask.
  - The 500 ms post-save timer is gone: the page leaves immediately, and its buttons stay disabled until it unmounts.
- **FE-F9-8**: the visit screens' edges.
  - `/visits` without `workId` explains that visits are per treatment and links to Works (no empty list, no `workId=null` Add).
  - Add and Edit opened from Visit History carry `from=visits` and return there.
  - A failed list read has a Retry instead of a × that cleared nothing.
  - An edit of a missing visit shows the error and no form. **Server:** `updateVisitByWorkId` returns `success: false` for a missing row, and the route answers **404** instead of a success over 0 rows.
- **FE-F9-9**: the WebCeph modal.
  - It is pinned `dir="ltr"` through a `display: contents` wrapper, so it stays LTR inside the Arabic patient-info route.
  - A pending link read shows *Checking…*, and a failed one shows the error with Retry. Only a real 404 shows the Create card.
  - One success-banner timer at a time, cleared on unmount. The file input is reset through a ref.
- **FE-F9-10**: `formatXrayDate` reads DICOM `YYYYMMDD` and anything else through `formatDate`. It never parses the file name, and an unreadable value is "Unknown Date". This also removes the card from FE-F3-3's browser-locale census. **The `YYYYMMDD` format is still the DICOM assumption.** F9's query for the owner stands: check one real `meta` file.
- **FE-F9-11 (partial)**: the upload timeout budget.
  - `media.contract#WEBCEPH_UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 255 s` is derived in its comment from the service's worst case (2 calls × 3 attempts × 20 s / 60 s caps, plus back-off, = 246 s).
  - It is applied as the two upload routes' `requestTimeout` and as the client's `timeoutMs`, and `webceph-service.ts` points at it.
  - **Not done in the first pass:** `race: 'Asian'` remained hard-coded. **Done in the follow-up below.**
- **FE-F9-12 (partial)**: a11y.
  - `DentalChart` is **one** tab stop with a roving tabindex (←/→ along an arch, ↑/↓ between arches, Home/End, Enter/Space). The 30 slots are named ("Between UR1 and UL1"). Teeth are named by `aria-label`, and their images are decorative.
  - Both notes labels are associated with their textareas. The error ×s have `aria-label`s.
  - Both forms' tab strips have `tablist` / `tab` / `aria-selected` / `tabpanel`.
  - **Not done:** primary teeth on the visit chart (`TeethSelector` has them). There are no primary-tooth chart images, so this needs a UX decision, not a bug fix.
- **FE-F9-13**: hand-written types over contracts.
  - Diagnosis types are derived from `workContract.DiagnosisBody`, and `diagnosis-fields.test.ts` fails if a contract field has no row.
  - `VisitFormData` is `Required<Omit<AddVisitBody, …>>` plus the three select ids.
  - `LatestWires` and the `as PhotoType[]` cast are gone.
  - The patient-info contract models `xrays` (`patient.contract#xrayRow`; `XrayInfo` became a `type` for `sendData`), so the `as Xray[]` cast is gone.
  - `media.createPatient` / `uploadImage` / `uploadFromFile` / `patientLink` responses model what the modal reads (`patientLink` was a D2 `z.unknown()`). The create route falls back to the submitted id when WebCeph omits it, so no link row is stored without one.
- **FE-F9-14**: altitude and polish.
  - `Diagnosis` went from 957 lines to 343 plus `diagnosis-fields.ts`, a config of 44 fields across 5 tabs.
  - `TeethSelector` lost `React.memo`, 7 `useMemo`s and 3 `useCallback`s. `NewVisitComponent` lost its 6 `useCallback`s.
  - The 3D Scans downloads carry `download`, so a failure does not navigate the SPA tab. The not-connected notice sends only admins to the admin-only Integrations tab and asks everyone else to contact an administrator.
- **FE-F9-15**: `ContentRenderer`'s unreachable `case 'work'` diagnosis branch and `workIdFromPath` are deleted, and the stale comments are fixed.

**F8**
