### Runtime verification — 53 of 53 checks passed

**Not on the owner's dev box.** This ran in a cloud container, like F9, against a **throwaway** PostgreSQL 16.
- The database was installed with plain `npm run db:migrate` on an empty database (the fresh-install path now works), then `npm run db:setup -- --yes …` and `npm run db:seed:demo`: 20 fictional patients.

- The probe (`scripts/e2e/.rb3-probe.tmp.mjs`) launched Chromium through `executablePath: '/opt/pw-browsers/chromium'`. It was deleted afterwards, along with its session file and the temporary `.env`. No screenshots. The database and the dev servers were stopped.
- **Real writes to the throwaway DB** where the write was the point: the diagnosis save, the visit edits, the Final Photo adds, one 10,000 IQD payment, and one expense edit that the probe's first draft sent unmocked.
- **Mocked or aborted:** the diagnosis GET → 500, the WebCeph link → 500, the 3Shape lists, the exchange rate → 404, one expense PUT → 400, and the payment POST in the amount checks.
- The one `sendmedia2` call used `prog=Telegram` with no Telegram configured. It passed the path guard and failed at "session not configured", before any send.

Checks, grouped:

### Follow-up · 2026-09-29 — the WebCeph race (owner decision, completes FE-F9-11)

The owner's call: **"we are in Iraq — change the race accordingly, and make it clear that this race is chosen by default."**
- **The value.** WebCeph offers four norm sets: african / asian / caucasian / hispanic. Cephalometric norm sets group Middle Eastern populations (Arab and Kurdish) with the **Caucasian** norms. WebCeph's "Asian" norms are East Asian. So every patient created until now was analysed against the wrong norms.
- **One definition.** New `shared/webceph-race.ts` holds `WEBCEPH_RACES`, `WEBCEPH_DEFAULT_RACE = 'caucasian'` (with the reasoning in its comment) and the labels. The media contract's `race` is now `z.enum(WEBCEPH_RACES)` instead of any string. `webceph-service` falls back to the shared default (it was `'asian'`) and validates against the shared list. `docs/WEBCEPH_SETUP.md` says the same.
- **Visible, not assumed.** The WebCeph dialog's *Create* card has a **Race (cephalometric norms)** select. It is pre-selected to *Caucasian (default)*, and a hint under it reads "**Caucasian is chosen by default** — the norm set used for Middle Eastern (Iraqi) patients. Change it only for a patient of another background." If staff pick another value, the hint changes to "Changed from the default (Caucasian) for this patient". Either way it adds that the race is sent once, at creation.
- **Test.** `shared/webceph-race.test.ts` (3 tests) pins the default, the four values and the contract's refusal of anything else. The gate is green: 0 errors, F0's 4 warnings, 34 files / 279 tests, D1 0 / D2 8, no route-table diff.
- **Runtime, 8/8**, on the same throwaway demo database, with both create requests aborted so nothing reached WebCeph:
  - The select opens at `caucasian` / "Caucasian (default)", the hint shows, and all four options are offered.
  - *Create* sends `race: "caucasian"`.
  - Picking Asian shows the "changed" hint and sends `asian`.
  - `POST /api/webceph/create-patient` with `race: "Asian"` → 400 at the contract.
  - No page errors.
  - The probe, the temporary `.env`, dev and the database were removed or stopped afterwards.
- **Per-deployment note.** For a center outside the region, the default is the one constant in `shared/webceph-race.ts`, and the per-patient select already overrides it. If the product is sold outside the Middle East, the default should become a Settings option the way `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY` is. That was not built here.
- **Patients already in WebCeph keep the race they were created with.** The app does not send race again after creation. Correcting existing ones would be done in WebCeph itself.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)
- **Font Awesome icons are in accessible names** wherever the `<i>` lacks `aria-hidden` (e.g. the visit form's *Update* reads as " Update"). RB3 added `aria-hidden` on the screens it rewrote. The rest is **F25**'s sweep.
- **`WorkComponent` status changes (Reactivate) do not check for a Final Photo visit.** Reactivating a work finished by a visit leaves that visit's `f_photo` set, so the work reads Active with a final-photo visit on file, and a later Final Photo tick hits `photof_index` (now worded). Whether Reactivate should also clear the flag, or warn, is an owner call. **F7's surface.**
- **The set cost of an aligner set on a non-USD work** can still be entered (only the payment is refused now). **F17**, with FE-F8-6's note.

## RB2 · 2026-09-28 — F8's two 🟠

At the owner's request, the two 🟠 F8 filed were fixed straight after the audit, and the session then stopped. Nothing else in F8 was touched. **2 fixed.** **`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, and the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones. 229/229 tests in 26 files. Contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline. Build green. **No route-table diff** (client-only change). Not committed.

### What changed
- **FE-F8-1** — `ExpenseSummary` now totals **the rows the table shows**: `expenses` + `loading` come from the page's own `useExpenses(filters)`, count = rows, IQD and USD summed separately. The currency match is trimmed and case-insensitive, like the `citext` column (live values are exactly `IQD`/`USD`). The date-only read it used to prefer is gone from the client, along with everything that existed only for it: `useExpenseSummary`, the phantom `ExpenseSummary` interface (it modelled `totalExpenses / byCategory / byCurrency`, fields the endpoint never sent), `expenseSummaryQuery` and `qk.expenses.summary`. The list is never paginated (`expensesQuery` sends no `limit`), so the sum is exact. The server route stays for now (see below).
- **FE-F8-2** — `routes/Expenses.tsx` **derives** the expense being edited instead of seeding it into state: `currentExpense = editingId != null ? editingExpense ?? null : null`. `currentExpense`/`seededExpense` and their setters are deleted. Update vs create is decided by `editingId`, not by whether a row happens to be loaded. The modal opens for an edit only once its row is present (`expenseModalOpen`); a cached row is instant. That closes the second half of the finding too: before, the form rendered as an empty *Add* form while the row loaded, and a Save in that window would have created.

### Runtime verification — 11 of 11 checks passed
Dev `:5273` was started with the three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env**. Every non-GET was **aborted**. One read was **delayed** 1.2 s (`GET /api/expenses/:id`) to exercise the cold open. No screenshots. The probe (`scripts/e2e/.rb2-probe.tmp.mjs`) was deleted afterwards.
- **FE-F8-1:** the cards equal the table's own count and per-currency sums **unfiltered (83 rows)**, with **category 1 (33 rows / 151,750 IQD — the cards read 3,001,750 before the fix)**, with **currency USD (2 / 200 USD)** and with **category 5 (12 rows)**.
- **FE-F8-2:**
  - A cold edit renders **no modal** while its row loads, then opens as *Edit Expense* with 60,000.
  - Edit → Cancel → Edit the same row opens *Edit Expense* with 60,000. Its Save sends **`PUT /api/expenses/4759`** and no POST.
  - A → B → A each opens its own row (60,000 / 430,000 / 60,000).
  - *Add New Expense* still opens empty, and its Save sends `POST /api/expenses`.
- One probe check first reported a failure that was the probe's own: Playwright's `innerText()` waits for the element, so the "400 ms" read had silently waited out the delayed fetch. An instrumented rerun showed the title appears only after the response (≈ 4.6 s into the run, 1.5 s after the click). The check now uses `count()`, which does not wait.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)
- **`GET /api/expenses/summary` has no consumer any more.** That covers the route in `expense.routes.ts`, `getExpenseSummary` + `getExpenseTotalsByCurrency` and `expense.contract#expenseSummary`. It is recorded on **FE-F8-14** (DEAD). Removing it is a route-table diff, so it belongs to that cleanup, not to a 🟠 fix. **✅ Removed in RB3.**
- **A failed background refetch closes an open edit.** `Expenses.tsx`'s error branch (`editingId != null && editingError`) does not check whether the row already loaded. So if the by-id query refetches (window focus, stale) while the modal is open, and that refetch fails, the modal closes and the typing is lost. This predates RB2, and it takes a network or 5xx failure mid-edit. Gate it on `!editingExpense` when F8's remaining findings get their RB slice. **✅ Fixed in RB3.**

## RB1 · 2026-09-28 — every open Phase B 🟠, plus the owner-decided 🟡s

RB was started ahead of F8/F9 at the owner's request. RB1 = every open Phase B 🟠 plus the 🟡s the owner decided (RA1's precedent); F8/F9 findings get a later RB slice. The work ran over three sessions: the first wrote the code and stopped before checking any of it, the second reviewed it, fixed what the review found, gated it and runtime-verified it, and the third closed the last runtime check, applied the migration and closed the ledger. **10 fixed** — all six 🟠 (FE-F6-1, FE-F6-2, FE-F7-1, FE-F7-2, FE-F7-3 and **FE-F7-19**, which was found and fixed in this session) and four 🟡 (FE-F6-6, FE-F7-4, FE-F7-5, FE-F7-7). **`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, and the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones. 229/229 tests in 26 files, 11 of them new (see *Added in the second session*). Contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline. Build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed exactly as expected** and was accepted: `+ GET /api/work-doctors`, and `addworkdetail` / `updateworkdetail` / `deleteworkdetail` moved from `authorize(admin|front_desk)` to `authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical)`. Migration `1789460300000_default-work-currency.sql` was **applied on 2026-09-28** with the owner's go-ahead: `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY = IQD` locally, and CDC carried the row to the mirror. Nothing was committed. The tree also still carries the **uncommitted RA1–RA5 + FE-F5-1 work**, so do not commit without asking.

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RB1 (2026-09-28)
| finding | decision |
|---|---|
| FE-F6-1 | **persist** the Add form's Alerts box as one context alert in the create transaction |
| FE-F6-2 | codebook **0 Arabic · 1 English · 2 Kurdish** (the senders' + legacy data's meaning; Kurdish gets the Arabic body) — no data change needed: the 3 live `1`s are English, the one `2` was set while 2 meant Kurdish |
| FE-F7-3 | new works default from a **clinic setting** (`DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY` option, Settings → General); unset = no default, the select starts empty + required |
| FE-F7-1 / -5 / -7 | as already recorded in the F7 ledger (Doctor ∪ commission + own doctor; remove EUR from works; doctors/assistants write items, `item_cost` finance-only) |

### What changed, per finding (written in the first session, reviewed in the second)
- **FE-F7-3** — `NewWorkComponent`: the `> 10,000 → IQD` keystroke heuristic removed; initial currency `''`, seeded render-phase from `optionQuery(DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY_OPTION)` for a NEW work only (never over a user choice); edit mode seeds `work.currency ?? ''`; currency select is `required`, lists `WORK_CURRENCIES`, and is **disabled with a "Locked" hint when the work has payments**. Server: `WorkService.validateAndUpdateWork` gained the **currency lock** (`CURRENCY_LOCKED` 400 when the currency changes and `TotalPaid > 0`, for admins too, before the role gate; `needsCurrentWork` now also triggers on a present `currency`). `getDefaultWorkCurrency()` exported from `WorkService`. New **migration `migrations/pg/1789460300000_default-work-currency.sql`** — seeds the option from the majority currency of existing works (dry-run SELECT on the live DB → `IQD`; a fresh deployment gets `''`). **Applied 2026-09-28** in the third session, with the owner's go-ahead. It seeded `IQD`; the change is row data only, CDC replicated it, and there is no Supabase DDL. On a deployment where the option is unset, the form has no default (the 404 path of `optionQuery` → null). `GeneralSettings`: the option renders as a closed select (Not set / IQD / USD), and a save now also invalidates `qk.settings.option(name)` for each saved option (the single-option key is a *sibling* of `settings.options()`, so the work form would otherwise keep a stale default).
- **FE-F7-19 (new, 🟠, found here)** — clinical add-work always violated `ck_works_cur`; `validateAndCreateWork` now resolves the currency (clinical → clinic default; finance → chosen, else default; neither → `MISSING_CURRENCY` 400 with a role-specific message).
- **FE-F7-5** — new **`shared/work-currency.ts`** (`WORK_CURRENCIES = ['IQD','USD']`, `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY_OPTION`, `parseWorkCurrency`). `work.contract.ts`: `optCurrency` (enum, ''/null → absent) on `workCreateFields` + `updateWork`; `addWorkWithInvoice.currency: z.enum(WORK_CURRENCIES)`. **Also the patient intake** (`patient.contract.ts` `intakeSchema`, both arms) — it creates a work + invoice, and accepted EUR. `work-queries.insertFullPaymentInvoice`: EUR arm removed, and any non-USD/IQD currency now **throws** instead of booking cash into neither column. EUR stays on the patient estimate and cost presets (display-only).
- **FE-F7-2** — `workItemFields.implant_length/diameter` → new `optMillimetres` (non-negative, ≤ 999.99, decimals allowed).
- **FE-F7-4** — `updateWork.body` gains `estimated_duration: optInt`; `discount: z.union([z.null(), moneyInt])` (null first). Verified with a tsx probe that Zod 4 keeps a present key whose value parses to `undefined` (so presence-keyed `updateWork()` still clears a blanked field).
- **FE-F7-1** — new `employee-queries#listWorkDoctors()` (active AND (position 'Doctor' OR `percentage`)), route **`GET /api/work-doctors`** in `staff.routes.ts`, `staff.contract.workDoctors`, `qk.lookups.workDoctors()`, `workDoctorsQuery()`. `NewWorkComponent` uses it and appends the work's own doctor as `"<name> (current)"` when missing (state `currentDoctor`, set in the edit seed); the select got `name="dr_id"`.
- **FE-F7-7 + FE-F6-6** — `shared/auth/roles.ts#RoleCapabilities` gains **`editRecords`** (admin + front desk — work edit/lifecycle/delete, patient edit/delete/estimated cost) and **`adminWrites`** (admin — transfer, direct discounts). Server: `work-item.routes.ts` add/update/delete → **`authorize(CLINICAL_ROLES)`**, with `withoutClinicalCost()` dropping `item_cost` from a clinical body; `work-item-queries.updateWorkDetail` writes `item_cost` **only when the key is present** (so a doctor's edit keeps the front desk's cost). Client: `WorkCard` props `isAdmin` → `canTransfer`, new `editRecords` (fail-closed) gating Edit / Complete / Discontinue / Reactivate / Delete, ⋮ hidden when empty; `WorkComponent` passes caps, hides the payment-history actions column + "Add New Payment" behind `writeFinance`, `colSpan` fixed (was 6 on a 4-col table); `WorkDetailItem` hides the Item Cost input behind `writeFinance`; `NewWorkComponent` — edit-mode guard (`workId && user && !caps.editRecords` → lock message), the duplicate-work dialog hides "Finish & Add New" for clinical, `isAdmin` → `caps.adminWrites`. Patients: `EditPatientComponent` guard (`edit.notPermitted`, EN+AR added), `ViewPatientInfo` hides Edit Patient + the cost pencil, `PatientManagement` hides the row Edit/Delete icons — all on `caps.editRecords`.
- **FE-F6-1** — `createPatient.body.alerts: z.string().optional()`; route passes `alerts?.trim()` as a third arg to `PatientService.createPatientWithIntake`, which inserts one context alert (severity 2 = Moderate, type NULL) **inside the create transaction** via `createAlert(input, trx)` (`createAlert` gained an optional executor). `AddPatientForm`: stale comment fixed, hint under the textarea (`add.alertsHint` EN+AR, `aria-describedby`), new `.fieldHint` class in `AddPatientForm.module.css`.
- **FE-F6-2** — new **`shared/patient-language.ts`** (`PATIENT_LANGUAGE`, `PATIENT_LANGUAGE_OPTIONS`, `patientLanguageKey`, `reminderLanguage`). The three senders (`whatsapp-batch-plan.ts`, `messaging-queries.ts` ×2) call `reminderLanguage(...) === 'en'`; `AddPatientForm`/`EditPatientComponent` map the options, `ViewPatientInfo.getLanguageDisplay` uses `patientLanguageKey`.

### Added in the second session (review fixes + tests)
- **`WorkService.validateAndUpdateWork` — a blank currency now means "unchanged".** `optCurrency` turns a blank select into a present-but-`undefined` key, and the presence-keyed `updateWork()` would have written `currency = NULL`. That violates `ck_works_cur` (because `total_required` is NOT NULL) and surfaced as a 500. The key is now dropped before anything reads it. The same shape still exists on `total_required` (`optMoney` `''` → NULL → `23502`), but no form sends it blank, so it was left alone.
- **`NewWorkComponent` — a required field on a hidden tab no longer blocks Save silently.** This is the "silently from the Dates/Keywords tabs" half of FE-F7-1, which would have got worse with a currency select that now starts empty when no default is configured. The three tab panels carry `data-tab`, and the form's `onInvalid` switches to the tab holding the **first** `:invalid` control, then calls `reportValidity()` on the next frame so the browser shows its bubble. Idempotent: every invalid control resolves to the same tab, and the re-fired event finds that tab already active.
- Indentation fixed on the RA-era `notifyApprovalsChanged()` lines inside the invoice-delete handler that RB1 re-indented.
- **Tests (11 new):** `shared/contracts/work.contract.test.ts` pins FE-F7-2/-4/-5 by parsing the real payloads: `3.5` / `11.5` accepted, `1000` / `-1` refused, EUR refused on `addWork` and `addWorkWithInvoice`, blank currency → absent, `estimated_duration` kept, a blank one kept as a present key, `discount:null` → `null`. `shared/patient-language.test.ts` pins the codebook and `reminderLanguage`. `role-registry.test.ts` gains the **roleCaps ↔ server role-set drift guard**: `writeFinance`/`viewFinance`/`editRecords` ≡ `FINANCE_ROLES`, `adminWrites`/`manageUsers` ≡ `ADMIN_ROLES`, and an unknown role gets nothing.
- Checked: nothing else reads `employeesQuery('?percentage=true')`, and `keys.test.ts` does not pin the lookups list.

### Runtime verification — 44 of 44 checks passed, plus 1 on the real option after the migration
Dev `:5273` was started with the three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env**. Every non-GET was **aborted**. `/api/auth/me` was mocked to `clinical` for the role checks, and `/api/options/DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY` was mocked for the default-currency checks. No screenshots were taken. The probe is **`scripts/e2e/.rb1-probe.tmp.mjs`** (untracked, delete when done). Re-run it with `E2E_BASE=http://localhost:5273 node scripts/e2e/.rb1-probe.tmp.mjs`.

## RA5 · 2026-09-17 — FE-F4-6, the unsaved-work guard

The last Phase A deferral that was waiting on a **decision** rather than on another session's files. **`npm run gate` green** — 0 errors, the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones; 208/208 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline; build green. No route or mount changed, so `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt` is untouched. **1 fixed.**

**🟡 FE-F4-6 — a modal that holds unsaved work now asks before a gesture discards it.** `Modal` takes an opt-in `unsavedGuard` (`{ isDirty?, watchInput?, resetKey?, message? }`); declaring it routes **backdrop, Escape, the header ✕ and the footer Cancel** through one `requestDismiss()` that raises the app's own `useConfirm` first. Six decisions worth keeping:

- **Opt-in, not a new default.** Of the 73 render sites roughly 50 are viewers, pickers and confirms where instant dismiss is correct; flipping `closeOnBackdropClick` globally would have degraded those 50 to protect 20, and would not have touched Escape — the gesture that actually destroyed the payment form in FE-F4-1.
- **`watchInput` listens for a NATIVE `input`/`change` on the content node, not React's `onInput`.** Synthetic events bubble through the *React* tree, so a portaled child — the lookup manager stacked on the expense form — would have marked its parent dirty. It also means programmatic seeding never arms the guard: React writes controlled values through the DOM property and dispatches nothing, so `ExpenseModal`'s row-seeded form and `PaymentModal`'s suggested amount stay clean until the user actually types. Verified both ways at runtime.
- **The dirty flag is a REF, and that is load-bearing.** It was written as state first (to satisfy `react-hooks/refs` on the callback handed to `children()`), and that **ate the first keystroke of every guarded form** — the listener sits deeper than the node React delegates to, so it runs *before* React turns the same event into `onChange`; a `setState` there forces a synchronous re-render mid-dispatch, the controlled input is rewritten with its pre-keystroke value, and React's handler then reads that stale value back off the DOM. `"typed"` arrived as `"yped"`; `fill()` lost its text entirely. A ref never renders, so it cannot interleave. The lint rule gets a one-line disable with that reason, and `smoke-modal-guard.mjs` asserts the exact string so it cannot come back quietly.
- **Children-as-function, not a context.** `<Modal>{(dismiss) => …}</Modal>`. A context can only reach components rendered *inside* `Modal` — the header's ✕ — but the footer's Cancel is created in the consumer's own body, above the provider, and Cancel sits directly beside Save where a mis-click costs the most. The function form covers all four exits, needs no new context module and no `ModalHeader` change, and makes the guarded buttons visible in the diff. `ReactNode | ((dismiss) => ReactNode)`, so the other 66 sites are untouched.
- **A programmatic `onClose()` is deliberately not routed through the guard** — saving and closing must never ask.
- **No re-entrancy guard, on purpose.** While the confirm is open it owns the top of RA2's escape stack and its own overlay covers the dialog beneath, so no second gesture can reach the guarded modal.

**`contexts/confirm-context.ts` is new** — the bare `createContext` + its two types, split out of `ConfirmContext.tsx` so `Modal` can read the confirm without importing the provider (which renders `ConfirmDialog` → `Modal`). Every use was deferred to render time, so a bundler survives that loop today, but a cycle through an `export default` is the shape that becomes a TDZ crash the moment something evaluates at module scope. `Modal` reads the raw context rather than `useConfirm()`, which throws without a provider — it must stay usable outside `RootLayout` (`/chair-display` is such a tree).

**The confirm chrome is translated once, in `common:unsaved.*`** (EN + AR; the `_arCoversEn` assertion forces the Arabic half). No call site passes a string, so nothing lands on the i18next ratchet — `PaymentModal`'s `resetKey` is numeric for the same reason. An Arabic session gets an Arabic prompt with zero work per modal.

**Seven sites opted in**, one prop each:

| site | guard | why |
|---|---|---|
| `PaymentModal` | `watchInput` + `resetKey: paymentSuccess ? 1 : 0` | the FE-F4-1 repro's own form. The key flips once the payment is written: the receipt view is not unsaved work |
| `ExpenseModal` | `watchInput` | money entry, seeded during render |
| `LabCaseModal` | `watchInput` + `resetKey: dataUpdatedAt` | track mode **saves and stays open** (advance / hold / note), so the flag has to forget what was already written. `dataUpdatedAt` moves only when this case refetches, and only its own writes do that (`refetchOnWindowFocus` is off repo-wide) — which is why the six-handler counter first sketched for it was unnecessary |
| `SetFormDrawer` · `BatchFormDrawer` | `watchInput` | drawers dismiss on Escape/backdrop like any modal — `isDrawer` only disables dragging |
| `ItemFormModal` | `watchInput` | its sessionStorage draft survives a RELOAD, not a close — the close effect drops it |
| `TaskFormModal` | `watchInput` | — |

**`NewWorkComponent` was dropped from that list although the finding names it**: both of its `<Modal>`s are *confirmation dialogs* ("Active Work Already Exists", "Confirm Completed Work Creation") — the new-work form itself is a route, not a modal — so their `!loading` gating is already the right behaviour.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (Playwright, no screenshots left behind), six of the seven sites; every run ended on "Keep editing" or a discard, never on Save, and `invoices` for work 12979 still holds only its one pre-existing row:

| site | untouched → gesture | typed → gesture | after "Keep editing" |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PaymentModal` (Escape · backdrop · ✕) | closes instantly, although the amount is pre-seeded `0` | 2 dialogs each time | `12,345` intact — **including the first keystroke** |
| `PaymentModal`, `shwan_language = ar` | — | 2 dialogs, prompt reads تغييرات غير محفوظة / تجاهل / متابعة التعديل | `999` intact |
| `ExpenseModal` (backdrop · footer Cancel) | closes instantly | 2 dialogs | note intact |
| `ItemFormModal` (Escape) | closes instantly | 2 dialogs | `abc123` intact |
| `SetFormDrawer` (drawer backdrop) | closes instantly | 2 dialogs | value intact |
| `BatchFormDrawer` (drawer backdrop) | closes instantly | 2 dialogs | note intact |
| `TaskFormModal` (footer Cancel) | closes instantly | 2 dialogs | `guard` intact |
| **unguarded** payment-history modal (Escape) | closes instantly | — | — |

Two checks in there are about the *design*, not the feature: the unguarded modal proves the other 66 sites are unchanged, and a DOM assertion that a stacked dialog is a **portal sibling, not a descendant** proves why `watchInput` can use a native listener at all. `BatchFormDrawer` also read as "not dirty" on a first pass — correctly: the field typed into was `readOnly`, so no input event ever fired.

**`scripts/e2e/smoke-modal-guard.mjs` is new** and carries the PaymentModal half of that table, including the first-keystroke assertion.

**`LabCaseModal` is the one site not runtime-verified**: `lab_cases` has **0 rows**, the modal opens only from a board card, and creating a case would write to the live clinic DB (and mirror it). Typecheck + the identical one-prop pattern cover it; it is worth a click the next time a real case exists.

**Deliberately NOT done here:**
- **The other ~13 data-entry modals** (`RestockModal`, `StockAdjustModal`, `PaymentFormDrawer`, `EditTimepointModal`, `HolidayQuickModal`, `CreateTemplateModal`, `SaveConfigModal`, `AlertModal`, `PhotoSessionDialog`, `TransferWorkModal`, `RenameFolderModal`, `AlignerDoctorsSettings`, `EmployeeSettings`). One prop each, but worth a day of live use on the seven above first.
- **`ConfirmDialog`'s own English chrome.** Every translated `confirm()` passes `t()` for message/title/confirmText and **never `cancelText`** (`PaymentModal:348,749`, `WorkComponent:745`), so an Arabic session already reads an English "Cancel". The fix is to default `ConfirmDialog`'s chrome from `common:` — four lines, kills the whole class, and the `unsaved.*` keys are the natural home. Its own row, not this one.
- **`watchInput`'s blind spot** — state changed by clicking something that is not a form control (a button-grid date picker, a canvas). Documented on the type; a site that needs it passes `isDirty` too, which is why the two are OR-ed.
- **Browser Back with a modal open** still discards silently. That needs a router blocker, not this primitive.
- **Settings tabs** have the same defect one level up — `hasChanges` already exists there and only renders a dot badge, so switching tabs drops pending edits. Not a modal, so this cannot help; F21/F22.

## RA4 · 2026-09-17 — the rest of the Phase A backlog

The scheduled RA row. **`npm run gate` green** — 0 errors, the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones; **208/208 tests across 23 files** (was 198/21 — RA4 adds two test files, below); contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline; build green. No route or mount changed, so `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt` is untouched. **22 findings closed outright, 2 partially (`FE-F2-10`, `FE-F0-1` — their remainders are other sessions' calls), 17 deliberately deferred with reasons.**

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (Playwright via `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`; read-only — nothing written, no screenshots left behind). Every claim below marked ✔ was read off the live DOM.

### The funnel and the server-state policy

**🟡 FE-F1-3 — the CSRF pre-flight has its own deadline.** `fetchCsrfToken()` runs *before* the mutation's `withTimeout` is armed, so a server that accepted the connection and never answered `/api/csrf-token` hung every mutation in the tab with no abort and no toast — and single-flight meant one stuck fetch stalled all of them. It now carries a 10s `withTimeout(null, …)` (shorter than the 30s request budget: it is a tiny same-origin GET, and failing fast lets the mutation surface an ordinary error). Deliberately **not** chained to the caller's signal — the fetch is shared by every concurrent mutation, so one caller navigating away must not abort the one the others await.

**🟡 FE-F2-3 — `retry: 2` is now the policy the comment describes.** `query/client.ts` exports `isTransientQueryError`: 5xx and status-less failures (network / our own timeout) retry; **4xx and a fail-loud contract-drift throw do not**, because both are deterministic — a 404 on a deleted lab case cost 3 requests and ~3s of spinner before the screen could say "not found", and a Zod mismatch was replayed twice against a server that would produce the identical mismatch. An `AbortError` (caller navigated away) never retries.
- **Four of the six local `retry: false` opt-outs deleted** as subsumed: both exchange-rate factories and `optionQuery` (all three are the 404-is-a-normal-empty-state shape), and `diagnosisQuery`, whose queryFn swallows every error and therefore never rejects at all — its `retry: false` could not fire.
- **The two 3Shape ones KEPT**, with the reason written into the comment: an Unite box that is off or unreachable surfaces as 5xx/network, which the new default *would* treat as transient, but here it is actionable and the user should be told to check the workstation rather than wait out two backoffs.
- Pinned by a new unit test (`query/client.test.ts`, 6 cases) — this is exactly the rule that regresses silently, since a bare numeric `retry` looks identical at the call site.

**🟡 FE-F2-2 — 401 ownership settled, and the funnel's branches are live again.** The decision went to **keep the global interceptor and stop it destroying the status**. `public/index.html` is the one layer that sees every fetch *including* the sanctioned raw-fetch escape hatches (blob downloads, the label POST, the whatsapp client), so it keeps the redirect; it now returns the 401 `Response` instead of rejecting with a bare `new Error('Authentication required')`. `core/http.ts` therefore builds a real `HttpError(401)`, and `loaderQuery`'s and `withAuth`'s `status === 401` branches — both previously unreachable — run: `queryClient.clear()` fires, and the route boundary renders `RouteError`'s purpose-built "Unauthorized / you need to log in" card instead of the generic 500 one while the navigation is in flight. Both branches **dropped their own `location.href` assignment** (the interceptor already navigated, and two assignments race), and the interceptor guards with a `redirecting` flag. Each side carries a comment naming the other as the owner. **F0's `window.location.href` baseline drops 12 → 10.**

**🟡 FE-F2-6 — the one loader that bypassed the cache now uses it, and `PatientManagement` reads the cache.** `patientManagementLoader`'s five raw `fetchJSON` calls duplicated five existing `queries.ts` factories verbatim; the results were returned as loader data and never entered the RQ cache. It is now a pure prefetcher (`ensureQueryData` ×5, returning `null`) and the screen reads the same five keys with `useQuery`. Consequences, all three from the finding: `typeOptionsQuery` is **no longer dead** (FE-F0-1's verdict was right — it was dead *because of* this), a lookup edited in Settings can now reach these dropdowns without a full route re-navigation, and `emptyOnHttpError`'s per-lookup tolerance is kept but documented. `ensureQueryData` rather than `loaderQuery` on purpose: `loaderQuery` maps a failure onto a `Response` for the errorElement, which would defeat the per-lookup tolerance.
- **A latent nullability lie surfaced and was fixed honestly:** the deleted hand-written types declared `key_word: string` / `type: string`, but both are nullable in the DB *and* in the contracts. `?? ''` keeps the rendering byte-identical (react-select renders nothing for a `null` label either) without re-introducing the lie.
- ✔ live: work types **22**, keywords **89**, tags **1**, patient types **7** options, all served from the cache.

**🟡 FE-F2-7 — the diagnosis deep-link warms its chunk.** `patientShellLoader` called `preloadPatientPage(params.page)`, but the diagnosis route (`:personId/work/:workId/diagnosis`) has no `:page` segment, so it no-opped on the largest patient sub-page in the tree (`pages/Diagnosis.tsx`, 957 LOC). The loader now mirrors the derivation `PatientShell` already does for rendering, off `request.url`.

**🔵 FE-F2-9 — `patientShellLoader` returns `null`.** Its six-field payload had no reader (`PatientShell` has no `useLoaderData` and never did), and it was built with three `as` casts out of parsed contract payloads. Interface, payload and casts are gone; the shape now matches `labTrackingLoader`. The docblock records that `currentPage` was a decoy — `Navigation`'s `currentPage` prop comes from `PatientShell`'s own `effectivePage`.

**🔵 FE-F2-10 (partial) + FE-F0-1 (partial) — the dead inventory.** `patientInfoLoader`, `workDetailsLoader` and their result interfaces deleted (superseded by `patientShellLoader`, which does strictly more and is wired to every `/patient/*` route). `qk.aligner.work` deleted — the one genuinely dead key of the ten; the other nine are documented parent/prefix keys and stay as documentation of the hierarchy. **Still open:** `approvalsHistoryQuery` — `GET /api/approvals/history` is a live contracted route with no consumer anywhere, i.e. the maker-checker audit trail is unreachable in the UI. **F5 owns that call** (build the screen or retire the endpoint), so it is not a cleanup. The remaining unreferenced exports belong to F17/F20 (`aligner.types.ts` ×3), F23 (`portal.schemas.ts`), F16 (`whatsapp-*` ×5) and `formatters.ts#formatMoneyInput`.

**🔵 FE-F2-12 — three placeholder pages retired.** `details`, `history` and `messages` rendered a hardcoded "…view coming soon…" card, appeared in no nav, and were reachable only by typing the URL. They now fall through to the unknown-page card. ✔ live: `/patient/7639/details` → "Page Not Found · The page "details" is not available."

**🔵 FE-F1-9 — 15 dead exports the F0 scanner structurally could not see.** Each was referenced by its own module's `export default { … }` object, which made a textual scan count a second use. Three of those default objects had **no default importer at all** (every consumer uses named imports), so they went too, and with them: `core/utils.ts` `deepClone` + `formatPhoneNumber` (whose `digits.startsWith('+' + cc)` branch was unreachable regardless — `digits` is `\D`-stripped one line above); `core/iniParser.ts`'s 7 unused functions (`validateIniConfig`, `getValue`, `removeKey`, `getSectionKeys`, `getSections`, `hasKey`, `countKeys`); `core/fileSystemAccess.ts`'s `listHandles`, `clearHandles`, `getFileFromDirectory`, `isPermissionError`, plus `getHandle`/`pickFile` demoted to module-internal. `core/storage.ts` keeps its default object (two real importers) but loses `clear()` — 0 callers, and as written it would have wiped `shwan_theme`/`shwan_language`/`shwan_arabic_font`/`chairId`. **−290 lines across the four files.** ✔ live: every consuming screen still renders — patient files, Settings → Protocol Handlers (602 chars of panel, correct tab, no 404s, no page errors).

**🔵 FE-F1-11 — the six small ones, all six done.** `parseLocalDate(date instanceof Date ? date : date)` → `parseLocalDate(date)` (both branches were the same expression); `isLocalStorageAvailable()` memoized per page load (it was a `setItem`+`removeItem` probe on **every** get/set/remove, so one read cost three localStorage ops); `readTextFile`'s ignored `_encoding` parameter dropped; `postFormData`'s vestigial `headers: {}` removed and `...options` moved **first** like every other helper (spread last, a caller-supplied `method`/`body` silently won); `photo-render-watch`'s `timer: 0 as unknown as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>` replaced with an optional field; and the `index.html` FOUC RTL check now prefix-matches all three static routes exactly like `isRtlRoute` (it prefix-matched only `/expenses/` and exact-matched the other two — latent until `/dashboard` or `/appointments` grows a child).

### The shell and the shared primitives

**🟡 FE-F4-3 — the patients-folder UNC is server state again.** The `localStorage['patientsFolder']` mirror is gone (nothing in the tree ever removed that key, so a clinic that moved its share left every already-used browser building `explorer:\\OLD\Clinic1\<id>` forever, unrecoverable short of clearing site data). `patientsFolderQuery` carries `staleTime: 1h` — long, but bounded, which is the whole point. The finding's second half is fixed too: the `${patientsFolder}${personId}` concatenation **normalises the trailing separator**, so a Settings edit that drops the final `\` no longer yields `\\CLINIC\Clinic17639`. ✔ live: `localStorage.getItem('patientsFolder')` is `null` after opening a patient page.

**🟡 FE-F4-4 — the jump list and the checkbox under it agree.** Name matching moved into `utils/patientSearch.ts#matchesPatientName(name, input, prefixOnly)`, shared by both widgets (they had drifted into two copies of the same rule, wrong the same way). Two fixes in one: it is **case-folded** (`startsWith` is case-sensitive against a `citext` server — typing `ali` offered nothing while Enter found `Ali`), and `PatientManagement` now passes its **"Match from beginning of name only"** flag down, so with the box unchecked the dropdown matches substrings like the table below it. Pinned by `utils/patientSearch.test.ts` (4 cases). ✔ live, 6,828-row list: `"نزار"` (second word of `"زيان نزار"`) → **8 options** where the old rule gave 0; `"jaim"` for `"Jaime Dolatowski"` → **1 option** where the old rule gave 0.

**🟡 FE-F4-5 — seven patient destinations are reachable from the keyboard.** The More-actions trigger is now `role="button" tabIndex={0}` with `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`; the flyout opens on **hover, click and focus**, and closes on Escape or when focus leaves **both** the trigger and the flyout (they are separate DOM subtrees — the flyout renders outside the sidebar for positioning — so a single `contains` check is not enough). Three decisions worth keeping:
- **Enter/Space OPENS, it does not toggle.** Focusing the trigger already opens the flyout, so a toggle meant a keyboard user's first Enter closed the menu they had just opened. Caught at runtime, not in review.
- **Escape sets a one-shot guard before returning focus.** Focusing the trigger is itself an open gesture, so without it Escape closed and instantly reopened the menu. Also caught at runtime.
- The flyout container's `onKeyDown` carries an annotated `no-static-element-interactions` disable — it is a container whose interactive children are the `<Link>`s, and the handler is only a scoped Escape-to-close.

✔ live, full keyboard round-trip: focus → open (`aria-expanded="true"`, 7 links) → Enter → still open → Tab → focus lands on **"Compare Photos"** inside the flyout and it stays open → Escape → closed, focus back on the trigger, no reopen.

**🔵 FE-F4-7 — both casts past the fail-loud guard are gone.** `Navigation`'s local `PatientInfo` (eight optional name fields + an index signature, reached via `as`) is now `z.infer<typeof patientInfo.response>`, and the four unreachable name fallbacks (`patientName`, `Name`, `FullName`, `fullName` — not on the contract at all) went with it. `PatientQuickSearch`'s `PatientOption` is now `z.infer<…patientPhones.response>[number]`, which removed the `as PatientOption[]` cast **and** the `p.name || ''` fallbacks it forced (the contract guarantees `name: string`, `phone: string | null`).

**🔵 FE-F4-8 — the print-queue chevron turns.** `cn(styles.badgeChevron, { up: isExpanded })` emitted the **literal** class `up` while the rule in the CSS Module compiles to `._badgeChevron_x._up_x`. Now `{ [styles.up]: isExpanded }`. Swept the repo for the same shape: this was the **only** bare-key `cn(styles.x, { key: … })` against a module — the other four object-form call sites already use `[styles.…]`.

**🔵 FE-F4-9 — `PatientShell` reads the router's location, not the window's.** `window.location.pathname.endsWith('/diagnosis')` during render, two lines under `useLocation()`: the router writes history before it commits the location into context, so a `v7_startTransition` render could see the new pathname beside the old `page`/`workId`.

**🔵 FE-F4-11 — the four handle-less modals stopped eating text selection.** `ConfirmDialog`, `files/FilePreviewModal`, both `LabelPreviewModal` shapes and `PhotoEditor`'s two confirms now mark their bespoke title bars `data-modal-drag-handle`, so the primitive scopes dragging there instead of treating the whole body as a drag surface (and setting `user-select: none` on `<body>` at pointerdown). These are exactly the shapes whose *content* is text a user might want to copy. The primitive is untouched — the memo at `[[modalheader-draggable-modals]]` already records them as deliberate non-migrations.

**🔵 FE-F4-12 — a catch that could not fire, removed rather than faked.** Assigning an unregistered scheme to `location.href` does not throw, so the `try/catch` + `toast.error` around `csimaging:` and `dolphin:` was unreachable — on a PC without the handler the button was silent *and* the code read as if it reported the failure. Both catches are gone, with a comment saying what a real signal would take (a `blur`/`visibilitychange` race) and that it is separate work. The two now-unreachable toast keys were removed from **both** locale catalogs.

**🔵 FE-F4-15 — two tab strips, two different right answers.** They are not the same widget, so they did not get the same fix:
- `SettingsTabNavigation` **is** tabs (it switches an in-page panel): full WAI-ARIA now — `role="tablist"`/`role="tab"`, `aria-selected`, `aria-controls`, a matching `role="tabpanel"` + `aria-labelledby` on `SettingsComponent`'s content div (ids shared through two exported helpers), **roving tabindex** so the strip is one tab stop, and Arrow/Home/End navigation that **moves focus with the selection** (without that, the previously-active button becomes `tabIndex -1` on the next render and strands the keyboard user).
- `AlignerModeToggle` is **not** a tablist — each button `navigate()`s to a different route — so it became a `<nav aria-label>` with `aria-current="page"`. A `role="tab"` there would promise an in-page panel that does not exist.
- Both got `type="button"` (a bare `<button>` defaults to submit).

✔ live: 16 tabs, all `type="button"`, exactly **1 tab stop**, `aria-labelledby` on the panel resolves to the selected tab, ArrowRight moves selection *and* focus General → Database; aligner toggle = 4 buttons, one `aria-current="page"`.

**🔵 FE-F4-16 — the Appointments tab stops lighting up on patient routes.** `p.includes('/appointment')` matched `/patient/:id/appointments`, `/new-appointment` and `/edit-appointment/:id` as well as the daily screen the tab actually navigates to. Now anchored to `/appointments` and its children. ✔ live on `/patient/7639/appointments`: Dashboard/Appointments/Search all inactive, only the patient chip active.

**🔵 FE-F4-18 — five params, one reader.** `PatientShell` built `view`, `filter`, `workId` and `phone` on every render for nobody (`ContentRenderer` reads only `params.tpCode`, and pulls workId/visitId from the query string itself); the `isNewPatient` prop was destructured to `_isNewPatient` and never read (the "new patient" branch is reached by `page === 'add'`). Both interfaces are down to `{ tpCode?: string }`, and the `[key: string]` catch-alls went with them.

**🔵 FE-F4-19 — `cleanPhone()` has a caller.** `PhoneDisplay` inlined the identical `replace(/[^\d]/g,'')`; it now calls the util, which had zero callers.

**🔵 FE-F4-20 — the unreachable option keyboard handler, removed for the right reason.** The listbox options' `tabIndex={0}` + Enter/Space `onKeyDown` could never fire (the input's `onBlur` closes the list before Tab can land on one) — but they were also *wrong*: this is the `aria-activedescendant` pattern, where focus stays on the input and options must not be focusable. The docblock now says so. ✔ live: `optionTabIndex: -1`.

## RA3 · 2026-09-17 — FE-F4-2, the other half of `Modal.tsx`

RA2's deferral held for one session. **`npm run gate` green** — 0 errors, the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones; 198/198 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline; build green. No route or mount changed, so `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt` is untouched. **1 fixed.**

**🟡 FE-F4-2 — a dialog with no name now says so, instead of pointing at nothing.** `Modal` dropped `fallbackTitleId`/`useId` and emits `aria-labelledby={ariaLabelledBy}` directly, so an omitted prop means the attribute is **absent** rather than a dangling reference (which is strictly worse: it also suppresses the content fallback some screen readers use). The prop carries a docblock saying to pass it and why the old default was worse than nothing.

**No `ariaLabel` prop was added**, contrary to the finding's proposed fix. Every one of the 10 unnamed sites already renders a visible heading — the four "header-less by design" shapes (`ConfirmDialog`, the two `PhotoEditor` confirms, `LabelPreviewModal`) are missing a `<ModalHeader>`, not a title — so each was named off its own `<h2>`/`<h3>`. `aria-labelledby` at a visible heading beats an invisible `aria-label` (the visible-label/accessible-name match), and an unused prop is API surface nobody needs.

| site | name source |
|---|---|
| `ConfirmDialog` (every `useConfirm()`) | `useId()`, **not** a literal — a confirm can be raised from inside another `ConfirmDialog`, so two can be mounted at once |
| `PaymentModal` | one `payment-modal-title`, carried by the `<ModalHeader>` in the form branch and by the `<h2>` in the mutually-exclusive success branch |
| `AdminUserManagement` reset-password, `TransferWorkModal`, `LabelPreviewModal` (queue + single), `PhotoEditor` (remove + leave), `StandPOS` sale-complete, `StandSalesHistory` void | literal kebab id on the existing heading, matching the `titleId="…-modal-title"` convention the other 57 sites already use |

**The whole census is now enforced-clean, not just the 10 touched.** A scan over all 67 `<Modal>` render sites resolves every `ariaLabelledBy` against an `id=`/`titleId=` in the same file: **0 missing, 0 dangling.** That also caught the finding's file count being wrong — corrected in the write-up above.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (patient 7639 / work 12979, Playwright, no screenshots left behind), reading `aria-labelledby` off every live `[role="dialog"]` and resolving it with `getElementById`:

| scenario | before | after |
|---|---|---|
| `Add Payment` open | `aria-labelledby="_r_2_"`, `resolves: false` | `"payment-modal-title"`, `resolves: true`, name **"Add Payment"** |
| + underpayment confirm stacked on it | `"_r_1_"`, `resolves: false` | `"_r_0_"` (the `useId`), `resolves: true`, name **"Underpayment Warning"** |

Nothing was written by the repro — the confirm was left unanswered.

**Deliberately NOT done here:** nothing from FE-F4-2. The remaining F4 rows (FE-F4-3 onward) stay with the scheduled RA.

## RA2 · 2026-09-17 — FE-F4-1 (F4's 🟠), brought forward

Same call as RA1: sitting on a 🟠 that destroys a filled payment form until the scheduled RA row was not worth it. **`npm run gate` green** — 0 errors, the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones; 198/198 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline; build green. No route or mount changed, so `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt` is untouched. **1 fixed.**

**🟠 FE-F4-1 — Escape is now served from a stack, and only the top modal answers it.** `Modal.tsx` replaces the per-instance `document` keydown listener with a module-level `escapeStack` plus **one** listener (`pushEscapeEntry`/`removeEscapeEntry`/`handleDocumentEscape`). Four decisions worth keeping:
- **An open modal always registers, even with `closeOnEscape={false}`.** The top entry swallows the key either way and only *closes* if it is allowed to, so a deliberately non-dismissible dialog (`NewWorkComponent`'s `closeOnEscape={!loading}` mid-save, `AdminUserManagement`'s `!resetting`, `StandSalesHistory`'s `!voiding`) can no longer let Escape through to close the modal beneath it.
- **Bubble phase, deliberately.** `LookupContextMenu` is a popover, not a Modal — it stays out of the stack and still beats it with a capture-phase `stopImmediatePropagation()`. Registering the stack in capture would have run *first* and closed the modal out from under the menu. Runtime-checked: with the menu open over the Expense modal, Escape closes the menu and the modal stays.
- **The entry is a stable object reading live props through a ref.** Re-pushing on every `onClose` identity change (`ConfirmContext`'s `handleCancel` is `useCallback([pending])`, so it changes on every confirm) would have shuffled an already-open modal back to the top of the stack.
- **Removal is by identity, not `pop()`** — a modal lower in the stack can close first.

`event.stopPropagation()` is kept: on `document` it is a no-op for siblings, but it still blocks `window`-level listeners, which is what stops `SlideshowPlayer`'s Escape→exit from firing underneath an open modal. That behaviour is unchanged.

**`LookupManagerModal`'s workaround retired** with it — its captured `stopImmediatePropagation` effect, the `closeOnEscape={false}` prop and the now-unused `useEffect` import are gone, and its docblock records what replaced them. `LookupContextMenu`'s workaround stays (see above); the F4 write-up's "both workarounds" phrasing has been corrected in place.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`**, three scenarios, no screenshots left behind:

| scenario | before | after |
|---|---|---|
| `PaymentModal` + underpayment confirm, one Escape | 2 dialogs → **0** | 2 → **1**, and the form still holds `100` / `50` / `0`; a second Escape → 0 |
| single `PaymentModal`, one Escape | 1 → 0 | 1 → 0 (unchanged) |
| Expense modal → right-click lab select → "Edit values" → Escape | (was hand-patched locally) | "Add New Expense" + "Manage Labs" → **only "Manage Labs" closes**; second Escape closes the expense modal and `body.style.overflow` returns to none (refcount released) |

Nothing was written by the repro: `invoices` for work 12979 still holds only the pre-existing row.

**Deliberately NOT done here** (stays with the scheduled RA): FE-F4-2's dangling `aria-labelledby` — the same file, but a different defect with a wider blast radius (12 files need an `ariaLabelledBy` or a new `ariaLabel` prop), and folding it in would have made this change unreviewable next to the Escape fix.

## RA1 · 2026-09-16 — all three 🟠 plus F3's safe set

Brought forward from the scheduled RA slot: sitting on three 🟠 until after F4 was not worth it. **`npm run gate` green** (0 errors; the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones; 198/198 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline; build green). **Route-table snapshot reviewed line-by-line and updated** (`vitest -u`) — see below. Runtime-verified on dev `:5273` over `/dashboard`, `/appointments`, `/calendar`, `/expenses`, `/patient-management`: no boundary crash on any of them, the toast live region is present in the DOM, and the header still renders the user off the trimmed `GlobalStateContext`. **16 fixed, 1 partial, 8 deliberately deferred.**

**🟠 FE-F1-1 — `core/http.ts` now fails loud on a non-JSON 200 when a schema was passed.** The `return response.text() as unknown as T` fall-through threw away the schema; an unmatched `/api/*` returns the SPA shell as 200 `text/html`, so a renamed endpoint resolved with 11 KB of HTML as the payload. Non-JSON now throws an `HttpError` carrying the content-type and the first 500 chars of the body when (and only when) a schema was supplied — a schema-less read still gets its text. The deeper fix (a JSON 404 terminator for `/api`) stays backend scope and is still worth doing.

**🟠 FE-F1-2 — uploads no longer abort at 30 s, on either side.** Client: `timeoutMs: 120000` on `SetFormDrawer` (the aligner PDF — same endpoint `PatientSets` already overrode, which is why one screen worked and the other didn't), `Videos.tsx`, `FileExplorer.tsx` and both `SequenceSidebar` call sites. Server: `POST /api/videos` and `POST /api/patients/:personId/files/upload` had **no** `timeouts.long` at all — both now have it, so a video or a multi-file photo drop is no longer 408'd mid-stream by the global 30 s `requestTimeout`.

**🟠 FE-F2-1 — every appointment write now invalidates `qk.calendar.all()`.** All five sites: `AppointmentForm` (create), `EditAppointmentForm` (reschedule), `PatientAppointments` (delete), and the two quick check-ins (`WorkComponent`, `PatientManagement`) which create a same-day appointment and previously invalidated **nothing at all** — those two now also invalidate `qk.patient.all(personId)`. Each carries a comment naming what breaks without it, so the next author sees why.

**🟡 FE-F2-4 — folded into the same change.** `PatientAppointments` delete moved from `qk.patient.appointments` to `qk.patient.all`, which covers the `hasAppointment` **sibling** key the Works screen reads.

**🟡 FE-F2-5 — the comment that hid FE-F2-1.** `/calendar`'s "100% SSE-driven real-time data" is replaced with what is actually true (three React Query reads, no SSE, no `refetchInterval`) plus an explicit instruction that appointment writes must invalidate `qk.calendar.all()` themselves — and a note that the old wording is why five sites shipped without it. The three sibling routes whose "SSE-driven" comments **are** accurate were left alone.

**🟡 FE-F3-2 — a hold-tier write now tells the approval bells, from all six sites.** The filed finding covered `useExpenses`' update/delete; the same hole was in four more files, so all six `outcome === 'pending'` branches now call `notifyApprovalsChanged()`: `useExpenses` ×2, `NewWorkComponent` (work update), `EditPatientComponent` (patient delete), `PatientManagement` (patient delete), `WorkComponent` (work delete + invoice delete). Before this, `notifyApprovalsChanged()` was called from exactly one place in the repo — when an admin *resolved* a request — so nothing that *created* one ever moved a badge.

**🟡 FE-F3-4 — toasts are now announced.** `role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="false"` on the container, `role="alert"` on error toasts only (an error usually means the action did **not** happen), and `aria-hidden` on the decorative icon.

**🔵 FE-F3-7 — "Reset and Reload" does something.** It cleared three `localStorage` keys that exist nowhere in the app, making it an exact duplicate of the button beside it. It now clears the two `sessionStorage` entries that can actually poison a load (`labelPrintQueue`, `currentUser`), wrapped in try/catch for private mode, and **deliberately leaves** `shwan_theme`/`shwan_language`/`shwan_arabic_font` — display preferences cannot cause a crash and silently wiping them would be a surprise. The stale key list is recorded in the code comment.

**🔵 FE-F3-10, -17, -18, -19, -20 + the partial on -6/-15 — dead code and purity.**
- `ConfirmContext` settles the superseded promise through a ref **before** `setPending` instead of inside the updater (updaters must be pure; StrictMode calls them twice).
- `useWhatsAppAuth` loses `connectionAttempts` (never anything but 0, no consumer) and `fetchQRCode` (no consumer), plus the now-unused `HttpError` import.
- `useAppointments.recoverFromConflict` now calls the shared, unit-tested `isInvalidStateTransition` instead of its byte-identical inline copy — the predicate CLAUDE.md documents finally has a caller, and its tests cover live code.
- `PrintQueueContext` drops `toggleLogo`, `updateLabels`, `buildLabelsForPrint`, `isModalOpen`/`setIsModalOpen`, the `addedAt` field and the `RichLabel` type; `substr` → `slice`. A header comment records what went and why, so nobody re-adds a second implementation.
- `useDateManager`: the "database stored procedure" comment (there are none) and `formatDateLabel`'s unused parameter.
- `GlobalStateContext` is down to the three members that have consumers (`user`, `whatsappClientReady`, `whatsappQrCode`) with a `useMemo`'d value like every other context in the tree. **FE-F3-6 is only PARTIAL**: the dead weight (FE-F3-15) and the memo are done, the split into `useAuthUser` + `WhatsAppStatusContext` is not.
- `ErrorBoundary`'s unreachable default fallback is gone, `fallback` is now a **required** prop, and `showDetails`/`onReset`/`navigate` + the `useNavigate()` that only fed them went with it; `ErrorBoundaryWithNavigate` is now `ErrorBoundaryWithReset` (the `resetKey` was the only load-bearing thing it did). `ErrorBoundary.module.css` (109 lines) and its `.d.ts` are deleted. The class docblock now states why it must stay a class (`React.lazy` rejections) and that `fallback` is required.

**🟡 FE-F3-1 — the weekend question is settled: FRIDAY IS THE ONLY DAY OFF** (owner's call, 2026-09-17). The code was right and the doc prose was wrong, so the fix went both ways:
- `MonthlyCalendarGrid` loses `isWeekend()` and the `.weekend` class entirely — not "corrected to Friday", *removed*. `CalendarViewService` never emits a Friday (`if (d.getDay() === 5) continue`, so the six Sat–Thu columns stay aligned), which means **every cell this grid renders is a working day and there is nothing to tint**. The old `getDay() === 0 || === 6` greyed out Saturday and Sunday, two of the busiest days of the week. The 23 lines of `.weekend` CSS went with it, and the component docblock now states the week shape and what it used to get wrong.
- **`CLAUDE.md` corrected.** The Product-direction block said "~247 working days/yr (after Fri+Sat weekends + ~14 holidays)" — wrong premise, so the arithmetic under it was wrong too. Now ~299 working days/yr (52 Fridays + ~14 holidays), which moves the 20-year ceiling from ~2M to **~2.4M appointments** and the index example from "~415 of its ~8,300 rows" to "~500 of that month's ~10,000". A new top-level **"Clinic week — Sat–Thu, Friday off"** note makes it a findable fact instead of a parenthetical inside a capacity calculation, and lists the six places that implement it.
- Swept every `getDay()` in the tree first (11 client + 6 server): `CalendarViewService`, `calendarDate#addWorkingDays`/`getWeekStartSaturday`, `SimplifiedCalendarPicker`, `useDateManager`, `utils/arabic-day.ts` (index 5 → `''`) are all Friday-only and correct. `MonthlyCalendarGrid` was the only dissenter in the codebase.
- **Verified in the running app** (dev `:5273`, month view): columns render `SAT SUN MON TUE WED THU`, 30 day cells, **0** cells carrying a weekend tint, no page errors.

**Bonus, not a filed finding — `timeouts.*` is no longer `<anonymous>` in the route-table snapshot.** `requestTimeout()` names its closure `timeout(<ms>ms)` via `Object.defineProperty`, the same trick `authorize()`/`validate()`/`requireRecordAge()` already use. The snapshot is the committed record of what runs before each handler, and a route's timeout budget is part of its posture: an upload route silently losing `timeouts.long` should now show up as a diff. **Snapshot diff reviewed before accepting**: 7 lines, all middleware-chain text — 2 genuinely new insertions (`/api/videos`, `/api/patients/:personId/files/upload`) and 5 pre-existing `<anonymous>` entries becoming legible (`/api/wa/send`, `/api/wa/sendmedia`, `/api/wa/sendmedia2`, `/api/aligner/sets/:setId/upload-pdf`, `.../files/delete-batch`). **No route added, removed, reordered or re-gated.**

|---|---|
| FE-F3-3 🟡 | 53 sites across 36 files owned by F5–F23. A single sweep through `utils/formatters.ts` is the right fix but it touches a dozen sessions' screens at once and each needs its own visual check; it belongs in a focused pass, not bolted onto RA1. |
| FE-F3-5 🟡 | Collapsing `useWhatsAppAuth`'s two render-phase syncs into one reducer is a real state-machine refactor on a screen that can only be verified against a live, **unpaired** WhatsApp client. Worth doing with F16, not blind. |
| FE-F3-8 🔵 | Removing the three `as Appointment[]` casts means widening `appointmentRowSchema` to declare the fields the cards read — which has to move in step with `AppointmentCard`'s readers (F11) **and** stay assignable from the server's `DailyAppointmentsOptimizedResult`. Half-doing it would trade a cast for a server compile error. |
